The Stone which the builders rejected, the same has become the head of the corner: this is the Rock whereon Christ established His Church, which He redeemed from the nations.

[f. 25rA] *Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

The darkness of transgressions holds fast my heart, O Sovereign Lady, and I dare not look up unto Heaven with mine eyes. Therefore I cry: Enlighten my mind, soul, and heart with the commandments of Christ!

*Troparion:*

I look upon thy divine Icon, and honor thee as the Sovereign Lady and All-Pure Mother, as the Archetype; this I kiss, and bow down before it, and praise it, knowing the honor of thee and of Him to be one.[^17]

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Calm the cruel surges of the flesh, and quench the flame of passions, O Virgin, wherewith the cruel serpent weaves for me a multi-twisted snare of transgressions, desiring to snatch me away unto destruction.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having come forth naturally from thee, O Theotokos, the Word was wholly clothed in Adam; pray Him therefore to deliver us from passions, various tribulations, and eternal fire.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Foreseeing in the Spirit the Incarnation of the Word, the Prophet Habakkuk proclaimed, saying: When the years draw near, Thou shalt be known; when the time comes, Thou shalt appear; glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to the Creator and Maker of all, thou hast ineffable power and unconquerable strength, O Virgin, and thou savest those who come unto thee in faith. For this cause I cry unto thee: Sovereign Lady of the world, help me!

[f. 25vA] *Troparion:*

Cast down the warfare of invisible spiritual enemies which utterly assaults my wretched heart, O Sovereign Lady, granting divine stillness and peace, that I may joyfully hymn thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Hope of the hopeless, Helper of the poor, Consolation of the weeping, Cleansing of those who sin, Guide of the wandering, Healer of the sick, and Restoration of those who fall!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O Pure and Blessed Virgin Mother, who alone didst change the curse of all: together with the Apostles, Martyrs, and Prophets, pray Him Who came forth from thy womb to save the souls of those who hymn thee.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Grant us Thy peace, O Son of God; for other than Thee we know no god, we name Thy Name, for Thou art the God of the living and of the dead.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Save me, O Sovereign Lady, from the depth of tribulations, sorrows, griefs, and bodily passions, and preserve my soul in divine stillness.

*Troparion:*

Thou hast been a fervent and firm defense unto me throughout all my life, O All-Immaculate One. Therefore I pray thee: even after death extend thy rich mercy unto me.

*Glory...*

[f. 26rA] *Troparion:*

O thou who didst give birth to the Creator and Redeemer of all, deliver me from bodily passions that burn my heart and draw me unto improper deeds.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having appeared as a spiritual place of sanctification, wholly sanctify me, O Sovereign Lady, and pray together with the Saints and Wise Apostles to save me, O Virgin.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Imitating the type of Jonah I cry: O Good One, free my life from corruption, and save me, O Savior of the world, who cry: Glory to Thee!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thou dost preserve me while alive, delivering me from tribulations; do thou also, O Virgin Mother, translate me by thy tender mercy unto eternal life when I depart from the earth.

*Troparion:*

Thou art an indestructible wall, thou art a firm Protection, O Good Theotokos; thou art a mighty defense unto thy servant; and for this cause I ever call upon thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having acquired an abyss of mercy toward God, O Good Theotokos, despise not me who fervently flee unto thy protection and cry aloud: Have mercy on me, O Mother of the God of all!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Cast me not away, O Christ, abhor me not, O Savior; the Ewe that bore Thee in the flesh prays Thee together with Thine Apostles, Prophets, and Passion-Bearers.

[f. 26vA] *Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*

The Prophets foretold thee, O All-Pure Maiden, as the Cloud of the ever-existing Divine Light, O Virgin, and Ark, and Lampstand, and Jar, and Unhewn Mountain; for from thee without seed came forth in the latter times Christ our God, even as the Father was well pleased.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

When the fire was harmless unto Thy children in the furnace, O Savior, and harmed them not, then the three, as with one mouth, sang and blessed, saying: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Seeing me wretched with transgressions, O Pure One, and with bodily pleasures, and blown about toward the depth of destruction: let thy tender mercy hasten and deliver me, O All-Pure Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

A refuge and mighty protection, and deliverance unto all the faithful, O Theotokos: be thou my Guide unto the Creator, granting me deliverance from transgressions by thy tender compassion.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou didst destroy the curse, O Pure One, having given birth to Christ the Joy; do thou also loose the curse of sin which has settled upon me, by thy power, O All-Immaculate One, and grant me joy.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

We thy servants ever pray thee, O All-Pure and Spotless One, day and night praying with contrite mind: grant us deliverance from our transgressions by thy prayers.

[f. 27rA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him at Whom angels and all hosts tremble as Creator and Lord, sing, ye priests; glorify, ye children; bless, ye peoples; and exalt Him above all unto all ages!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Arise, anticipate and conquer the enemies who ever disturb and afflict us, manifesting thy power, O Holy Virgin, Helper of the world.

*Troparion:*

Thou didst save the world from death and corruption by thy divine Childbirth, O Good Virgin; now also by thy prayers snatch us away and save us who praise thee from passions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Great and ineffable is thy strength, O Virgin, having unconquerable divine compassions and tender mercy; therefore save us who truly call upon thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

As an animate Vineyard, O Virgin, thou didst put forth for us the ripe Cluster, which poured out the wine of remission and drove away the drunken stupor of sin.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

The light-bearing cloud, wherein the Master of all descended from heaven like dew upon the fleece, and became incarnate for our sake, He Who is without beginning having become man: all of us magnify thee as the pure Mother of our God.

[f. 27vA] *Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

In the sea of life, weighed down greatly with carnal pleasures, I fall down before thee and cry aloud: Have mercy on me who flee unto thee, O Sovereign Lady, and stretch out unto me a hand of salvation, delivering me from the depth of destruction.

*Troparion:*

Unto thee I confess my sins, O Virgin; before thy face I present my shame, and cry from my soul: Have mercy and cleanse me, O All-Pure One, for after God I have placed my steadfast hope upon thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thee who didst bear without seed the Creator and Master I call upon from my soul, and cry diligently: Save me from my secret transgressions, and deliver me from the unquenchable fire, O All-Pure and Good Theotokos.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Impassable Door, Gate of Paradise, Path of those who are being saved, Way of salvation: together with the Martyrs and Prophets, with the Righteous and the Venerable, and with the Divine Apostles, pray, O Pure One, that our souls may be saved!

*Then: It is truly meet... and the usual Dismissal.*

---

# TONE 2

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 28rA] *On Saturday at Small Compline,*[^18]

*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*(Which let those who are slandered, in sorrows and in tribulations chant)*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

In the deep of old, the overwhelming power drowned all Pharaoh's host; and the Incarnate Word consumed all-wicked sin: the supremely glorious Lord, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Readily attentive unto all in afflictions, and helping in sorrow, O Good Theotokos: grant grace unto those who dare to hymn thee, O Sovereign Lady, Joy of the sorrowful!

*Troparion:*

Having acquired an abundant grace, O Sovereign Lady: by thy bold prayer marvelously deliver me from tribulations, thy poor servant, O Joy of the sorrowful!

*Glory...*

[f. 28vA] *Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Deliver us, we pray thee, who flee unto thee, O Theotokos, from enemies visible and invisible, and destroy every counsel of those who war against us.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Take away the reproach of men and the slanders of conspirators from me, O Theotokos, I pray thee, that with zeal I may glorify the Lord Whom thou didst nourish.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish us through Thyself, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put transgressions to death, and plant Thy fear in our hearts, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Destroy the conspiratorial wiles of enemies, O All-Immaculate Theotokos, fail not with thy prayers, guarding us who praise thee.

*Troparion:*

Look down, O Pure One, with thy merciful eye, and deliver me from every plot of enemies visible and invisible, blinding the pupils of their eyes.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Quench the evil assault of enemies which burns like fire, who ever seek to destroy us, O Virgin, with the dew of thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Unquenchable Lamp, Ray shining ever: enlighten me who am held fast by the night of sorrows by thy prayers, O thou who didst give birth to the Sun of glory, Christ!

[f. 29rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O Merciful One, Thine incomprehensible power.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

We pray thee, the bridge of salvation, sleepless prayer, firm protection: have tender mercy and behold our unbearable grief, diseases, afflictions, passions, and visit us in good season, O Mother of God, granting speedy joy!

*Troparion:*

We are not deprived, O Sovereign Lady, of thy protection in sorrows. Therefore now also help us speedily, who are grievously burdened, stretching forth thy hand, O Pure One; be the healing of our diseases, O Mother of God, granting speedy joy!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The lawless have not hoped in thee, O Sovereign Lady, but have hoped in a boastful tongue, in a deceitful tongue, which is ever enviously sharpened, roaring unjustly to shed the blood of a neighbor; but do thou, O Pure One, break their jawbones!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Subdue, O Sovereign Lady, the exalted neck of the enemies who boast greatly, and their counsels and concealments of malice, who prompt their hearts unto evil things against me all day long; but grant strength

[f. 29vA] *Theotokion (continued):*

and victory unto those who call upon thee, O Mother of God, granting speedy joy!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

The glowing coal having appeared, the Sun shone forth from the virginal womb, granting the enlightenment of the knowledge of God unto those who wander in darkness.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

True Intercessor, Hope of Christians, O Full of Grace: accept supplications from us who diligently call upon thee and pray unto thee.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee as the Fount of life, O Pure One, which pours out the waters of immortality, we earthborn call thee blessed.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The enemy has armed himself subtly against us, desiring to destroy us with his tongue as with a sword; but anticipate him by thy might, O Birthgiver of God!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Who shall recount the abyss of thy protections, O Pure One, and the strength of thy power? Therefore speedily anticipate us who are in distress.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O my God!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Being the Mediatress of compassions, appear now unto those who call upon thee, and deliver us from all tribulations and perils, O Bride of God!

[f. 30rA] *Troparion:*

Destroy the evil deeds of the enemy, and stop the slanders of the unjust, O Blessed and All-Pure One, delivering the innocent from sorrows.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Surrounded by cruel sins and overwhelmed by perilous distresses, we flee under thy divine Protection, O Mother of Christ God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth to the Incomprehensible Lord, thou wast shown to be a Virgin again after childbirth. O the marvelous wonder wrought in thee, O Bride of God!

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*

Fervent prayer, and saving insurmountable rampart, fountain of mercy, refuge for the world: earnestly we cry unto thee: O Theotokos and Sovereign Lady, anticipate us, and deliver us from perils, who alone art a speedy protectress.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Thy marvelous Nativity, O Christ, from the Virgin prefiguring clearly, Thou didst preserve the youths unburnt in the furnace, who sang in hymns: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Ark of tender mercy, O Pure Virgin! for thou loosest measureless griefs and tribulations of those who call upon thee in distress and circumstance. Therefore now also help those who praise thee, O Blessed One.

[f. 30vA] *Troparion:*

Manifest thy speedy protection, manifest it, for thou art able as the Mother of God, unto those who call upon thee from the heart, falling down with tears: speedily soothe also the suffering of thy servants without delay.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The mouths of men, like cruel lions, having concealed a grave, were opened bitterly to swallow me up: but do thou, O Theotokos, having appeared as the hope of the hopeless, cast down their strength, O Blessed One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Let the enemies see and be put to shame, let them understand and behold thy power contending against them on our behalf: and cast them down into the lowest pit, O Blessed One, hope of the hopeless.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew youths, and changed the flame into dew, God: sing, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Our refuge, and the rejoicing of the world, O Mother of God: make haste with tenderheartedness, and speedily grant thy grace unto us who are afflicted, and protect thy servants, O Good One.

[f. 31rA] *Troparion:*

A cruel counsel have the assemblies of the godless cruelly taken against us, as of old Ahithophel;[^19] but we cry aloud: by thy prayers break it in pieces; cast down their strength, O Theotokos.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Unto those who call upon thee, O Theotokos, from the soul in perplexity, in every tribulation, and in diverse sicknesses, and cruel perils: speedily give ear, O Sovereign Lady, delivering them ever by thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

May thy name, O Theotokos, be glorified on earth: for He Who is born of thee granted thee as a mighty hope and rampart unto us sinners; for through thee every breath has recourse unto God.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Every tongue is at a loss to praise thee worthily, and even an intellect from the world above is in perplexity to hymn thee, O Theotokos: nevertheless, being Good, accept our faith, for thou knowest our divine love; for thou art the protectress of Christians, and thee do we magnify.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Let every tongue that devises deceit be stopped; let lying lips be silenced, and mouths that speak iniquity unjustly against the Righteous One, through pride and hostile envy together, O Theotokos, by thy prayer and that of the saints of Christ.

[f. 31vA] *Troparion:*

Watchful in prayers, the pure Birthgiver of God, do we all invoke, held fast by sickness and sorrow, crying aloud: O Pure Sovereign Lady, speedily deliver thy servants ever from the afflictions that hold us fast, for after God we have no other protectress.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

A great refuge unto those who sail, a calm haven unto those tossed by storms art thou, O Theotokos. Therefore we also have recourse unto thee, crying aloud: Let us not be put to shame, O Mother of true life: but with Thanksgiving and fervor let us magnify thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Accept, O All-Pure Maiden, this divine hymn, bestowing grace upon those who hope in thee, and unceasingly ask peace to be sent unto the Churches, and unto our sovereign ruler victories and triumphs: that every Christian nation may magnify thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: after the Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 32rA] *On Sunday at Small Compline,*[^20]

*As indicated on Saturday at Small Compline.*

*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Of old the all-surpassing power drowned the entire host of Pharaoh in the deep; and the Incarnate Word consumed all-destructive sin: for the Lord is exceedingly glorious, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Being formed out of thee on our behalf, the Unwaning Sun manifestly showed thee to be a rational and most luminous Heaven. Therefore deliver me from the stench of passions: that with love I may hymn thee, the only Mother and Virgin.

*Troparion:*

Having carried the noetic Coal, as with tongs,[^21] and having cleansed human nature, I pray thee, O Sovereign Lady: cleanse the filth of my many transgressions, and deliver me from defiling passions by thy prayers.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to Christ, the Fountain of mercy, O All-Pure One, and having opened Eden unto men: open unto me the doors of thy mercy, O Theotokos, and grant forgiveness of sins, O Sovereign Lady of the world.

*Both now...*

[f. 32vA] *Theotokion:*

Sunder the bonds of my transgressions, O Sovereign Lady, praying unto thy Son and God, and cast down the sin that torments me ever, O Virgin: that being saved I may hymn thee always, O All-Immaculate One.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Having established me upon the rock of faith, Thou hast enlarged my mouth against my enemies; for my spirit rejoices in singing: There is none holy as our God, and there is none righteous beside Thee, O Lord!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Drive away the mist of my passions and my darkening by the rays of thy prayers, and enlighten me, O All-Pure Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth to Christ Who shone forth before the morning star, the Unwaning Sun from the Sun.

*Troparion:*

By thy childbearing, O All-Pure One, thou hast raised up the fallen and shattered tabernacle of David thy forefather. Therefore raise me up also, O Theotokos, thy servant who am shattered by passions and transgressions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Be unto me a weapon, O Virgin, driving away sins that consume my soul, and show me forth as a second Paradise, free from assault and guarded by thee, bearing the flowers of the Spirit.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having stretched out the hand of tender compassion, receive me, O Mother of God, and snatch from every condemnation him who flees unto thee and falls down before thee, and calls upon thy help.

[f. 33rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I hymn Thee, O Lord, for I heard Thy report and was afraid; for Thou didst come unto me, seeking me who had strayed: therefore I glorify Thy great condescension unto me, which is my renewal.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to the Fountain of dispassion, heal me who am wounded by many passions, O All-Immaculate Maiden, and snatch me from eternal fire, O only God-graced One.

*Troparion:*

By the remedy of thy prayers, O Sovereign Lady, and with plaster and oil, heal now the bruises of my soul and sinful wounds, O Pure One: as having given birth to the divine ember of nature.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Loose the bonds of my passions, O Sovereign Lady, and tear asunder the chains of my sins with the weapon of thy prayers, who didst give birth to Christ Who grants deliverance unto those bound in hades.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Heal the passions of my soul which have most wretchedly darkened me, and enlighten my mind, driving far away, O Pure One, the darkness of demons that grieve me and spread nets to bring me into distress.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Enlightenment of those lying in darkness, and salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior: early unto Thee do I awake, O King of peace; enlighten me with Thy radiance, for we know no other God beside Thee.

[f. 33vA] *Troparion:*

As thou didst deliver the world from the ancient curse, O Sovereign Lady, having given birth to Christ, the blessing for the world: deliver me from improper thoughts and from every sorrow; for thou alone art the rejoicing of the faithful.

*Troparion:*

A demonic host surrounds the city of my soul, and attempts vehemently to despoil it, O All-Glorious Sovereign Lady, City of the King of all; defend me with the towered walls of thy prayers, and save me.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

A cruel tempest of corruptions of evil spirits troubles my mind: anticipate speedily, and calm it, who didst give birth to Christ the Almighty, the only Helmsman of all creation.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Make the light of repentance shine upon me, O Mother of God, who am shut up in deadly darkness; stretch out a helping hand unto him who lies prostrate, and by thy prayers raise me up, O Pure One, to do the statutes of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O God!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to eternal Life, O All-Pure One: when my soul departs from the body, put death to death by thy prayers.

[f. 34rA] *Troparion:*

The man-hating serpent hunts to devour me; render him powerless, O Sovereign Lady, as thou didst bear Him Who crushed the heads of the serpents.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Me who have walked crooked paths, direct unto the straight path, who didst give birth to the Way of life, Christ our God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Heal the passions of my soul, and overcome the perplexity of grief, O All-Pure One, who didst bear God for the salvation of men.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*

Being a fountain of tender mercy, vouchsafe mercy unto us, O Theotokos; look upon the people who have sinned; manifest thy power as always: for hoping in thee, we cry aloud unto thee, Rejoice! as once did Gabriel, the Supreme Commander of the Bodiless Powers.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The all-wise youths served not the golden image, but went themselves into the flame, and mocked their gods. Amid the flame they cried aloud, and an Angel bedewed them, saying: The prayer of your lips has been heard.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

A wave of passions troubles my mind and shakes my soul: but do thou who didst bear the Lord, Who by divine command bridles the surging sea, O Virgin:

[f. 34vA] *Troparion:*

turn it unto the calm of dispassion, and stay the agitation of the flesh.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee as the portal of the noetic Orient, which appeared from Heaven, I thy servant pray: may I enter Heaven through thee, O Virgin. Therefore receive me, O Sovereign Lady, and guide me by the light.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

By His voluntary crucifixion upon the Cross, Christ thy Son put to shame the principalities of darkness, and destroyed the corrupting power of death: through Whom, O All-Pure Sovereign Lady, put to death the passionate mind of my flesh.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Cease not to pray, O Pure One, unto God Who was born of thee, for those who flee with faith unto thee and ask deliverance, the only Protectress of the world, hope and defense of the hopeless, a constant help unto those distressed in cruel afflictions.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Looking not upon the golden image, the all-glorious youths, seeing the immutable living image of God, sang amid the flame: Let all material creation hymn the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

O Tabernacle fashioned by God, being the Holy of Holies, having within thee the Great High Priest Who revealed the type of the ancient tabernacle: show forth my heart, O Virgin, as a holy Tabernacle of Christ the King of all of us on high.

[f. 35rA] *Troparion:*

O divine Ark who didst carry the tablets of the Covenant, thy Son Christ Who cannot be circumscribed, O Virgin, the All-Pure Word of God: grant through thy prayers that He may be written upon the tablet of my soul by the finger of God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

From thy womb, O All-Immaculate Sovereign Lady, was cut the precious Stone as from a mountain, and as the only Almighty Ruler He crushed the steps of every delusion: through Whom do thou now destroy the images of passions of my soul, having crushed the jaws of noetic enemies.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

With living waters do thou refresh my heart which has withered through the flame of sin, O Virgin Theotokos, preserving me ever in compunction as with fear I sing: Let all creation hymn the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

The Luminary, God Who shone forth before the sun, Who came unto us in the flesh from the Virgin's womb, ineffably incarnate: O Blessed and Pure One, thee, O Theotokos, do we magnify.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

The Sun of righteousness, Christ, didst thou cause to shine forth, O Pure One, and wast shown to be a most luminous Heaven, through Whom the night of my passions [f. 35vA] *Troparion:*

scatter by thy prayers, and enlighten my soul, and illuminate my heart compunctiously.

*Troparion:*

Judge, O Theotokos, and plead the cause against my cruel adversary, and deliver me from his affliction: for thou didst bear the Good and Righteous Judge, Who in the depths of the Godhead condemned death.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

He Who as God brought the incorporeal beings into existence, was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Maiden. Therefore deliver me thy servant from the encirclement of bodily passions and from carnal wisdom by thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst loose the sorrow of Eve, having given birth to the New Adam, Who created Adam's nature of old: therefore also loose the handwriting of my transgressions, delivering me from tribulations, sorrows, and passions, O Virgin Unwedded.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 36rA] *On Monday at Small Compline,*[^22]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Of old the all-surpassing power drowned the entire host of Pharaoh in the deep; and the Incarnate Word consumed all-destructive sin: for the Lord is exceedingly glorious, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having blossomed an ever-blooming wreath of praise in the Spirit, the Church, O Pure One, offers it unto thee, the Bride of God, in song, singing with the Archangel Gabriel: Rejoice! and purely crowning thee, O Queen full of grace.

*Troparion:*

Having cultivated the life-bearing Vine, Christ God, O All-Hymned Mother of God, holy land of the Father, thou hast appeared beyond understanding, O God-graced One, and hast watered the whole world with a life-bearing drink, O Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Cruelly surged about and drowning in the transgressions of the whole world, thou didst bear, O All-Pure One, God and Lord of all of us, and Christ the Helmsman, Who faithfully directs us unto the saving haven.

*Both now...*

[f. 36vA] *Theotokion:*

A refuge and city of salvation, all we the faithful pray unto thee, Mary, Mother of God, and warmly beseech thee: accept our supplications, of thy faithful servants, and loose us all from the condemnation of transgressions.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish us through Thyself, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put to death transgressions; and plant Thy fear in our hearts, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

The beginning of the turning away of God from men was the deception of Eve; but the Holy Theotokos brought God back unto us again.

*Troparion:*

The glorious Joachim gives birth unto thee for the world, the treasure of life, O All-Hymned One, and rejoices in goodly offspring concerning thee: as hope having come through barren parents.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

From Anna joy blossomed forth unto the world: for as a Virgin thou didst bear the King, and our race rejoices together with thee, having been loosed from the ancient curse.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The bush in Sinai prefigured thy marvelous Nativity, O Virgin: for thou wast unburnt by the fire of the Godhead, having received Him in thy womb, O Pure One.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thy unsearchable power, O Compassionate One.

[f. 37rA] *Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

As a ladder set up, in a manner befitting God, did Jacob foresee thee, O Virgin, upon the summit whereof God was established.

*Troparion:*

The angelic condescension, which is the coming of the Word unto us, was manifested through thee, O All-Hymned One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Blessed are thy womb, O All-Hymned One, and thy breasts, O Blessed Virgin: for for their sake we all have found life.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Mystically we hymn thee, O Mother of God, with Orthodox voices: as the Church preserved by thy prayers.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Enlightenment of those lying in darkness, and salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior: early unto Thee do I awake, O King of peace; enlighten me with Thy radiance, for I know no other God beside Thee.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

As more honorable than the Ark of the Law do we hymn thee, O Theotokos Mary: for thou, O All-Hymned One, didst carry the Creator and God of all, as the tablets.

*Troparion:*

As the throne of the Word of God do we glorify thee, O Theotokos: upon which God, sitting as man, appeared and became higher than the Cherubim.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou didst loose, O Maiden, all the human race from bitter bondage, and with the freedom of Christ didst honor female nature in thy divine Nativity.

*Both now...*

[f. 37vA] *Theotokion:*

Thou didst bear a Son, O Virgin, and women manifestly conquer the enemy: therefore they flee unto thee, O Maiden, holding their children.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O God!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

O Pure Theotokos, thou art joy unto the world; faithfully with the Angel we cry unto thee, Rejoice! O Virgin: vouchsafe us also thy joy, and destroy our grief.

*Troparion:*

The dwelling of unassailable joy, thee, O Ever-Virgin Mother, do we faithfully praise: vouchsafe us also to be the dwelling of the unassailable Most High.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Higher than the Heaven of heavens didst thou appear, O Bride of God, in all divine glory: for from thee our God shone forth and appeared unto us.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Now has female nature rejoiced; now has grief ceased, joy has blossomed: for the Mother gave birth unto Joy, the Savior Christ God.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2.*

*Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїа сꙋ́щи):*

Beholding the cruel slothfulness of my soul and the exhaustion of my heart, O Mother of God, heal them by thy prayers, and vouchsafe me the portion of the saved,

[f. 38rA] *Sessional Hymn (continued):*

delivering me from darkness and torment: for thou art my only hope and consolation.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Unto the golden image served in the plain of Dura, the three youths paid no heed regarding the ungodly command, but cast into the midst of the flame, being refreshed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

The fleece of Gideon of old prefigured the descent of the Word of God upon thee, O Pure One: for as dew didst thou receive conception, O Virgin unwrathful. Therefore we all cry unto thee: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One.

*Troparion:*

New and fearful, faithful and marvelous are thy mysteries, O Mary, Mother of our God: for through thee we all were reconciled unto God and the Sovereign Lord. Therefore with the Angels we now sing unto thee: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Gideon of old foretold thy divine Childbearing clearly, O Virgin: he brings the bowl filled with water from the wringing of the fleece; for the Word in a divine manner dwelt wholly within thee, O All-Pure One: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth to God and the Savior of all, O Mary, thou hast become the restoration of the despairing, and unto sinners

[f. 38vA] *Theotokion (continued):*

cleansing, renewal, and hope unto the hopeless, and help unto those who hymn thee: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew youths, and changed the flame into dew, God: sing, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

A New Paradise hast thou appeared unto us, O All-Pure Theotokos, having brought forth not the tree of death, but of life, as a Garden that seedlessly budded forth the Lord: by Whom all we are nourished with immortal life.

*Troparion:*

The entire Church of Christ hymns thy Nativity, O Theotokos: for all sinful men are saved, fleeing with love unto thee: for Christ came upon earth to save men.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The foremother is freed from condemnation through thee, O Virgin Theotokos, and now women suffer for Christ, and female nature rejoices: as Thecla the protomartyr cries aloud.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

None has perished, O Pure One, who in Orthodox manner acquired the hope of faith toward thee, O Virgin Mother of God: except only those who through envy turn away from thee, and do not bow down before thine inscribed image.[^23]

[f. 39rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

The Luminary, God Who shone forth before the sun, Who came unto us in the flesh, ineffably incarnate from the Virgin's womb: O Blessed and Pure One, thee, O Theotokos, do we magnify.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Incline thine ear unto me, O Most Holy Virgin, who praise thy Nativity with faith, in words of praise; and having accepted the song of my lips as the widow's mites, ask forgiveness for my sins.

*Troparion:*

Thy beauty shines, the brightness of purity flashes, O Pure One, and more than these shines forth thy Nativity: for God, the Creator of the sun and of all creation, was born of thee. Therefore all we magnify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Hymning thee in a manner befitting God in songs as the Light of Purity, the Rod of Virginity, and the Mother of God, O Theotokos, with the voice of praise we pray unto thee: establish us in virginity, and preserve us in purity.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

When thou didst preserve body and soul undefiled unto God, O Pure One, Christ the King desired thy beauty, and revealed thee as the Mother of His incarnation, O All-Glorious Mary, treasury of my salvation.

*Then, It is truly meet: and the usual Dismissal.*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 39vA] *On Tuesday at Small Compline,*[^24]

*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Come, ye people, let us sing a song unto Christ God, Who divided the sea and guided the people whom He brought forth from the bondage of Egypt, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

My speech is completely inadequate to hymn thee, O Pure One, for the darkness of my sins covers me. But O Mother of God, receive me who am sorely pressed:

*Troparion:*

Under thy protection do I flee, O All-Pure One, from countless sins and snares: do thou make God and the Sovereign Lord favorably inclined toward me, O All-Hymned One, and save me.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou art my fervent mercy-seat, O Sovereign Lady; fleeing unto thee from foolishness I have been saved, and find salvation of the soul: for thou art able to do all things, as thou art the Mother of the God of all.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The deceiver of souls drew me into the pit of destruction: but stretch out thy mighty hand unto me, O Virgin Theotokos, and speedily lead me up unto the light.

[f. 40rA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish us through Thyself, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put to death transgressions; and plant Thy fear in our hearts, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having fallen among many robbers, wretch that I am, I was stripped, wounded, and abandoned: but do thou, O Pure Theotokos, despise me not.

*Troparion:*

Of old the serpent stripped Adam, having deceived him through the tree; and by the same means he easily took captive my soul: but by thy prayers, O Sovereign Lady, show mercy upon me.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I bring my secrets unto thee, my true Protectress and defense: that thy Son may not expose them before me at His righteous judgment.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Have mercy upon us, O Pure One, have mercy: for we have no salvation at all from works. Therefore with fervent faith we cry unto thee: Have mercy upon thy servants.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I hymn Thee, O Lord, for I heard Thy report and was afraid; for Thou didst come unto me, seeking me who had strayed: therefore I glorify Thy great condescension unto me, which is my renewal.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Despise me not, O Sovereign Lady, who am incurably sick and have become impoverished: but give me the oil of thy grace, O Virgin, [f. 40vA] *Troparion (continued):*

and through thy providential cares, enrich me from God with incorruptible gold.

*Troparion:*

I have been stripped of all good things, and I lie in evils, clothed more than all who fell of old. But adorn me with the former, and deliver me from the latter, O Bride of God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I have surpassed the harlot in fornications, and the publican always in extortion, in word and intention: yet vouchsafe me, O Sovereign Lady, before the end, to acquire the repentance of them both.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

I have settled in a sorrowful wilderness, having distanced myself from thee, O Parent of God: who will give me wings that I may fly and come unto thee, my hope, saving the faint-heartedness of me, a wretched one?

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord: direct us in the light of Thy commandments, for we know no other God beside Thee.

*Troparion:*

As the Never-Setting Light shone forth from thy womb unto those upon earth, O Sovereign Lady: enlighten my foul-smelling soul, O Pure One, and drive away all gloom of my heart.

*Troparion:*

A cruel night covers me, of my crafty and evil deeds. But I cry unto thee, O Sovereign Lady:

[f. 41rA] *Troparion (continued):*

direct me unto the divine light of thy Son and Master, O All-Immaculate One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Receive me also, O Pure One, as thy Son and the Creator of mankind received the prodigal son, crying aloud with him: I have truly sinned; and save me, O Sovereign Lady.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Heal with thy prayers my heart wounded by the assaults of the evil one, O Most Pure One, who ineffably didst give birth to Him Who in the flesh was wounded upon the Cross.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O my God!

*Troparion:*

I have fallen into the pit of sin, and held fast by fear, I look not up unto God Who forgets not: but I cast myself upon thee, O Bride of God.

*Troparion:*

My heart has been grievously crushed by the wave of inscrutable transgressions; guide me by thy all-powerful prayer, O Virgin Theotokos.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Vouchsafe me to see the beauty of thy glory when the time comes for me to be separated from my flesh: for thou art my support, O Sovereign Lady, that I may find release from bonds.

*Both now...*

[f. 41vA] *Theotokion (continued):*

By thy prayers unto God, deliver thy servants who flee with faith unto thee, from assaults, perils, and afflictions, O Most Holy Sovereign Lady.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*

Thee do we magnify, O Theotokos, crying aloud: Rejoice, O Rod from which without seed God budded forth, having slain death by the Tree!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Unto the golden image the all-wise youths gave no heed, but went themselves into the flame and mocked their gods, and amid the flame they cried aloud, and an Angel bedewed them, saying: The prayer of your lips has been heard.

*Troparion:*

Now the enemy serpent strives to take me into his belly, for from every side he brings unbearable temptations upon me, cutting off all my progress: but anticipate and deliver me, O Virgin Mother, from his evil plot.

*Troparion:*

The adversary defiles and confounds my senses and mind, and strives to cast me into ruinous perturbation.[^25] Therefore I cry aloud unto thee: my only refuge, O Bride of God, deliver me from the hand of this deceitful one.

*Glory...*

[f. 42rA] *Troparion:*

O my Christ, Who of old didst deliver the three youths from the burning furnace: do Thou Thyself, O Word, through the supplications of Thy Virgin Mother, also deliver and bedew me from the flame which I have kindled by boundless evils.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Predatorily finding my heart, the corrupter put it to death. But do thou clothe me with divine power, O Mother unwedded, and vouchsafe me to conquer him, that with faith I may call unto thee: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto God Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew children and changed the flame into coolness: sing, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

Grievously buried by my many evils, I am utterly unable to lift up my eye unto Heaven; but I cry unto thee: have mercy, O only Theotokos, upon me who have fallen.

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Pure One, from the wrath of thy Son and God into which I have fallen; and in the hour when He will make inquiry, be thou my help, and deliver me from the goats on the left hand.

*Glory...*

[f. 42vA] *Troparion (continued):*

Raise me up, who am mentally slain by my countless evils, O Virgin Bride of God; and by thy prayer deliver me from every delusion of the soul-corrupting enemy and murderer.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O my soul, uncorrected by good works, how hast thou evilly blackened thy first beauty? How hast thou cast away all defenses before thy Creator, and received wicked things? But come, and run with tender compunction unto the Theotokos.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

God the Word of God, Who by ineffable wisdom came to renew Adam, who evilly fell through the eating of corruption, and Who for our sake was ineffably incarnate of the Holy Virgin: with one accord we the faithful magnify Him in songs.

*Troparion:*

By grievous deeds I have truly defiled my senses, and I am full of all shamelessness. But cleanse me, O Most Pure One, asking for me a time of tender compunction, that I may unceasingly magnify thee.

*Troparion:*

Justify me, O Theotokos, when the hidden deeds of each man shall appear openly: for thou truly hast the power; and deliver me from darkness, and [f. 43rA] *Troparion (continued):*

place me in the realms of light by thy prayers, and where there is ineffable joy.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I beseech Thee, O Word: before the coming of the end, grant me fervent repentance, and the compunction of tears, together with chastity, humility, and divine love, and peace unto Thy flock, through the supplications of her who bore Thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Higher than intelligible and sensible beings in glory and honor, O Virgin, except God alone: abhor me not, who have surpassed in transgressions both ancient and new men who have sinned upon earth; but by thy prayer save me.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after the Our Father: the usual Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Coronation of the Most Holy Theotokos / The Holy Trinity and the Virgin Mary amid the angelic hosts)*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 43vA] *On Wednesday at Small Compline,*[^26]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Come, ye people, let us sing a song unto Christ God, Who divided the sea and guided the people whom He brought forth from the bondage of Egypt, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Devoutly confessing thee with soul and lips to be the true Theotokos, O Pure One: save me now from cruel perils, diseases, and transgressions, O Most Pure One.

*Troparion:*

All have known the abyss of good things lying in thee: therefore fleeing diligently unto thy divine protection, O Theotokos, we are saved.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Pray for us who sing thy praise, O Most Pure One, unto Him Who was incarnate of thy most pure and precious blood, that we may be delivered from transgressions and bitter sicknesses.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thee as a boast have all we the faithful acquired, a refuge and support, and rejoicing, and salvation for souls, a hope and a wall, O woman full of the grace of God.

[f. 44rA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish us within Thyself, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put to death transgressions; and plant Thy fear in our hearts, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Through thy prayers, O Pure One, grant that God Whom thou didst bear may be propitious unto thy servants who flee unto thy protection and faithfully bow down before thy Childbearing.

*Troparion:*

Give ear unto the voices of my prayer from the depth of my heart, O Virgin, which I offer unto thee, O All-Praised One; and save me from passions and assaults.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Direct all my life, O Virgin, my hope and protectress, turning away the assaults and violent temptations of the enemy, O Bride of God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having carried upon thine arms the hypostatic Wisdom of God as the Mother of God: pray that we may be delivered from ignorance and delusion.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thy unsearchable power, O Compassionate One.

*Troparion:*

Unto the wounds of my soul and the defilements of the flesh, O Sovereign Lady who didst bear God, grant release:

[f. 44vA] *Troparion (continued):*

for I have defiled myself with passions and thoughts; and amid the worldly tempests of life, O Virgin, establish me in hope and faith.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Deliver me from assault, O Mother of God, and from storm and perils, by thy prayers, O only All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Storm-tossed by the waves of this life, snatch me away, O Virgin, guiding me unto thy haven.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord: direct us in the light of Thy commandments, for we know no other God beside Thee.

*Troparion:*

Having an invincible weapon against the continuous temptations of the enemy, we are manifestly delivered from every malice, knowing thee, O Pure Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

O Fulfillment of the Law, higher than the Cherubim: thou didst bear the Incarnate Word of God, the Only-Begotten Son: implore Him on behalf of thy servants.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Carrying in thine arms the Creator of all things, O Pure One: grant Him through thy prayers to be propitious unto us who now flee unto thee from the whole heart.

*Both now...*

[f. 45rA] *Theotokion (continued):*

From the consolation and sickness of soul, a supplication do I, a wretched one, offer unto thee: who alone didst bear the Word, the Cause of tender mercies, have compassion and save me.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O my God!

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be the haven of salvation, as I sail the sea of this much-sorrowing life, I call upon thee, O Sovereign Lady: be thou the helmsman of my soul.

*Troparion:*

I have been stripped of the garment of my chastity, having been afflicted: but O Ever-Virgin Mother, who didst bear God, grant me a garment of joy.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I have fallen away from a pure life, living in slothfulness, passionate one that I am: but lead me up, O Blessed Sovereign Lady, uniting me unto the commandments of thy Son.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Vouchsafe me thy mercy, O Theotokos, who didst bring forth the Most Merciful Word, Who by His own Blood delivered mankind from corruption.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2, Podoben: "We have become partakers of the divine" (Бж҃їю бы́хомъ):*[^27]

Vouchsafe me the divine entrance, O Pure One, by thy prayers, O Ever-Virgin; and having torn asunder the cruel bonds of passions, free me from the coming flame.

[f. 45vA] *Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Unto the golden image served in the plain of Dura, the three youths paid no heed regarding the ungodly command, but cast into the midst of the flame, being refreshed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

He Who was incarnate of thee, O Parent of God, was nailed upon the Cross and tore up the handwriting of Adam: implore Him now, O Virgin, that they who call upon thee with faith may be delivered from every malice: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh.

*Troparion:*

Thou art a good hope and protectress unto the faithful, O Sovereign Lady: and now we pray thee to grant an ocean of mercy unto all who hope in thee and call aloud unto thee: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Encompassed by the cruel darkness of life, I found none to sorrow with me or suffer with me: by thy brightness, O Virgin, disperse the darkness of transgressions, and illumine me to sing unto thee: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having been clothed through Baptism in the fair garment of the saving commandments, I, a wretched one, have blackened it through slothfulness. But now I flee unto thee, O Virgin, asking to be clothed again through thee in the garment of salvation.

[f. 46rA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto God Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew children and changed the flame into coolness: sing, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

My mind now faints, having fallen into the depth of dishonor, for from all sides I am encompassed by diverse evils: but do thou heal me, O Virgin, clothing me in the light of dispassion.

*Troparion:*

Having acquired thee through faith as a firm tower of strength, a stair, a protectress, and a defender, we are saved now, singing of thy childbearing, O Pure One, and exalting Him supreme unto all ages.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

We know thee, O Theotokos, as the radiance and fountain of immortality, for thou didst bear the Word of the Immortal Father, Who delivers all from death, and they exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

A stream of healings dost thou ever pour forth unto us the faithful, O Pure One, and receiving grace ungrudgingly, we hymn thy childbearing, O Pure One, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

God the Word of God, Who by ineffable wisdom came to renew Adam, who evilly fell through the eating of corruption, and Who for our sake was ineffably incarnate of the Holy Virgin: with one accord we the faithful magnify Him in songs.

[f. 46vA] *Heirmos (continued):*

and Who for our sake was ineffably incarnate of the Holy Virgin: with one accord we the faithful magnify Him in songs.

*Troparion:*

O Maiden blessed of God, all my hope have I fervently set upon thee: save me, O Mother of the true Life, and pray, O Pure One, that I who magnify thee with faith and love in songs may be filled with ever-existing nourishment.

*Troparion:*

Having appeared as the portal of the Divine Light, O Virgin, enlighten the gloom of my soul with the brightness and flashes of immaterial light, and vouchsafe me, O Pure One, through thine intercessions to be delivered from eternal fire, that I may unceasingly magnify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Beholding those sick in soul and body, and cast into cruel passions, O Sovereign Lady: by thy tender mercy heal and release those now distressed with grief, that with faith and love they may magnify thee in songs.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Into thy womb entered the Son Whom the Pre-Eternal Father begot from the womb; He became perfect man, and revealed thee unto us who faithfully bow down before thine ineffable Childbearing, O Mother of God, as a fountain of graces.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: after the Our Father: and the Dismissal.*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 47rA] *On Thursday at Small Compline,*[^28]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Of old the all-surpassing power drowned the entire host of Pharaoh in the deep; and the Incarnate Word consumed all-destructive sin: for the Lord is exceedingly glorious, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

As fair, as all-comely, as undefiled among women, God chose thee and dwelt in thine immaculate womb: implore Him, O All-Immaculate One, to deliver from the stain of sins all who sing thy praise.

*Troparion:*

Ordained at the right hand, O Pure One, as Queen before the King Who shone forth from thy womb, implore Him, O Bride of God: that He may show me forth as a defender at His right hand in the day of recompense.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to the Heavenly Rain, thou didst wholly renew human nature that had dried up through improper deeds. Therefore I pray, O Bride of God: show forth the parched furrow of my soul also to be fruitful.

*Both now...*

[f. 47vA] *Theotokion (continued):*

Having died by the plantation of knowledge, yet through the Tree of life, O Pure One, we have been called unto eternal life by Christ God, Who beyond understanding blossomed forth from thee, O Theotokos: implore Him with boldness that our souls may be saved.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

The wilderness, the barren Church of the Gentiles, blossomed as a lily at Thy coming, O Lord: wherein my heart was established.

*Troparion:*

Coming forth from thy womb, O All-Immaculate One, the Creator clothed Himself in me, a man, granting a garment of incorruption unto him who had been stripped by many evil deeds.

*Troparion:*

Thou didst give birth to the Most Pure Word of God, O Sovereign Lady: diligently implore Him to have compassion upon my humble soul, which is distressed by salty dishonor.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, O Most Pure One, and with thy potent remedy cure my humble heart, which has been embittered by the serpent's venom.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

As a Mother having boldness before thy Son, O Sovereign Lady: ask help for people who are afflicted, and cast down the arrogance of the lawless.

[f. 48rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Thou didst come from the Virgin, neither an ambassador nor an Angel, but Thou Thyself, O Lord, incarnate, and didst save me, the whole man. Therefore I cry unto Thee: glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

Rain down upon me drops of compunction, O Sovereign Lady, taking away all the burning heat of my heart and my sorrow, and driving far away wrathful assaults.

*Troparion:*

Despise me not, O Most Pure One, who am wounded by the weapon of voluptuousness and lie in sores: but heal me by the Spear and the Blood of thy Son Who was crucified, our God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Enriched by every creation of the Master, yet having become sorely impoverished, vouchsafe me divine grace, O All-Immaculate One: that I may magnify thee as my good Protectress.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Christ, the Father's Radiance, shone forth from thy womb, O Virgin Unwedded, and enlightened the universe by His Crucifixion, and destroyed demonic darkness.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou wast a Mediator between God and men, O Christ God: for through Thee, O Master, we have found an approach from the night of ignorance unto Thy Father, the Source of Light.

[f. 48vA] *Troparion (continued):*

Having given birth to the Way of life, O Most Pure One: direct me now unto the right way, who have been foolishly cast down into the trackless void and the precipice of cruel falls.

*Troparion:*

Mindlessly I became estranged from the knowledge of God; I lived prodigally, having strayed into the far country of passions: but turn me back and save me by thy consolation, O Virgin Pure.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

With thy living waters refresh thy servant who is kindled by the flame of sin and scorched by demonic assaults, O Virgin Mother Most Pure.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Lo, within thy womb, O Most Pure Theotokos, thou didst hold Christ God beyond speech, as Isaiah proclaimed, and beyond nature didst give birth unto Him, O Parent of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O my God!

*Troparion:*

Make me not a joy unto demons at the judgment to come, O Sovereign Lady: but having looked upon me favorably in good time, implore the Judge and thy Son.

*Troparion:*

Having angered Thee with thoughts, O Lord, and with my crafty and unclean deeds, I bring Thy Mother before Thee in prayer: having compassion, save me!

*Glory...*

[f. 49rA] *Troparion (continued):*

Deliver me from condemnation, O Sovereign Lady, who am self-condemned by transgressions, as thou didst bear the Judge and God of all, O All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Implore Jesus the Savior, Whom beyond nature thou didst bear in the flesh, O Virgin Mother Most Pure, that thy servants may be delivered from perils.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2.*

*Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїа сꙋ́щи):*

Beholding Thee hanging dead upon the Tree, O Christ, Thy Virgin Mother wept bitterly and said: O my Son, what is this strange mystery! How dost Thou, Who grantest eternal life unto all, die of Thine own will upon the Cross by a shameful death?

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The god-opposing command of the lawless tyrant raised a high flame: but Christ spread spiritual coolness upon the pious children; He is blessed and exceedingly praised.

*Troparion:*

Being my strength, and song, and salvation, and firm protection, and invincible wall, O Sovereign Lady: conquer the demons that war against me and ever seek to put me to death.

[f. 49vA] *Troparion:*

Having incarnated God by thy virginal blood, thou didst deify mankind, O Virgin. Therefore I pray: deliver me who am defiled by passions and corrupted by the enemy's deceits, by thy prayers.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The furnace prefigured thy Childbearing, O All-Immaculate One: for it did not burn the youths, just as the restless fire did not burn thy womb. Therefore we pray thee: deliver thy servants from eternal fire.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

A most pure conception and incorruptible Childbearing didst thou alone manifest, remaining a Virgin: for thou didst conceive Christ, O Pure One, God over all, Who became man: for the salvation and deliverance of the faithful.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The fiery furnace of old in Babylon divided its operation by divine command, burning the Chaldeans, but cooling the faithful who sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:*

Be zealous for good things, turning away from evils through the care of divine deeds, O my soul, having the Theotokos Who became a maiden from thee, and an unashamed Protectress for all, as she is merciful and loving toward good.

[f. 50rA] *Troparion (continued):*

Thou didst loose the human bond of the ancient condemnation. Therefore I pray thee, O Parent of God: loose every evil bond of my heart, binding me, O Most Pure One, with the divine love of the Creator.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to the Radiance of the Father's glory, O Theotokos: enlighten my heart that mourns over the shaking of transgressions, and show me forth as a partaker of the ever-existing glory, that with faith I may glorify thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The true Sun of righteousness shone forth unto us from thee, O Parent of God, enlightening all things with the rays of the Godhead, the Most High incarnate, Whom we hymn.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

The Son of the Unoriginate Father, God and Lord, incarnate of the Virgin appeared unto us, to enlighten those in darkness, and to gather the dispersed: therefore do we magnify the All-Hymned Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

By tasting food mixed with death, Adam received bitterness from the tree: but thy Son, O Most Pure One, nailed upon the Tree, poured forth the sweetness of dispassion. For this cause do we praise thee.

[f. 50vA] *Troparion:*

A receptacle art thou, who beyond speech didst bear Christ the King and Lord, Who destroyed the kingdom of hades: implore Him diligently, O Maiden, to vouchsafe the Kingdom on high unto all who honor thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Wound my humble heart, O Sovereign Lady, afflicted by voluptuous closures, as thou art the Parent of the Good One, and art all-good: lead me into the good doors of repentance.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having become dead when lifted up upon the Cross, O Jesus, Thou didst thereby slay the serpent. Therefore I cry aloud unto Thee: anoint and revive my soul, O Word, which is slain by crafty deeds, through the prayers of her who bore Thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual. And the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Supplication of the Most Holy Theotokos in the temple / The Virgin before the altar surrounded by saints)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 2)

[f. 51rA] *On Friday at Small Compline,*[^29]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:*

Come, ye people, let us sing a song unto Christ God, Who divided the sea and guided the people whom He brought forth from the bondage of Egypt, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thou art a fountain of life, O Virgin Mother Pure, who didst bear the Author and Lord: having implored the Life of all, save those who praise thee with faith.

*Troparion:*

Having thee as an all-powerful support and protectress, O Most Pure One, we who confess thee to be the Theotokos are saved from the worldly tempests of this life, O All-Immaculate Virgin.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth to the Fountain of immortality, heal me who am wounded by passions, O Maiden, and snatch me away from eternal fire, O only woman full of the grace of God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou art ever a refuge unto the faithful, O Virgin, and a mighty help unto those who run unto thee: save us from every need and harm of the adversary.

[f. 51vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish us within Thyself, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put to death transgressions; and plant Thy fear in our hearts, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Truly as the Golden Censer, and the Jar of Manna, and the Divine Mountain, and the all-comely Palace of God do we name thee, O Virgin.

*Troparion:*

Being the temple and sacred dwelling of the Word, O Theotokos: be thou ever unto me the cleansing of transgressions, O Most Holy Virgin.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Neither earthly tongue nor mind of the bodiless hosts is able to recount thy childbearing: for beyond nature and understanding didst thou, O Theotokos, give birth unto the Creator.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Be thou the support, and refuge, and protection, O Virgin Parent of God, unto those who flee unto thee with faith and confess thee to be the Mother of God.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thy unsearchable power, O Compassionate One.

*Troparion:*

Thee as a great help have we Christians acquired, O Theotokos: snatch us away from cruel perils.

*Troparion:*

O Mother unwedded, Sovereign Lady, who didst conceive God in thy womb: deliver all of us from assaults and afflictions.

*Glory...*

[f. 52rA] *Troparion:*

As an invincible wall and mighty hope have we the faithful acquired thee in assaults, O Pure Theotokos.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having acquired thy prayer as a firm stair, O Sovereign Lady, we are delivered from diverse afflictions.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord: direct us in the light of Thy commandments, for we know no other God beside Thee.

*Troparion:*

Incarnate of thee, O Parent of God, have we the faithful known the Son born without seed, true God, and man by nature: therefore do we glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

Fleeing always under thy protection and defense with faith, O Most Pure One, we are delivered through thee, O Theotokos, from every grievous assault.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Deliver us from assaults and the tempest of thoughts, O Most Pure One, from every wrath, every sin, and famine and pestilence, and eternal torment, O Virgin.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Being our protectress and salvation, and hope for Christians, O Sovereign Lady: save those who ever faithfully hymn thee with love, O All-Hymned Virgin.

[f. 52vA] *Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: bring me up from corruption, O my God!

*Troparion:*

Thou Who of Thine own will workest all things, having of Thine own will settled in the womb of the Mother unwedded, didst enrich with incorruption those ailing with corruption, as Thou art merciful.

*Troparion:*

Higher than the heavenly Hosts and holier art thou, O All-Immaculate One, having beyond nature contained the Uncontainable Word within thy womb.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Direct me unto the path of repentance, O Sovereign Lady, who have strayed on the way of life and frequently fall into sins through waywardness.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Despise not our supplications, O Pure One, for we have set our hope on thee: for thou art a refuge unto thy servants, O Sovereign Lady, and the cleansing of souls.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*[^30]

The Word without seed didst thou conceive, and the only Son, Christ, didst thou bear: a new Heaven didst thou bring forth as a Child, Thine own Creator: therefore do we magnify thee, O Theotokos!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Unto the golden image served in the plain of Dura, the three youths paid no heed regarding the ungodly command, but cast into the midst of the flame, being refreshed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

[f. 53rA] *Troparion:*

The Intelligible Son consubstantial with the Father didst thou incarnate by the power of the Most Holy Spirit, O Sovereign Lady full of the grace of God: implore Him unceasingly to have compassion upon those who sing: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh!

*Troparion:*

O Virgin Pure, Mother unwedded, Holy, Blessed, Restoration of the falling, Deliverance of sinners: save me, the prodigal, save me, crying unto thy Son: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Being a firm haven, an awesome protectress, and an insurmountable wall through prayers unto thy Son for those distressed in needs and storm-tossed in sorrow, O Theotokos: save thy servants from manifold temptations.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou alone art the hope and help of the faithful, O Parent of God: make haste to help thy servants who are submerged in sorrows, perplexed on every side, and in sicknesses, and who flee unto thee with love from the whole soul.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto God Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew children and changed the flame into coolness: sing, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.

[f. 53vA] *Troparion:*

Being a Living Fountain, as thou didst give birth unto the Water of life: bedew my soul that is melted by the flame of sin, O Virgin Theotokos, that I may glorify thee unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

Those put to death and brought down unto mortal and corruptible dust, thou alone hast raised up, having given birth unto Christ our God, the Author of life, O Sovereign Lady, Virgin Pure and Blessed.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Pure Sovereign Lady, from eternal fire and condemnation, and rescue me from men who work evil and seek to trip me up: that I may ever bless thee, whom all creation divinely blesses.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

In the likeness of man was the All-Divine seen from thee, O Pure Virgin: implore Him unceasingly to have mercy upon us who live in malice and tremble before eternal torment.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

God the Word of God, Who by ineffable wisdom came to renew Adam, who evilly fell into corruption through the eating of food, and Who for our sake was ineffably incarnate of the Holy Virgin: with one accord we the faithful magnify Him in songs.

*Troparion:*

Vouchsafe me the divine love of mankind, O Maiden, who ineffably didst give birth unto God, the only Lover of mankind, Who borrowed flesh from thee:

[f. 54rA] *Troparion (continued):*

deliver me from the coming flame and from every torment, who praise thee with love.

*Troparion:*

Having acquired thee as a mighty Protectress, as a hope and wall, and foundation, and firm protection, and invincible support, and stormless haven, and sole sovereign refuge: we are saved, O All-Hymned One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O Virgin, Parent of the Light: drive away the clouds of my soul, and grant me to behold clearly, O Sovereign Lady, the saving beauty that shone forth ineffably from thy most pure womb as a light unto the nations, O All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth to the Divine Light: enlighten my heart darkened by many assaults of passions and alienating thought, O Maiden, ever granting me drops of tears that cleanse the defilements of sin, O Virgin.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: after the Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Vignette: Baroque floral ornament)*

[f. 54vA] *(Woodcut: The Theotokos of the Caves with Saints Anthony and Theodosius of the Caves / The Most Holy Virgin enthroned with the Pre-Eternal Child, Archangels Michael and Gabriel, and the venerable founders of the Kyiv Caves Monastery)*[^31]

---

# TONE 3

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 3)

[f. 55rA] *On Saturday at Small Compline,*[^32]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 3. Heirmos:*

He Who of old by divine nod gathered the waters into one gathering and divided the sea for the people of Israel, this is our God, exceedingly glorified: unto Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

As the Mother of God revealed by the Divine Spirit and the good pleasure of the Father, we the faithful crown thee with songs of praise, O Most Pure Bride of God; and with the Archangel we kiss thee in songs for our salvation.

[f. 55vA] *Troparion:*

From the ribs of Adam was Eve fashioned by the ancient creation of God; but from the womb of the Theotokos appeared Christ the Son our God, Who unchangeably became man, the Pre-Eternal having become subject to time.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

He Who condemned the womb of Eve in sorrows to bring forth children in pain, settled in thy womb, O Virgin Pure, the Son our God: He appeared in the flesh beyond speech and loosed the debt of our first mother.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having fallen into the depth of despair through burdens, we diligently cry unto thee, O Parent of God: O Sovereign Lady, help those submerged by the deeds of crafty transgressions, for after God we have thee as our only hope.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who broughtest all things from non-being, being created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit, O Supreme Almighty Ruler: establish me in Thy love!

*Troparion:*

Aaron's rod that budded without being watered prefigured thee, O Most Pure Theotokos, who without seed didst give birth unto God incarnate immutably.

*Troparion:*

The Prophet foresaw thee in the Spirit as a lampstand carrying the Divine Fire, O Most Pure One, shedding fragrance and eternal life unto those in the world.

*Glory...*

[f. 56rA] *Troparion:*

Let us approach with Gabriel the Archangel, saying unto the Virgin in songs: Rejoice! For through thee was loosed the ancestral curse of condemnation.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having thee as a wall of salvation, O Most Pure Theotokos, we sinners are saved: despise not, O Sovereign Lady, nor reject our supplications.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Thou hast placed Thy steadfast love toward us, O Lord, for Thou didst deliver Thine Only-Begotten Son unto death for our sake: therefore we cry unto Thee, praising: glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

Habakkuk the beholder of God, seeing thee of old, O Most Pure One, proclaimed thee to be the spiritual mountain of virtues overshadowed by the Spirit: for from the south the Word came from thee, having assumed flesh.

*Troparion:*

Daniel saw thee in the Spirit as a great unhewn mountain, manifestly revealing the purity of thy virginity, O Sovereign Lady: from which the Stone was cut, Christ the Word, casting down the deceit of idols.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

David foretold thee as a marvelous and fertile mountain: for the Only-Begotten Son from the Father was well pleased to dwell in thee, O Pure One, being incarnate: therefore in the Spirit we cry unto thee: Rejoice!

*Both now...*

[f. 56vA] *Theotokion (continued):*

Being all-good and a fervent protectress unto sinners and the humble, O Parent of God, Most Pure Sovereign Lady: save thy servants from perils, and afflictions, and sins.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

As Isaiah saw figurately God upon an exalted throne, borne aloft by the Angels of glory, he cried aloud: Woe is me, wretched one! For I have foreseen God incarnate, the Never-Setting Light, Who rules over peace.

*Troparion:*

The Rod of Jesse, the Virgin Mary, brought forth the unfading Flower, God the Unoriginate, without seed from the Divine Spirit and the Father, Who rules over the sovereign kingdoms of the gentiles, upon Whom the nations have hoped.

*Troparion:*

The Prince of Peace came, to reign upon the throne of David, God incarnate of thee, O Theotokos. O wonder! Loosing the womb, He honored the princes of Moab, and revealed thee who didst bear Him as Queen.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Calling upon thy spotless beauty, from which Christ clothed Himself in flesh without seed, O Virgin, Isaiah cries aloud, proclaiming: The Lord of Glory comes upon a light cloud, and driving away the darkness of deceit, He will enlighten us!

*Both now...*

[f. 57rA] *Troparion (continued):*

Having conceived of the Holy Spirit the Word consubstantial with the Father, O Virgin, thou didst give birth unto Him in two natures, being perfect God and perfect man: Whose appearance in the flesh we honor with faith.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

The uttermost abyss of sins has encompassed me, and my spirit fails: but stretching forth Thy high arm, O Master, save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst save Peter!

*Troparion:*

The all-wise one prefigured in songs the image, O All-Hymned One: upon which God, incarnate hypostatically of Thee, rested, and ineffably glorified Thee as He was born.

*Troparion:*

A silver-woven garment wast thou unto the Word, O Virgin All-Hymned: for having taken flesh from thee as a divine Purple, He reigned, being divinely arrayed.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou didst become the receptacle of the divine union, O Bride of God, brighter than gold: for through thee God became as man, and God conversed with men as man.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Put to death the heresy of malice of those who act impiously toward thee, O All-Hymned Virgin: for they melt with envy, beholding the most glorious likeness of thine icon.[^33]

[f. 57vA] *Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 3:*[^34]

In the warmth of faith I cry unto thee, O Theotokos, with unworthy lips and a defiled heart: save me who am submerged in sins, have compassion upon me slain by despair: that being saved I may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Virgin, help of Christians!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

To serve the golden image with Persian honor the three children refused to bow down, singing in the midst of the furnace of fire: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

The bush and the flame joined congruously together and both appearing unconsumed manifestly prefigured thee, O Virgin: for thou didst bear God, and remainest a Virgin.

*Troparion:*

The fleece and dew shown in change unto Gideon prefigured thy childbearing: for thou alone, O Virgin Mother, didst carry in thy womb the Divine Word as rain.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The fire of my sin creates a flame for me worse than Gehenna: quench this by thy mercy, O Pure One, guiding me unto the light through repentance.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Honoring the appearance of thy likeness, O Most Pure Theotokos: as the prototype, we all have thee ever as a protectress and favorable covering before God.

[f. 58rA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The Babylonian furnace burned not the children, nor did the fire of the Godhead kindle the Spirit: therefore with the children we faithfully cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:*

The Father chose thee, an Unwedded Bride: finding thee as a most radiant lily amid thorns, shining with the brightness of beauty, by the Divine Spirit, as a dwelling for the Son.

*Troparion:*

The wall of the heavenly Hosts, the All-Immaculate Virgin, unto salvation do I praise throughout the ages: for she carried their Creator in her womb, Who by unconfused union took flesh from thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having preserved an instealed virginity, O Virgin, thou didst truly appear as the Mother of the Son of God: having become the Bride by the good pleasure of the Father, and an incorruptible receptacle for the glory of the Spirit.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

God, immaterial and invisible by nature, simply and ineffably beyond nature is born man of the Holy Virgin: seen as twofold in one hypostasis, in which He is seen in songs.
