Calm the raging wave of my soul: for a multitude of transgressions, temptations, and sorrows have risen up against me; but do thou thyself save me, O Sovereign Lady.

[f. 92rA] *Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^55]

Fleeing all unto thy protection, O Virgin, we cry unto thee: accept the supplication from us, and pray unceasingly unto the Lover of mankind that thy servants may be saved.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

In the furnace the fire slew the Chaldeans, who unjustly trapped the righteous, but [spared] the Abrahamic children; exceedingly praised Lord, God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

Swiftly destroying the oppression of the Hagarenes with the sword of thy prayers, O Mary, preserve thy people and thy flock, who cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

O Tabernacle of equal majesty, accept me who flee unto thee, lest the enemy pursuing to destroy me seize me, as I sing unto thy Son, O Mother of God: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O Mother of God, Mary, swiftly anticipate thy servant, who is submerged in tempest-tossed temptations and has no help, yet cries unto thee: O my hope, Theotokos, have mercy on me!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Dispel now human temptations, since they are the cause of sins, O Good Theotokos, by thy divine prayers, and deliver thy servants from grievous transgressions and from every temptation.

[f. 92vA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

O Redeemer of all, Almighty One, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst bedew those who believed, and didst teach them to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:*

A lawless nation has risen up against us, boasting to destroy thy servants; having consumed them, O Most Pure One, shelter those that cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:*

Thy many compassions mercifully save us, O only Mother of God, from sinful judgment and diverse temptations: for having given birth unto God, thou dost also have mercy upon His world.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Have tenderhearted compassion now upon my supplication, and grant me joy instead of sorrow, that I may sing of thee, O Sovereign Lady, and cry unto thy Son: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Since thou art my strength and helper, I fear not the rage of enemies, but sing of thee, O Sovereign Lady, and cry unto thy Son: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Eve through the disease of disobedience brought in the curse, but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of thy bearing in the womb didst make blessing bud forth unto the world: therefore we all magnify thee.

[f. 93rA] *Troparion:*

With the radiance of the Light that shone forth from thee, O Virgin, enlighten my mind, driving away the gloom of sin and consuming the mist of my sloth, as I diligently fall down before thee, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

Having ineffably borne Christ God, O Theotokos Mary, ever pray Him that thy servant may be saved from calamities, O Mother unwedded.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Mighty Deliverer of those that hope in thee, guide me unto the quiet haven of eternal life, O Theotokos, by thy most powerful prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth unto the Lord Jesus, O All-Lauded Virgin, pray Him that I may be delivered from eternal torment, O only Protectress of the sorrowful, full of divine grace.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: The Mother of God enthroned with Christ Emmanuel within an architectural baldachin, inscribed: "Rejoice, Habitation of God and the Word")*

[f. 93vA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing the Holy Trinity with cherubim and floral vines)*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 4)

*On Tuesday at Compline,*[^56]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 4. Heirmos:*

I will open my mouth, and it shall be filled with the Spirit, and I will utter a word unto the Queen and Mother: and I will appear radiantly keeping festival, and rejoicing I will sing of her wonders.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having seedlessly given birth in the flesh unto the immeasurable Wisdom and Power of God, even Christ, O All-Immaculate One: thou hast acquired the dominion of strength and divine majesty by thy Childbirth, O Mother of God.

*Troparion:*

O thy Childbirth beyond understanding, O Most Pure One, whereby the multitude of the earthly was enriched with incorruption, and now worthily blesses thee, the Mediatrix of renewal!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Heal thy servant, sick in soul and body, O Virgin, by thine overshadowing: for I have known thee as the Protectress of all who are in grief, as having given birth unto our salvation.

*Both now...*

[f. 94rA] *Theotokion:*

Fiercely submerged in the abyss of griefs and tempest of immeasurable tribulations, stretch forth unto me the hand of salvation, O Sovereign Lady, and lead me forth from the depth of evils.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish thy singers, O Theotokos, thou living and plentiful fount of incorruption, who have spiritually formed a choir; and in thy divine glory, make them worthy of crowns of glory.

*Troparion:*

Thou dost manifestly sprinkle streams of healings upon the sick, O Virgin: for the Lord, being [the Lord of] mercy, Who was born of thee beyond understanding, showed thee as a fountain of compassion, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

A beautiful bridal chamber of the divine Word and a divine bridal hall beyond nature didst thou become, O Virgin Mother. Therefore open unto me the mercy of thy compassions, leading me up unto salvation.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

All my strength has been brought to death by the multitude of immeasurable evils: for I am tossed by a tempest of many griefs; help me, O Theotokos, who didst give birth unto Life and consolation unto those who weep.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Have mercy, O only Mother of God, have mercy, and have compassion on my wretched soul, submerged by evil winds and passions, and before the time of my death, have tenderhearted compassion to cleanse it.

[f. 94vA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Perceiving the unsearchable divine counsel of the Incarnation of Thee, the Most High, from the Virgin, the Prophet Habakkuk cried aloud in wonder: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

Having desired thee as being from royal root and loins, the King of all made thee higher than the Cherubim and Seraphim, and wholly dwelt in thee, O Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

For the sake of mankind's regeneration, the Creator, taking flesh, was born of thee, and showed thee forth as the true Protectress of Christians. Therefore unto thy protection do I flee, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Be protection, defense, and refuge unto me thy servant, O Virgin, and heal me from immeasurable evils, that I may sing unto thee, O Most Pure One: Glory to thy ineffable Childbirth!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

[I am full of] perplexity, tears, and groaning from my impassioned thoughts, and I implore thee, as thou art a fountain of mercy: deliver me from my sicknesses, leading me unto divine contrition.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

All things were amazed at thy divine glory: for thou, O Virgin who knewest not wedlock, didst hold in thy womb the God Who is over all, and didst give birth unto the timeless Son, granting peace unto all who sing of thee.

[f. 95rA] *Troparion:*

Thou dost ever pour forth waters of healing unto all who are sick, O Virgin, as a cloud clothed with Christ the King. Therefore I diligently pray thee: send down the dew of healings unto me who am sick.

*Troparion:*

Unceasingly imploring Him Whom thou didst bear, O Virgin Bride of God, as Savior and Master: grant me release from griefs and sicknesses, and lead me up unto incorruptible joy, forgiving my transgressions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou art my hope, O Most Pure One, and the praise of my salvation; therefore do I flee unto thy protection: abhor me not who am now swallowed up by many sins and sorrowful sicknesses, but anticipate and save me.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O mature Cluster, how dost Thou hang upon the Tree, O Sun of glory? How art Thou bound, Who didst darken the light of the sun in Thy Passion, O Savior? The Ewe-lamb who bore Thee of old cried aloud unto Thee, weeping as a Mother.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I have entered into the depths of the sea, and the tempest of my many transgressions has drowned me: but as God, Thou hast brought up my soul from corruption, O Most Merciful One.

*Troparion:*

The King of all and our God received the form of a servant from thee, O Virgin, and showed thee forth higher than the Cherubim and dreadful Seraphim, as the [Mother of] God.

[f. 95vA] *Troparion:*

As the only one who didst give birth unto the divine Life and Him Who grants salvation unto all: make me who am despairing worthy of salvation, and cut off the uprising of my passions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Grant thy help, and deliver me from calamities and sorrows, freeing me from temptations and from my transgressions, O thou who didst give birth unto deliverance for all.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

In thee, O Virgin, do we boast, and through thee are we delivered from fierce evils; hoping in thee, we who sing of thee fear not the invasion of wicked barbarians.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^57]

O Virgin All-Immaculate, Mother of Christ God, a sword pierced thy most pure soul when thou didst behold thy Son and God willingly crucified: cease not, O Most Blessed One, to implore Him to grant the forgiveness of our transgressions.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The godly-minded served not creation rather than the Creator, but having manfully trampled down the threat of fire, they rejoiced, singing: Exceedingly praised Lord and God of the fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

Crushed like an earthen vessel by passions, all my strength is gone, and lo, I draw near unto hades, completely ruined: anticipate me, O Mother of God, and snatch me from the griefs that hold me fast, by the hand of thy mercy.

[f. 96rA] *Troparion:*

My inward parts are torn by manifold evils, rather than living, I am torn apart by the multitude of my transgressions and sicknesses: but deliver me from both, O Most Pure One, who didst give birth unto Life for us.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Vouchsafe unto me, as thou art Good, to open the saving gate of the King, Christ the All-Compassionate, O Most Pure Virgin: that by thy prayers, O Blessed One, I may be delivered from soul-destroying sicknesses and assaults.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The sharp pains of my wounds from my immeasurable transgressions lead unto death of soul and body: but snatch me away, O Sovereign Lady, as thou art mighty, from all griefs and diseases.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The Childbirth of the Theotokos saved the pious youths in the furnace: then figured, but now realized, it moves the whole universe to sing unto Thee: Sing unto the Lord, all ye works, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Having acquired the thorn-bearing furnace of passions, I am pierced by their sting. Therefore do I despair, oppressed by the bonds of temptations, O Most Pure Mother of Christ God. Delivering me from them, grant forgiveness of all transgressions by thy prayers.

[f. 96vA] *Troparion:*

Being the candlestick of the Thrice-Radiant Light, by the beam of thy tenderheartedness take away the gloom of my transgressions, O Mother of God, granting deliverance from pressing sicknesses unto him who in faith sings of and touches thy most pure Childbirth.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The Pre-Eternal God, having ineffably become incarnate, wholly dwelt in thy womb, O Ever-Virgin: He made thee the Protectress of the world and universal Helper. Therefore do I pray thee, that I may be delivered from the fierce torment of passions, and from bonds and transgressions.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

At the hour of my departure, O Virgin, snatch me away from the hand of demons, and condemnation, and defense, and dreadful examination, and the bitter toll-houses, and the cruel prince, and eternal fire, O Mother of God.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy, enlightened in spirit; and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the holy festival of the Mother of God, and crying aloud: Rejoice, O Most Blessed Theotokos, Pure Ever-Virgin!

*Troparion:*

The earthly race has been exalted by thy Childbirth, a salvation unto all through divine union; and the heavenly host rejoices together with the earthly, singing of thee worthily, O Pure One, as being the Mother of our God and the Refuge of the whole world.

[f. 97rA] *Troparion:*

Completely bound by the wounds of these my sins, by immeasurable bruises and sicknesses: I call upon thy help and protection, O Sovereign Lady; grant it unto me, delivering me from all fierce calamities and griefs.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

A hymn of salvation and a weaving of praises does my sick soul now offer unto thee with love, unto thee who didst naturally give birth unto Christ God: graciously accepting these, fulfill all my petitions, O Theotokos, and preserve me by thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Enlighten the eyes of my soul, O Pure Mother of God: lest heavy sinful darkness overtake me, nor the abyss of despair cover me; but do thou thyself save and guide me, O Shameless Protectress of the faithful.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: Troparia, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Christ Emmanuel with the Theotokos amidst heavenly hosts and saints worshipping)*

[f. 97vA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing the Mother of God with Christ Emmanuel within an oval medaillon, with flanking cherubim and floral scrollwork)*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 4)

*On Wednesday at Compline,*[^58]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 4. Heirmos:*

Israel of old traversed the abyss of the Red Sea with unwetted feet; and by Moses' arms outstretched in the likeness of the Cross, he conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Truly and in strength hast thou given birth unto God and the Lord, and art alone truly called Theotokos, our strength, O Pure One. Therefore in faith we sing of thee and worthily glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

The Star out of Jacob shone forth from thee, O Most Pure One, He Who numbers the multitude of the stars as God. Therefore by His beams take away the darkness of my sins.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou art manifestly known unto all as the noetic bridal chamber of the Incarnation of God, O Pure and All-Immaculate One. Therefore we pray thee: deliver me from carnal passions, and from griefs, temptations, and tribulations.

*Both now...*

[f. 98rA] *Theotokion:*

As the Ladder of the Almighty's coming unto us, O Mother of God, whereby God descended unto the earth: raise me up from earthly carnal passions unto heaven, and lead me unto God, O Sovereign Lady.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Not in wisdom, nor in power, nor in riches do we boast, but in Thee, the Father's Hypostatic Wisdom of God: for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Fount of incorruption, consolation of the sorrowful art thou, O Sovereign Lady; therefore pour forth unto me also a fountain of the waters of thy prayers, and quench the furnace of passions.

*Troparion:*

Heal me who am wounded by unseemly thoughts, O Pure One, who didst heal the grievous wound of our nature by giving birth unto the Creator and Lord.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

In the abyss of my many transgressions, tossed by the storm of passions and temptations, direct me unto the exceedingly quiet haven by thy protection, O All-Immaculate One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Even as by the divine rain that descended into thy womb as upon the fleece, bedew my heart enflamed with the fire of passions, by thy prayers.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, the Church stood in her order, worthily crying: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

[f. 98vA] *Troparion:*

By the sweet fragrance flowing from the myrrh of thy Son, O Life of all, pour forth the myrrh of dispassion unto my soul, O Pure One, and consume all the mire of its passion.

*Troparion:*

Sprinkle me, defiled with the mire of sin, with the hyssop of thy prayers, and wash away the filth of my passions, and cleanse me, O Sovereign Lady, and show me forth as a habitation for Christ.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thee, the Book inscribed by the divine Finger and sealed, O Most Pure One, do I implore: with the fingers of thy prayers write for me the remission of transgressions, and deliver me from misfortunes.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O thou summit of all the holy mountains, who becamest a Temple unto God, even as the Prophet said of old: show me forth as an honorable temple unto Christ, O Sovereign Lady, by thy protections.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou, my Lord, hast come into the world as Light, and the Holy Light Who turnest back from the gloom of ignorance those who praise Thee in faith.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee as the dew of Hermon descending upon Zion, O Mother of God, I pray thee: quench the burning heat of my flesh.

*Troparion:*

Being the Giver of Life, O Theotokos, swiftly deliver me from sinful death and manifold passions.

[f. 99rA] *Troparion:*

Thou art the noetic alabaster vessel, O Pure One, of the Myrrh that shone upon earth from heaven: fill me also now with sweet fragrance.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast renewed man who was bowed down unto corruption; lead me also now forth, O Bride of God, from the depth of transgressions and passions.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from demonic blood by the Blood that mercifully flowed from Thy side.

*Troparion:*

Having become a most beautiful and glorious Palace of the King of Glory, O Sovereign Lady, thou hast glorified mankind. Therefore make me also worthy of incorruptible glory.

*Troparion:*

Thou didst stop the corrupt nature from sinning, so as to honor incorruption; dry up now, O Most Pure One, the torrents of my passions and the rivers of carnal wisdom.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Lull to sleep the impassioned movements of my body, and my carnal wantonness subdue unto my mind like a colt, having caused it to drink of the sleep of thy prayers, O Pure One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

I sing of thee as having given birth unto the exceedingly praised God, and I pray thee, O Maiden: deliver me from the dreadful trial and eternal condemnation, O Theotokos, and save me.

[f. 99vA] *Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^59]

Invincible Protectress, Mother of God, and ready intercessor for those that flee unto thee: deliver me from misfortunes, and despise me not, O Defender of all!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Abrahamic children in the Persian furnace, enflamed by love of piety rather than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

Being a fiery and God-receiving Bush, O Pure One, burn up the thorns of the evil thoughts of my soul, enlighten my mind, and dry up the abyss of passions.

*Troparion:*

Thou alone from the ages didst find majesty and divine glory upon the earth, having appeared as a second heaven: do thou then also bring to nought my entirely warlike enemies, the demons.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O divine Lily of unfading mercy, pour forth abundantly unto me the riches of thy compassions, and washing away the filth of my transgressions, quench the carnal burning.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having previously lived prodigally, I ever squandered the spiritual riches given unto me by God with carnal pleasures [f. 100rA] but as the prodigal, justify me through thy prayers, O Virgin.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Stretching forth his hands, Daniel shut the yawning mouths of the lions in the pit; and the youths, lovers of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:*

Tear asunder the cord of my transgressions, and calm the bodily impulses, cut off evil devices, and swiftly cleanse thy servant from secret thoughts, O Theotokos, Protectress and defense of all the faithful.

*Troparion:*

Mountain of God made manifest, unquarried and fertile, O Most Pure One, clean and overshadowed: cover me with the shadow of thy prayers, and deliver me from the snares of hunters, and preserve me from demonic arrows and defiled thoughts.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Grant me, O Sovereign Lady, to receive the divine fear and a spirit of contrition in my heart, and to bring forth a virtuous life; show me forth as terrible unto the evil demons, and make me a companion of the angelic choirs of divine glory, O Most Pure One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Open unto me swiftly the gates of life, the portal of my hope, and guide me, O Most Pure One, unto [eternal] life [f. 100vA] and show me, thy servant, to be an heir of the heavenly Kingdom and a partaker of the divine glory of the saints.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Eve through the disease of disobedience brought in the curse, but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of thy bearing in the womb didst make blessing bud forth unto the world: therefore we all magnify thee.

*Troparion:*

Look down upon thy servant by thy prayers, O Pure One, and swiftly anticipate, and deliver me from the invisible enemies that afflict and mistreat me: and save me from calamities, griefs, and manifold tribulations.

*Troparion:*

Heal me, O Virgin, who am wholly wounded with sinful wounds and condemned, and deliver me from evil thoughts: thou who didst give birth unto the Word without beginning, Who is Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

For the sake of Adam who fell of old into death, thy Son, Who rules over life and death, is numbered among the dead, O Most Good Lady. Therefore raise me up from passions and falling by thy supplications.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Save those that flee unto thee, O Theotokos, from calamities and falling, from sin and storm, and from the surge of bodily passions, and from the urgent sorrows of life, and from malice and wickedness.

*Then, It is truly meet: and the usual Dismissal.*

[f. 101rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece with foliate arabesques and floral scrollwork)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 4)

*On Thursday at Compline,*[^60]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 4. Heirmos:*

I will open my mouth, and it shall be filled with the Spirit, and I will utter a word unto the Queen and Mother: and I will appear radiantly keeping festival, and rejoicing I will sing of her wonders.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thou alone art Protection, Refuge, and Defender unto thy servants, O Pure Mother of God; therefore do I fall down before thee and cry: Save me, wretched as I am, as thou art merciful.

*Troparion:*

Defiled deeds wound my conscience, as I bear the conviction of them before my face; make haste, O Sovereign Lady, and be a help unto me, and before the end deliver and save me.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Cleanse my defiled heart, O Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto the Most Pure Word, and who art holier than all the Heavenly Hosts, O only All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

[f. 101vA] *Theotokion:*

I have placed the hope of my salvation upon thee, and unto thee, the Tenderhearted One, have I fled in faith: despise me not, O Hope of the hopeless, nor show me forth as a rejoicing unto demons.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish thy singers, O Theotokos, thou living and plentiful fount of incorruption, who have spiritually formed a choir; and in thy divine glory, make them worthy of crowns of glory.

*Troparion:*

Grant the dew of thy tenderheartedness unto me who melt with sinful heat, O All-Immaculate and Pure One, assuaging my grief and granting divine joy.

*Troparion:*

Dispel the darkness of my mind, O Theotokos, by the light of faith in thee, as thou art good, and I pray thee, establish me in the ways of repentance, as thou art compassionate and full of loving-kindness: that being saved, I may call thee blessed.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

By the sprinkling of thy mercy, O Virgin Full of the Grace of God, quench the embers of my passions, and rekindle the extinguished lamp of my heart, O Golden Lampstand, All-Immaculate One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

A wave of sin and a storm of unseemly thoughts drown me; but have tenderhearted compassion, O All-Immaculate One, and stretch forth the hand of help unto me as thou art merciful: that being saved, I may call thee blessed.

[f. 102rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Perceiving the unsearchable divine counsel of the Incarnation of Thee, the Most High, from the Virgin, the Prophet Habakkuk cried aloud in wonder: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

O All-Marvelous Mother of God, cause the dawn of repentance to shine forth upon me, and dispel the gloom of my wretched soul, and drive away, O Virgin, the evil thoughts of my heart.

*Troparion:*

Thee, the cleansing-seat of all mankind, do I implore in faith, O Blessed One, and ask: Make Thy Son a merciful Judge unto me, that I may glorify thee with praises.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Heal as a physician, O only Pure One, my humble and leprous heart, [stricken] by unclean assaults of passions, and snatch me from the demonic hand.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

With God-inspired voice of old, the divine Habakkuk called thee the Pure and Shadowed Mountain, most clearly proclaiming Him Who came from Teman and was incarnated of thee, O Sovereign Lady.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

All things were amazed at thy divine glory: for thou, O Virgin who knewest not wedlock, didst hold in thy womb the God Who is over all, and didst give birth unto the timeless Son, granting peace unto all who sing of thee.

[f. 102vA] *Troparion:*

Slain by passions and thoughts, O All-Immaculate One, I flee unto thy compassions, and run unto thy warm protection and help, O Sovereign Lady: who alone didst give birth unto Life, quicken my heart.

*Troparion:*

Consumed by the weapon of sin, heal me by thy efficacious medicine, O Mother of God who didst give birth unto the Savior, Who for my sake was wounded with the spear, O Most Pure One, and Who wounded the heart of the serpent.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Heal the crushing of my mind, O All-Immaculate One, and cure the passions of my soul, destroying the nocturnal darkness: that in praising I may ever sing of thee, O Blessed Theotokos, All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Beholding hanging upon the Tree the Cluster of the Vine which the Virgin brought forth, she cried aloud: O Child, Thou droppest sweet wine, taking away intoxication from the enemies who crucified Thee in all things longsuffering.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Celebrating this divine and all-honorable festival of the Mother of God, come, ye godly-minded, let us clap our hands, glorifying in faith God Who was born of her.

*Troparion:*

O only help of all, help us who are in calamities, and stretch forth thy hand, and guide us unto the haven of salvation, O only One Full of God's Grace.

[f. 103rA] *Troparion:*

Snatch me away, O Most Pure One, in the dreadful hour, from examination, and from the evil deception of demons, and from judgment, and fire, and darkness, and torment.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I sing hymns unto thee, O All-Hymned One, I glorify thine honorable majesty; do thou free me from unclean passions, and make me worthy of eternal glory.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

We are bound to sing of thee, yet truly according to thy worth we cannot. Therefore we sing of thee in silence, honoring the ineffable Mystery wrought in thee, O Virgin.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^61]

When Thy Most Pure Mother beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Word of God, weeping maternally she spoke: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How dost Thou, the Life of all, taste death, wishing to quicken the dead as Thou art compassionate?

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The godly-minded served not creation rather than the Creator, but having manfully trampled down the threat of fire, they rejoiced, singing: Exceedingly praised Lord and God of the fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Unchangeable One, O only One Full of God's Grace, I pray thee, O Pure One: by His right hand change my [f. 103vA] mind unto better things, which has been grievously altered by demonic temptations.

*Troparion:*

O Virgin Maiden who didst give birth unto Christ the King: have compassion and save me, who am bowed down with passions; establish me in faith, and guide me unto the path of salvation, O Salvation of the faithful.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Be an intercessor for me, O All-Immaculate One, unto Him Who was born of thee, and grant me the remission of grievous debts, and the divine entrance into the Kingdom of God, and the reception of delight, and communion in the Light.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O All-Immaculate Mary, Virgin unwedded and pure, boundless in goodness, who didst give birth unto God in the flesh: implore Him to deliver us from every sorrow and sin.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The Childbirth of the Theotokos saved the pious youths in the furnace: then figured, but now realized, it moves the whole universe to sing unto Thee: Sing unto the Lord, all ye works, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Having spent my life prodigally and committed every defilement, I tremble before the Judgment Seat, I tremble before the examination, I tremble before the answer of my condemnation: have mercy upon my wretched soul, O Pure One, and before my death, grant me enlightenment.

[f. 104rA] *Troparion:*

I have placed all the hope of my salvation upon thee, O Mother of God unwedded, and I ever call upon thee for help: save me from sorrows and hostile assaults, loose the bonds of my sins, and snatch me from eternal darkness.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Shown forth as higher than the angels, having ineffably incarnated God: implore Him, O All-Immaculate Sovereign Lady, that I may be above carnal assaults, and be delivered from the coming judgment and eternal torment, O Virgin.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Fill me with divine waters, O Maiden, who didst bear the Fountain in thy womb, and deliver me from the corruption of my sins, and guide me unto the life of salvation, O Pure One; drive away despondency from my wretched soul, O Virgin, and deliver me from demons.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy, enlightened in spirit; and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the holy festival of the Mother of God, and crying aloud: Rejoice, O Most Blessed Theotokos, Pure Ever-Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Destroy the pasture of sin of my defiled soul and body, O Full of the Grace of God, with thy Son's prayer, O All-Immaculate and Most Pure Sovereign Lady, and grant saving healing and pure godly fear.

[f. 104vA] *Troparion:*

Thou art my enlightenment, thou art my deliverance and rejoicing, thou art my champion, thou art my glory, praise, hope, and salvation, O All-Immaculate One; faithfully I bow down before thee and cry unto thee: Save me, thy wretched servant, and snatch me from the gates of hades.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Save me, O Pure One, who didst give birth unto the All-Compassionate Savior, and have compassion on thy servant, and guide me unto the paths of repentance; drive away the cunning scandal from our midst, and deliver me from his hunting, and snatch me from eternal fire, O All-Immaculate One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Clothed in flesh from thee, the Word, O All-Immaculate One, lived in the world as the Compassionate One, remaining undiminished; He Who was formerly bodiless cast down by divine power him who of old tormented all.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: The Virgin Mary clothed with the sun, standing upon the crescent moon, crowned by two angels, with cherubim above)*

[f. 105rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing Christ Emmanuel in a medallion flanked by cherubim and floral arabesques)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 4)

*On Friday at Compline,*[^62]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 4. Heirmos:*

Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, drown, I pray Thee, the mighty warriors of passion in the depth of dispassion, a participant of my soul: that unto Thee as with a timbrel, in the mortification of the flesh, I may sing a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

From the mire of passions, from the storm of thoughts, from the arrows of the evil one, and from every assault of the adversary: save my wretched soul, O Most Pure Birthgiver of God, and snatch me from eternal fire.

*Troparion:*

Raise me up, O Pure One, from the depth of transgressions, and guide me unto the light of the divine commandments of the Savior, Christ and God; cause the saving dawn of repentance to shine forth upon me, and grant eternal life.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I have fallen into the evil mire of my sins, I have been sorely choked by wicked deeds, and there is no [f. 105vA] standing: O Sovereign Lady, Virgin Mother, by thy power raise me up, and deliver me from fire and torment.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast abolished death and corruption, O All-Hymned Theotokos, having given birth unto the Fount of incorruption, Christ, Who adorned human nature with immortality and grace, O All-Immaculate One.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Not in wisdom, nor in power, nor in riches do we boast, but in Thee, the Father's Hypostatic Wisdom of God: for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Take away the darkness that has hung over my soul, O Pure Virgin, and tear asunder the bonds of sins, and save me by the intercessory prayers of thy protection.

*Troparion:*

Establish my heart, which is shaken by the assaults of passions, in the fear of God, through the most honorable loving-kindness of thy compassions, O Virgin All-Immaculate.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Despairing of all help, I have fled unto thee, O All-Immaculate One, a sovereign Defender and Protection: cast me not away from thy divine protection.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Adorned with golden robes as a Queen, thou standest now at the right hand of the King and God, O Sovereign Lady, praying for thy servants.

[f. 106rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Sitting in glory upon the Throne of the Godhead, Jesus, the Most Divine, came upon a light cloud, from the Most Pure Virgin, and saved those that cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ!

*Troparion:*

Held fast by despondency, wholly darkened by the gloom of passions, and enslaved to sin: free me, O Maiden Blessed of God, and reconcile me unto thy Son and our God.

*Troparion:*

Quicken me who am slain by the malice of the deceiver, O All-Immaculate One, as thou didst give birth unto Life, for unto thee have I fled; and raise me who am buried in the depth of falls, as thou art merciful, O All-Immaculate One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I have sunk down into the tomb of my measureless pleasures, and am held fast by despondency and sloth: but O Good One who didst give birth unto the resurrection of all, quicken me and save me.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The Prophet of old named thee the sacred, divine Mountain, O Pure Virgin, wholly overshadowed by virtues: from which the saving Word appeared, unto the recreation and enlightenment of our souls.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

The ungodly shall not behold Thy glory, O Christ; but waking early at dawn out of the night, we sing of Thee, O Only-Begotten, the Radiance of the Father's glory of the Godhead, O Merciful One.

[f. 106vA] *Troparion:*

May my humble soul rejoice, O Sovereign Lady, may it fear thy Son and perform His commandments from the heart; guide me by thy prayers, O All-Immaculate and Most Pure One.

*Troparion:*

With all my heart, O Good Sovereign Lady, confessing the Redeemer, bless my impassioned soul, driving away the ignorance of my heart, O All-Immaculate One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Preserve my soul as the apple of the eye under the shelter of thy wings, O Good and Most Pure One, and deliver me from the torment and torture of the wicked spirits.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Show thyself as delivering thy servant from the assaults of passions and from the wicked demons, O Most Pure One, for he has acquired thee as a mighty protection and an invincible Defender.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Prefiguring the three-day Burial, the Prophet Jonah, praying in the whale, cried aloud: Deliver me from corruption, O Jesus, King of hosts!

*Troparion:*

Cast down him who ever wars against me, O Sovereign Lady, and dispel the wicked cloud of my thoughts, O Mother of God, holy Portal of the full Sun.

*Troparion:*

Cause the dawn of repentance to shine upon me, O Sovereign Lady, and dispel the clouds of my evil thoughts, which are drawn [f. 107rA] and corruption.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Assuage the fierce tempest of my passions, and consume the storm of evil thoughts, O firm Haven of all and Protection, All-Immaculate One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The transgressions of my soul have multiplied more than the sand of the sea, and like a heavy burden they weigh me down: but O Virgin, having had compassion before the end, save me.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^63]

Warm protection in calamities, and our helper, and reconciliation unto God: through whom we were delivered from corruption, the Theotokos do we, the faithful, call blessed.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Abrahamic children of old in Babylon trampled down the furnace flame, crying aloud with praises: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

Thee, O Pure Theotokos, do I faithfully invoke: preserve my humble heart, and deliver me from the flame of torment and eternal darkness.

*Troparion:*

Tear up the handwriting of my transgressions, O Pure Virgin, with the Spear of thy Son, and deliver me, I pray thee, from every possession at the hour of my end.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Quench, O Pure Virgin, with the streams of my tears and the shower of thy compassions, the furnace which [f. 107vA] the multitude of my sins prepares for me through demonic action.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Deliver us from the misfortunes that come upon us, O only Mother of the Word, Ever-Virgin, and from every torment, who truly and understandingly sing of thee unto all ages.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

O Almighty Redeemer of all, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst bedew those who practiced piety, and didst teach them to sing: All ye works, bless the Lord, the Lord!

*Troparion:*

Heal my ailing soul, enlighten my darkened mind, and snatch me from the fire and from the unutterable torments of punishment, O Virgin Theotokos, Bride of God.

*Troparion:*

Having bowed down with irrational passions, I have descended into the depth of perdition, and have wrought the burning of the flame there: deliver me from them, O Virgin Theotokos, thy servant.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Dry up the abyss of my sins, having given birth unto the Abyss of loving-kindness, the Redeemer and Lord, and loose the bonds of my sins before the end, O thou who didst give birth unto the Savior of all.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having become incarnate from thy womb, the Lord rules over creation, and shows thee forth, O Virgin, as the Lady of all things visible and invisible: glorifying thee as His Immaculate Mother.

[f. 108rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Eve through the disease of disobedience brought in the curse, but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of thy bearing in the womb didst make blessing bud forth unto the world: therefore we all magnify thee.

*Troparion:*

Show forth my heart, which has been barren of divine virtues, to be fruitful, O Most Pure Virgin Theotokos: thou who wast born of a barren woman by the will of Him Who nourishes all, that I may sing of thee, the All-Hymned One.

*Troparion:*

Illuminate me with thy light, O All-Immaculate One, who didst give birth unto the Unapproachable Light, dispel the clouds of my passions, snatch me from the dark gloom, and make me worthy of the divine light: that I may sing of thee, O Virgin Mother.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

By the sprinkling of thy compassions, O Pure One, wash away the foulness of my heart, and vouchsafe me ever to pour forth streams of tears, subduing the torrents of passions and delivering me from torment.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth unto the Blessed God, O Blessed Theotokos: swiftly free me now, O Sovereign Lady, from evil carnal love, and subject me, who am perishing in sloth, unto the divine will.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 108vA] *(Woodcut: Full-page engraving of the Annunciation of the Most Holy Theotokos. Above, God the Father in glory with the Holy Spirit as a dove, inscribed: "The King shall desire thy beauty" [Ps. 44:12]. Archangel Gabriel bearing the scroll "Annunciation" blesses the Mother of God ["Most Holy Theotokos"], before whom lies an open book. At the top, the inscription reads: "Day unto day utters speech" [Ps. 18:3 LXX])*

The river of the Holy Spirit, O Virgin, shall be within thee,

that as holy earth she may bring forth the Flower, Christ.

Pray, O Virgin, that my soul also may be fruitful,

and from this earth may cultivate it unto the Land of the Living.

---

# TONE 5

[f. 109rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing the Mother of God and Christ Child within a floral and cherubic medallion)*

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Saturday at Compline,*[^64]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm, and saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

We are all at a loss to offer a song unto thee according to thy worth, O Sovereign Lady: for thy glory is above all; yet, O Bride of God, reject not the supplication offered unto thee with fear and love.

*Troparion:*

Having drawn near unto the water of thine inexhaustible Fountain, O Virgin Theotokos, we flee, crying aloud: O only Giver of joy, Most Pure One, ask peace for thy churches.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

God, Who was well pleased to take flesh from thee, O Pure One, appointed thee a haven unto those in calamities [f. 109vA] therefore falling down before thee, we cry aloud: Grant thy help unto thy servants.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

May thy prayer, O Most Pure Virgin, be relief unto thy servants, driving away passions, loosing sins, and cleansing every disease, O Birthgiver of God.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Thou art the hope of earthborn mortals, and our help, joy, protection, and refuge, O Sovereign Lady, Mother of Life; therefore we pray thee: send down thy help unto all who sing of thee, O Most Pure One.

*Troparion:*

Being sick and held fast by grievous misfortunes, O Compassionate God of all, Who seest all: we bring this thy Most Pure Mother unto Thee in supplication, O Savior; loose the bonds of our transgressions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O thou who alone didst give birth unto the Upholder, Creator, and Lord of all, while remaining a virgin: grant salvation from on high unto thy servants, who truly glorify thee as the Mother of God, O Bride of God.

*Both now...*

[f. 110rA] *Theotokion:*

Fill me with life-giving waters, O Sovereign Lady, who didst pour forth the divine Water unto the world; and calm the grievous torrents of my iniquities and the waves of my heart, by thy divine tranquility.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Perceiving Thy divine dispensation prophetically, O Christ, Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

As being higher than all creatures, God gave thee unto those on earth, as the cause of mediation unto Him, O All-Hymned Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be the divine Jacob, O Virgin Mother, we who honor thee diligently pray thee: shut not the doors of thy mercy unto thy servants, O Theotokos.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Knowing Thee to be the truly all-adorned garment of God, O unwedded Mother, we who honor Thee ask: clothe us in the garment of forgiveness.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

At thy Childbirth, O Most Pure One, the whole world was filled with joy: from whence the great Gabriel cried aloud unto thee from on high: Rejoice, O Virgin Mother Mary!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment: I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and cry unto Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

[f. 110vA] *Troparion:*

Truly we are chastened, yet not according to the full measure of the debt of our transgressions; but O Virgin, Most Pure Mother, turn away all the wrath of thy Son from us.

*Troparion:*

Having given birth without knowing wedlock unto the Only God, Who brought forth light out of darkness, O Pure One: diligently pray Him to send down divine light upon thy servants.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thy prayers have censed us, O Pure One, and as the divine Bride from Lebanon foretold by Solomon: shed fragrance upon thy servants, O Mother of the Creator.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having given birth seedlessly unto Christ, our Righteousness and Deliverance: thou didst make the nature of our forefather free from the curse, O Theotokos.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of my passions, O Master Christ, which is troubled by a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.

*Troparion:*

O Sovereign Lady Theotokos, who didst give birth unto the Creator: ask forgiveness for thy servants, and raise us up mighty to sing of thee.

*Troparion:*

Be a help unto us, thy servants, O Pure Sovereign Lady, who faithfully pray unto thee, as by thy prayers; and raise us up mighty to sing of thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having power according to thy dignity, O Pure Sovereign Lady: look down with thy merciful eye, and raise us up, thy servants, from corruption.

*Both now...*

[f. 111rA] *Theotokion:*

...unceasingly pouring forth a stream of compassions unto those that ask, O Good One: rain down also upon me the light of the commandments of thy Son, O All-Immaculate One.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^65]

O Pure Virgin, have mercy upon us who flee unto thy protection and unto thee fervently, and who ask thy warm mediation: for thou canst save all, as being the good Mother of the Most High God, ever encompassing us with thy maternal prayers, O Virgin Full of God's Grace.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord of the fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame, and bedewed the children who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

O Inscrutable Mercy of God, Christ: have compassion upon Thy servants for the sake of her who gave birth unto Thee, who unceasingly sing: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

We implore Thy grace, O Lord: heal our wound for the sake of her who gave birth unto Thee, of those who sing with fear: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Look down with thy merciful eye, O Mother of God, and deliver thy servants from every affliction, who sing with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Both now...*

[f. 111vA] *Theotokion:*

...Working evil, O Sovereign Lady, we fell away from thee; but we immediately found thy help, O Most Pure One, when we cried: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who was begotten of the Father before the ages, the Son and God, and in the latter times incarnate of the Virgin Mother: ye priests, sing praises; ye people, exalt Him above all unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

O our Sovereign Lady, Giver of good things: grant unto thy servants healing in passions, that we may unceasingly sing of thee, O Maiden, and exalt thee unto the ages.

*Troparion:*

Having ineffably given birth unto the Redeemer, O Pure One, and inexplicably nursed Him, remaining a Virgin: pray Him on behalf of those who sing of thee and glorify thee unto all ages.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Unto thee, the radiant Lampstand of the Redeemer, having woven a beauteous choir, we sing: All ye works of the Lord, sing unceasingly of the Virgin Mother, and exalt her above all unto the ages!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O Pure Ewe-Lamb, Virgin Mother, Maiden: make me pure from bodily passions, that I may be delivered from the snares of the deceitful one, hymning thee, O Full of God's Grace.

[f. 112rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

The song is indeed completed, but hope in Thee, O Master Christ, is unfailing toward Thee, the Creator, as is also Thy grace; yet grant firm strength on both sides unto Thy servants, through the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee.

*Troparion:*

Thou art strength unto the ailing, and near unto those who suffer, O Pure One, as the true Mother of Life. Therefore, fleeing unto thee, we have found relief from all sorrowful things, O Sovereign Lady, and have been saved by thy protection.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Beholding thy divine form iconographically, O Theotokos, we see thee in it as though visibly present, utterly abhorring the folly of heretics upon earth; falling down before which, we receive healing.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

We sinners have known thee, O Pure One, as an abyss of healings and a deep sea of graces. Therefore we pray thee: deliver from dire misfortunes those who hasten unto thy protection, O only Most Pure One.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: Troparion, and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 112vA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing the Mother of God Platytera in a central medallion flanked by cherubim and floral arabesques)*

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Sunday at Compline,*[^66]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm, and saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

A truly fearful wonder is accomplished in thee, O Virgin: for Him Whom nothing can contain, thou didst hold contained within thy womb, and didst inexplicably give birth unto Him, remaining ever a Virgin.

*Troparion:*

Rain down upon me the forgiveness of transgressions, O Sovereign Lady, who didst ineffably contain the Heavenly Rain that came unto thee, and didst remain a Virgin after incomprehensible Childbirth.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O only Blessed and Full of God's Grace, who didst give birth unto ineffable joy for mortals [f. 113rA] take away the sorrows of my soul, I pray thee, and make my heart glad.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

All desire, and the sweetness of life, shone forth from thee because of exceedingly abundant grace, O All-Immaculate Virgin: pray Him that he who now unceasingly glorifies thee may be saved.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

The things prescribed by the shadow of the law have passed away: for thou didst give birth unto Christ the Lawgiver, O Most Pure One, Who ordained the grace of purification and enlightenment for us, and took us out from the curse, O All-Immaculate Virgin.

*Troparion:*

God was born of thee, O Pure One, becoming incarnate, and was seen in the flesh, Who was formerly invisible: diligently pray Him, therefore, O Maiden, to deliver me from visible and invisible enemies, as I radiantly glorify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

A fierce wave of passions tosses me about, the deep of evil spirits holds me fast, sinful shame covers my breast, O Mother of God: do thou establish me, who radiantly sing of thee.

*Both now...*

[f. 113vA] *Theotokion:*

Having the divine Gabriel as Leader of the ranks, reverently, with joyful faith, we offer praise in unison, faithfully unto the Virgin Mother who knew not wedlock, the Theotokos, by whom we are delivered from misfortunes, sorrows, and diseases.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Discerning Thy divine dispensation, farsighted Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

Thou who within didst weave from thy virginal blood a garment of incorruption for Him Who clothes heaven with clouds, O Virgin: clothe me with it, who was formerly stripped naked by deception.

*Troparion:*

Thy womb, O Virgin, ineffably became the holy Palace of the King and God: dwelling in which, He made us Churches.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Have compassion, as thou art compassionate, upon my most wretched soul, O All-Immaculate Birthgiver of God, which is grievously blackened and defiled by passions and sin.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst bud forth as a Scepter of incorruption from the root of Jesse, O Pure One, and without a man, O Pure One, didst give birth unto the Child, the God of Adam and David, together both Creator and Lord.

[f. 114rA] *Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment: I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and cry unto Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

*Troparion:*

Remaining a Virgin of incorruption after Childbirth, thou bringest all corrupted mortals unto life, and clothest them with incorruption, O All-Immaculate One.

*Troparion:*

Anew hast thou given birth as young, O Most Pure One, unto Him Who timelessly shone forth from the Father before the ages: pray Him for peace, O Bride of God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

May I find thee as a Helper, O Virgin, who snatchest me away in the hour of receiving judgment, when I shall stand before the Judgment Seat of Him Who was born of thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Behold, my companion is good and spotless, from Lebanon, O Virgin Bride of God: through the Holy Spirit the Incarnation of the Son of God was made known in thee.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of my passions, O Master Christ, which is troubled by a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.

*Troparion:*

Without knowing man thou didst give birth, O Theotokos, unto Emmanuel, Who had compassion upon our lowliness. Therefore we ever dutifully glorify thee.

[f. 114vA] *Troparion:*

Beyond timeless mind, beyond the word of the Creator, O Most Pure One, thou didst give birth unto Him Who delivers from all corruption those who sing of thee as Theotokos.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Good One and Creator, O Full of Grace, Most Pure Sovereign Lady named of God: sweeten my afflicted soul.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

A new and ancient praise we offer unto thee, O All-Immaculate One: for we have nothing better for praise than to chant unto thee with Gabriel: Rejoice!

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^67]

Before the Judgment I now weep, pondering my wicked and grievous deeds, and the depth of transgressions that has possessed me from my youth, weighing down my mind; but by thy protection, O Pure One, grant me forgiveness, and vouchsafe me to obtain salvation.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord of the fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame, and bedewed the children who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Most Pure One, from evil despondency, and from the darkening of passions, and from ancient condemnation, that with faith I may glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

Put my passions to death, O thou who didst give birth unto Life; raise me up, O Bride of God, who lie in the tomb of insensibility [f. 115rA] that with love I may glorify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Arrayed in flesh, the Bodiless God, O All-Immaculate One, thou didst bring forth, Who delivers us that sing with fear: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having found thy pure prayer as a freely given healing, O Virgin, we ask for help, the good transformation of the soul, and health of body.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven an all-beauteous choir, sang: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

O Portal of Light, open unto me the light-bearing gates of repentance, showing me the straight path of righteousness, leading up into the entrances of the divine will.

*Troparion:*

Anew upon earth, O Virgin Mother Maiden, hast thou given birth unto the Son, co-unoriginate with the Father, Who through ineffable compassion was likened unto us that were corrupted by sins.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

All-chosen, all-beauteous, whom God loved, whom He chose, hast thou appeared, O Virgin, ever All-Glorified. Therefore we sing of thee, O Pure One, unto the ages.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

All-pure, and full of divine glory, my companion, said the Spirit of God: proclaiming thee [f. 115vA] O Pure One, whom we hymn in songs, chanting: It is meet for thee to rejoice, who didst give birth unto God and Man!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Cast not away now the sighs of those who hasten unto thee, O All-Immaculate One, but look upon us who are in sorrows: turn mourning into joy, and compunction into gladness, for those who sing of thee in hymns.

*Troparion:*

Thou art an immovable Foundation, O Pure One, having given birth unto the Savior, Who founded the earth upon the waters by divine commands: pray Him to establish upon Him us who radiantly glorify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thy Childbirth is beyond mind, O Theotokos, for conception without a man was in thee, and thy Childbirth was virginal: for He Who was born is God: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed who gavest birth unto Him.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Fearful art Thou, O Lord, and who shall then stand before Thy threat, O Christ, Only King, when Thou dost execute judgment? Therefore spare and save me, O Savior, by the acceptable supplications of her who gave birth unto Thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 116rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece showing a cherub with wings amidst floral arabesques)*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Monday at Compline,*[^68]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm, and saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Grant me a contrite spirit, O Good One, humility of heart, purity of mind, amendment of life, remission of transgressions, and fountains of tears, O All-Immaculate One.

*Troparion:*

Behold my affliction and compunction, and heal the ancient scars of transgressions, and grant me time for repentance, and the confession of sins.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

I weep for myself before my departure, pondering the abyss of my evils, O All-Immaculate One. Therefore I pray thee: entreat thy Son to deliver me from torments.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having time for repentance, depart from every evil, O my soul, and unto thy Creator cry aloud with tears: O my God, save me by the supplications of her who gave birth unto Thee!

[f. 116vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

The princes of my passions have encompassed me, O Most Pure One, and me, who was created according to the image and likeness of God, they have filled with shame; but deliver me from their harm, who sing of thee in compunction.

*Troparion:*

Subtly the enemy strives to ensnare me, desiring to show me forth as fuel for eternal fire, O Most Pure One; but destroy his cunning wiles and counsels, that with joy I may glorify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

In the lowest pit of many transgressions the enemies of the righteous have deceitfully cast me; being now helpless and wholly wounded, I invoke thy prayer, O All-Immaculate Sovereign Lady: save me!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

In sloth have I, the wretched one, spent my life; now I have drawn near unto the gates of death, and tremble at the snares of the enemy, crying aloud unto thee: deliver me from these unaccustomed trials, that being saved, I may glorify thee.

[f. 117rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Discerning Thy divine self-emptying, farsighted Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

I have been wounded by the arrows of sin, and now bear wounds all over my body; therefore I cry unto thee, O Most Pure One: by the swiftness of thy prayers, heal the wounds of my soul.

*Troparion:*

Have mercy upon thy servants, O Pure One: for we have acquired thee as Intercessor with God, and we pray to be delivered from every necessity and from eternal torment.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou art my hope, and shield, and confirmation, O Good One, and deliverance from grievous ills, and enlightenment of my soul, and praise, and wall, and stronghold.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Prodigally have I squandered the wealth of good works which Christ as the Good One bestowed upon me; despise me not, therefore, O Most Pure Maiden, who am perishing of hunger.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment: I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and cry unto Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

[f. 117vA] *Troparion:*

...Behold, O Sovereign Lady, the infirmity of my humble soul, and the weakness of my flesh, and the captivity of my mind, and save me who am beyond hope.

*Troparion:*

Ever pray God, Whom thou didst bear, to save all of us who confess thee to be Theotokos and glorify thy Childbirth, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Who does not call thee blessed, O All-Immaculate One? For thou didst ineffably give birth unto the Creator of the whole world, the Savior Christ and Only Master.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Send down upon me streams of tears, O Most Pure One, with which having washed away the foulness and mire of my sins, I may sing of the abundance of thy grace.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of my passions, O Master Christ, which is troubled by a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.

*Troparion:*

Into the depth of transgressions and iniquities have I been cast; but stretch forth thy hand, O All-Immaculate One, and bring me up from the hell of despair.

*Troparion:*

Thou knowest the multitude of sins and thoughts that trouble me. Therefore make haste and deliver me from them, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

[f. 118rA] *Troparion:*

...Spare me Thy servant, O Master Christ, through the prayers of her who purely gave birth unto Thee, when Thou shalt come to judge the world which Thou didst create.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Into the depth of transgressions has my soul been dragged down, O Pure Sovereign Lady, having become food for demons; therefore save me who am beyond hope.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^69]

Swiftly heal the grievously painful passions of my soul and the diseases of my flesh, stop the wanderings of my mind, O All-Immaculate One, and in the quiet of thought vouchsafe me to offer pure prayers unto the King of all, O Theotokos, and to ask forgiveness of transgressions.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord of the fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame, and bedewed the children who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

When I take into my mind my unseemly deeds and the multitude of transgressions, O All-Immaculate One, I fear and tremble; from them free me by thy prayers, O Virgin.

*Troparion:*

The sleepless worm and the unquenchable fire consume me continually and eat away my soul: may I not be delivered up unto them, O Most Pure Theotokos!

*Glory...*

[f. 118vA] *Troparion:*

...From outer darkness and fearful torment deliver me, thy servant, O All-Immaculate Virgin, who cry aloud unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

My flesh indeed have I defiled with shameful passions, and my mind have I darkened with foul thoughts; but have compassion, O Pure One, and save me, thine unprofitable servant.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven an all-beauteous choir, sang: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Unto thee, O Theotokos, I bring the infirmity of my humble soul, and the disease of my heart, and the deception of my mind I make known, asking thy help, O Maiden.

*Troparion:*

Be merciful unto Thy servants, O Word, through the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee, and save those who sing: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O Mother who didst give birth unto the Creator, have mercy upon all who sing with faith: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Deliver me from outer darkness and from the unsleeping worm, O Good Maiden, by thy prayers: for thou didst give birth unto the Creator of the world, Who does whatsoever He wills.

[f. 119rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Diligently pray God, Whom thou didst ineffably bear, O Pure Sovereign Lady, to deliver us from misfortunes and sorrows and from the coming fearful Judgment, and vouchsafe us the radiance of His saints.

*Troparion:*

More than all have I sinned, having transgressed Thy life-creating commandments, O Christ, and I have become like the unreasoning beasts; but by the prayers of Thy Mother, O Word, pluck me not out from this life unrepentant.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

As having given birth unto the Abyss of mercy, God the Word: have mercy, O Good One, upon the souls of all who flee beneath thy protection; for we all have acquired thee as an unashamed Intercessor with God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Ask for us cleansing and forgiveness of transgressions, and relief from all necessities, and amendment of life that is now pure and radiant: that we may glorify thine abundant grace, O Mother of God.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 119vA] *(Woodcut: Deesis headpiece showing Christ in the center flanked by the Mother of God on the right and John the Forerunner on the left)*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Tuesday at Compline,*[^70]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm, and saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Accept, O Theotokos, the prayers faithfully offered unto thee from the depth of the heart of thy servants, and bringing them unto thy Son, grant forgiveness of transgressions.

*Troparion:*

As truly being a Benefactress who enriches earthborn mortals and the Portal of salvation leading into life: cease not ever to intercede for all, O Virgin Mother of God, who pray unto thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Be inclined now by the supplications of those who sing of thee: as being a mighty help unto salvation, deliver those who honor thee from misfortunes by thy prayers, O Sovereign Lady, and preserve them whole.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Come quickly to our aid and stand before us warmly, O Most Pure Maiden; deliver and free from sorrow those who are compunctionate, who are sorely held by grievous afflictions.

[f. 120rA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Having illuminated the dark cloud of my heart with the divine lightning radiant with thy light, O Mother of God: show me forth as a son of the day, enlightened by virtues, O Guide of light unto our souls.

*Troparion:*

Desiring ever to sing of thee according to thy worth, yet being at a loss, we offer praise from vile lips: accept it with gracious favor, and give ear unto the prayers of those who faithfully pray unto thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Showing forth fruitful the souls that were barren, cleansing by thy prayer those that were frozen as ice: show forth also my soul yielding fruit, faith and a spotless life, O Birthgiver of God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

He Who holds the ends of all the earth in His fist, how is He embraced by maternal arms? And He Who satisfies every living thing with good pleasure, rich in outpouring, is nourished with milk from the breast, becoming poor as He is incarnate!

[f. 120vA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Perceiving in figure Thy divine self-emptying, the prophet Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be the one Mediatress with God, releasing the debts of sins for the guilty, O Most Pure One: with praises and hymns we honor thee.

*Troparion:*

God, having dwelt within thee, O all-immaculate Virgin, showed thee forth as a consecrated church: Whom ever entreat to preserve thy servants unharmed.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Hear the prayer of the poor who hope in thee, O Theotokos, and hasten to deliver us from misfortunes, through thy maternal boldness.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having acquired thee, O Mother of God, as sweet comfort in afflictions, help in adversities, and Protectress in misfortunes: we are delivered from every ill-treatment.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment: I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

*Troparion:*

Having in time given birth in the circumscription of flesh unto Him Who is without beginning, Who in truth is uncircumscribed by nature: entreat Him unceasingly for thy servants.

[f. 121rA] *Troparion:*

Thou drawest near swiftly unto those ever in distress, helping and guiding those who call upon thee in temptation unto salvation, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having shown God, Who is compassionate by nature, to be merciful through thy prayers: grant unto thy servants a peaceful life, and deliverance from transgressions, O All-Immaculate One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O thou who didst give birth unto the Live Coal which Isaiah beheld:[^71] consume the material substance of my sins, O All-Immaculate One, and enlighten me, I pray.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of my passions, O Master Christ, which is troubled by a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.

*Troparion:*

Having raised us up who were slain by the passions of sin, O Sovereign Lady, give us life by thy prayers: as having given birth unto the Destroyer of corruption.

*Troparion:*

Having thee as an unbeguiling hope, an invincible wall against enemies, and a firm help: we fear not the plots directed against us.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O pure haven for earthborn mortals, in sicknesses ever preserving unharmed thy servants who are sorely languishing: preserve them unharmed.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Enlighten my soul, O Bride of God, which has been darkened by a cloud of thoughts that come upon me by the plot of the man-slaying beast.

[f. 121vA] *Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^72]

By the Cross of thy Son, O thou who wast favored by God, all the deception of idols was brought to naught, and the demonic power was trampled down. Therefore we, the faithful, as is our duty, ever sing and bless thee, and truly confessing thee to be the Theotokos, we magnify thee.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord God of our fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame, and bedewed the youths, who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

Accept, O Sovereign Lady, the requests of thy faithful servants, taking us out of temptations, and saving from sorrows those who faithfully sing: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

Having given birth for us unto God, the Creator of good things: entreat Him, O Pure One, by maternal supplications, to show mercy unto those who boast in thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having acquired thee as an invincible Protectress and an unshakable foundation, and having clothed ourselves in thy protection, O Sovereign Lady, we conquer the wiles shot against us.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Look upon the humility of thy servant, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me from eternal fire, who unceasingly sing: Blessed art Thou, O God!

[f. 122rA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven an all-beauteous choir, sang: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Weaving praise for thee with voices and thoughts together, O Virgin, we the faithful cry aloud: Bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Solomon inscribed beforehand this thy beauty, calling thee the Near One unto God,[^73] as being closest unto Him, delivering those in the world by maternal boldness, O Maiden.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having thee after God as the hope of salvation in adversities, we the faithful cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Accept a song from lowly lips, O All-Praised Virgin, who cry aloud unto thee: Rejoice, O Healer of diseased bodies, and salvation of despairing souls!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Unto thee we pour out our prayer from sorrow: have mercy upon us sinners, that we may not be put to shame on the day of temptation, who call upon thee, the all-merciful Theotokos.

[f. 122vA] *Troparion:*

Despise not our earthly kinship, O Pure Sovereign Lady, but be a helper covering, strengthening in griefs, and delivering from all diseases: for in thee have I placed my hope, who art ready to save us.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

O God, hear now the prayer of Thy people, and deliver us from the harm of the enemy: behold, we bring unto Thee as true purification her who gave birth unto Thee, whom with love we call blessed.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Beneath the dominion of thy might, O Sovereign Lady, all we earthborn mortals fervently flee, asking mercy: and having poured out thy merciful compassions, sanctify us all.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Engraving of the Protection of the Most Holy Theotokos. The Mother of God stands upon clouds holding the veil above the hierarchs, clergy, and faithful, flanked by the Cyrillic monograms "MP" and "ѲY")*

[f. 123rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece featuring the Holy Face / Cross with instruments of the Passion and cherubim, with monograms "IC" and "XC")*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Wednesday at Compline,*[^74]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Unto God the Savior, Who guided the people in the sea with dryshod steps, and drowned Pharaoh with all his host: unto Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

O light-bearing Tabernacle of Christ the King: illuminate my mind, which has been darkened by the deceit of the adversary and blinded by the darkness of my transgressions.

*Troparion:*

From deceitful thoughts free my humble soul, O Birthgiver of God, and make it a holy dwelling for God: that as is my duty I may ever glorify thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

When by God's command I am about to depart from temporal life, O Most Pure One: show me forth higher than the demonic hands, granting me Angels as companions.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Why, O my soul, hast thou spent thy whole life in much despondency? Make haste now, therefore, and cry aloud unto the Mother of the Lord: Cleanse and save me, O Birthgiver of God!

[f. 123vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

By the power of Thy Cross, O Christ, establish my understanding, to sing and glorify Thy saving Crucifixion.

*Troparion:*

Out of the pit of passions and torments bring me up, who without doubt sing of thee, O all-blessed Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

Tear apart the sackcloth of my boundless transgressions, and equip me with the joy of virtues, O thou who art full of divine grace.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Grant me a drop of tears, O Pure One, that I may revive the perplexity of my heart, and diligently sing of thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Cast down, O All-Immaculate One, the arrogance of the bodiless enemies, and swiftly free me from their torment.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard the report of the power of the Cross, how Paradise was opened thereby, and I cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:*

Why hast thou likened thyself, O my soul, unto the barren fig tree,[^75] in no wise fearing the felling and the eternal flame? Make haste therefore before the end, and arise!

*Troparion:*

Who can tell in any way the boundless sea of evils committed by me, and the depth of transgressions? Save me, O Virgin all-immaculate, who am in despair.

*Glory...*

[f. 124rA] *Troparion:*

I weep for myself when I come to my senses, over the multitude of my transgressions and the unquenchable fire: and I entreat thee, O Pure One, to give me time for repentance.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

May the perverse enemy not snatch away my wretched soul as a lion; but by thy power crush his soul-destroying teeth, O All-Good One.
