*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Rising early at dawn we cry aloud unto Thee, O Lord, save us: for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know no other.

*Troparion:*

Look down and hear my voice, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me, I pray, from eternal condemnation.

*Troparion:*

I have been wounded by the arrows of sin, and I cry unto thee: heal the wounds of my heart, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Have mercy upon me, O only compassionate Lover of mankind, through the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee: for Thou art my God and Lord.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Have mercy upon me, O only All-Praised One, I pray thy goodness: and vouchsafe me mercy.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

The abyss encompassed me, the whale became my grave; but I cried aloud unto Thee, the Lover of mankind, and Thy right hand saved me, O Lord.

*Troparion:*

As a roaring lion, the deceitful one seeks to tear me apart and make me food for his malice, O Pure One; but free me from his harm.

[f. 124vA] *Troparion:*

Cleanse me, O Lover of mankind, by the prayers of her who purely gave birth unto Thee, and deliver Thy world from every sorrow, and vouchsafe it eternal glory.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The abyss of transgressions has encompassed me, bringing me down into the depth of despair; I pray thee, O Pure One: bring me up from nethermost hades, who glorify thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

My life has been filled with transgressions and every slothfulness. Therefore before my end, O Pure One, turn me unto repentance and save me, O All-Praised One.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^76]

Rejoice, firm wall won by God! Rejoice, all-vanquishing downfall of barbarians! Rejoice, raising up of the faithful Orthodox people, O Theotokos, who truly hope in thee; and by thy prayers deliver us from all misfortunes and from demonic enemies.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

In the fiery furnace, Thou Who didst save the hymn-singing children: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

By thy goodness, O Sovereign Lady, grant me forgiveness of involuntary and voluntary evils.

*Troparion:*

My mind grows faint at the ungodly suggestions of evil thoughts: help me, O Good One.

*Glory...*

[f. 125rA] *Troparion:*

Heal now the wounds of the passions of my soul, O Theotokos, by the medicines of thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Grant me a contrite soul and a humble thought, O Good One: that I may glorify thee.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Born of the Father before the ages, Son and God, and in the latter times incarnate of the Virgin Mother: ye priests, sing; ye people, exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

In slothfulness have I, the wretched one, ended my life, and I have drawn near unto the end of my living; I cry unto thee: snatch my humble soul from the nets of the adversaries.

*Troparion:*

Against bodily sicknesses, grievous wounds of the soul, and the assault of passions, grant me the unhidden power of thy healing, as the only compassionate one.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Storm-tossed in the deep of life, and engulfed by demonic waves, save me, O Pure One, and by divine striving lead me up unto a life well-pleasing unto God, O Maiden.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

As into a bridal chamber leading thee, O Virgin, Gabriel was sent, crying aloud and saying: Rejoice, all-glorious Palace of Christ, the King of all, wherein He having dwelt, deified all earthly nature!

[f. 125vA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Thee Who beyond mind and word, art the Mother of God, who in time didst ineffably give birth unto the Timeless One: we the faithful with one accord magnify with hymns.

*Troparion:*

Thee, who didst ineffably give birth unto God, have we acquired as Protectress, an immovable wall, salvation of souls, and fountain of miracles.

*Troparion:*

Be merciful unto me, O Word of the true God, in the day of Judgment, through the supplications of her who gave birth unto Thee, and unite me with those at Thy right hand.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Pure One, from eternal fire, the unsleeping worm, and every torment, who have placed my hope in thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

When my soul is about to be separated from my wretched body, deliver me from the torment of invisible enemies, O Bride of God.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Engraving of Christ the Pantocrator enthroned in glory, with the Mother of God, the Forerunner, angels, and kneeling apostles, with monograms "IC", "XC", "MP", and "ѲY")*

[f. 126rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece with floral arabesques and birds)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Thursday at Compline,*[^77]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm: and He saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Troparion:*

The spiritual paradise knows thee beyond comparison to be another Paradise, O Virgin Bride of God, surpassing the paradise in Eden, O Mother of God: for thou hast budded forth incorruption for mankind.

*Troparion:*

Adam was forbidden to find the Tree of Life in Paradise, having partaken of the tree of knowledge; but He Who took flesh from thee, O Virgin, has granted immortality.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

In the beginning Adam was formed from the earth by the most pure hands of the Almighty; and from thee, O Theotokos Virgin, was born without seed the New Adam, the Creator of mankind.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The ultimate abyss of boundless transgressions has encompassed me, and brings me down into the depth of grievous despair: O thou who didst give birth unto the Abyss of mercy, make haste and save me!

[f. 126vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

He Who made the earth the mother of living plants, which were not upon it before: coming again without seed from a woman who knew not man, as from the earth, Thou wast incarnate, O Word of God, without change.

*Troparion:*

Thou art the hope and help of earthborn mortals, joy, protection, and refuge, O Sovereign Lady, Mother of Life. Therefore we pray, send down thy help upon all who sing of thee.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

We who are sick and held by grievous temptations, O all-creating compassionate God of all, bring unto Thee in prayer Thy three-ranked Tabernacle,[^78] and we cry aloud unto Thee: release the afflictions of Thy servants!

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

A sinful wave sorely troubles me, dragging me down into the depth of transgressions, O Most Pure One; and a storm of contrary thoughts tosses my soul with tempest: O thou who didst give birth unto the Helmsman, make haste to rescue thy servant!

[f. 127rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Discerning Thy divine self-emptying, farsighted Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

Eve, having obeyed the serpent, brought forth sorrow for women; but thou, O Maiden, having believed the divine utterances, hast budded forth Joy for the whole world.

*Troparion:*

In the beginning Eve was born from the rib of Adam; and now God is born of the Mother, Bride of God: Whom being incarnate, the Virgin without a Father has brought forth.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Eve rejoices: for the Maiden, having ineffably conceived the Judge and given birth unto the Compassionate One, showed her freed from the ancient condemnation of the First Mother.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

My life has been filled with transgressions, and my thoughts are subject to passions, and my soul is condemned. Therefore by thy tenderheartedness have mercy and save me, O Sovereign Lady.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment: I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

*Troparion:*

Having found thee, O Virgin Mother, as a pillar and cloud of Light, the Giver of light: we who walk in the deceitful desert are delivered from grievous ills.

[f. 127vA] *Troparion:*

Rejoice, O thou who didst in the flesh bring forth God, Who before the ages was incorporeally begotten of the Father, and Who was pleased to appear again unto us.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The sun was vanquished by thy brightness, O Mary: for Him Who adorned the heavens with luminaries, thou didst embrace in thy arms, and didst nourish from thy breasts.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Condemn me not to the unquenchable fire, O Christ my Savior, through the prayers of the Pure One who gave birth unto Thee.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of my passions, O Master Christ, which is troubled by a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.

*Troparion:*

He Who of old saved the prophet Jonah in the sea monster: Him, the marvelous God, thou didst carry unharmed in thy womb, O pure Ever-Virgin.

*Troparion:*

Holding God the Lord of the sea without storm, as bearing the heavenly powers, O Most Pure Virgin, thou didst put to sleep the salty turmoil of deceit.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Having given birth unto Christ, the calm of all: by thy prayers unto Him, O Most Pure One, graciously calm the unstable storm of passions that assails me.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

In the day of sorrow, when I am about to be released from bodily bonds, stand by me, and snatch me from demonic dominion.

[f. 128rA] *Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^79]

Beholding Thee hanging willingly upon the Cross between thieves, O Christ, Thy Mother, torn in maternal compassion, cried aloud: O Sinless Son, how art Thou unjustly nailed to the Cross as an evildoer, desiring to give life unto the human race, as Thou art all-good?

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord God of our fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame, and bedewed the youths, who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

God fulfilled His coming promised unto Forefather Abraham, having become thy Son, O Blessed One, and in the nations He blessed his seed.

*Troparion:*

Thou art the spotless ladder of Jacob, for God designated thee alone to be Mother, for whose sake God united those offspring.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

The Son, exalted above all with the Father and the Spirit, having chosen thee, O Pure One, loved thee greatly as a dwelling for Himself, being incarnate.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Ever entreat God, Whom thou didst bear, to save me who am in despair, and to obtain forgiveness for me who cry aloud in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God!

[f. 128vA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven an all-beauteous choir, sang: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Thy Childbirth escapes inquiry, O Virgin, but is manifested by faith unto those who cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Not having fallen away from virginal glory, thou wast enriched with maternal honor, O Bride of God, into whom shone He Who worked wonders: Whom exalt above all unto the ages!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Behold, O Good and Most Pure One, the affliction of my humble soul, and swiftly transform my sickness: that I may glorify thee unto the ages.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Mercifully accept Thy Mother, O Word, who prays Thee to save Thy people, O Good One, whom Thou didst acquire by Thy precious Blood: that they may bless Thee unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name: Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

O Theotokos, Sovereign Lady, entreat the adored Trinity, of Whom without passion thou didst bear One in the flesh for us, to give peace unto those on earth, and forgiveness of transgressions unto those who sing of thee.

[f. 129rA] *Troparion:*

Though Emmanuel is understood to be one in Person, yet He is twofold in nature: for this proclaims two wills and energies in Him,[^80] whose Birthgiver we confess as the Mother of God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Behold, I weep, pondering the multitude of my sins, the onslaught of passions, the despondency of my soul, and the distraction of my mind: vouchsafe me salvation who am in despair.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

My enemies having come upon me, O Lord, like robbers, and having stripped off my lofty, radiant, and light-like garment, have laid many wounds upon me: do Thou stand before me, and raise me up while I am still alive!

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: Troparia: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Engraving of the Anastasis / Descent into Hades. Christ radiant within a mandorla, carrying the Cross staff, raises Adam and Eve by their hands from open sarcophagi, with Cyrillic monograms "IC" and "XC")*

[f. 129vA] *(Woodcut: Large headpiece depicting the Nativity of the Most Holy Theotokos. Saint Anna rests upon a couch attended by maidens, Saint Joachim looks on, and the infant Mary is being prepared for her bath)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 5)

*On Friday at Compline,*[^81]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 5. Heirmos:*

Christ shook out into the Red Sea the horse and rider, He Who crushes wars with a high arm: and He saved Israel, as he sang a song of victory.

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

All generations of mankind praise thee, O Virgin, as of old thou didst prophetically foretell;[^82] therefore accept me who sing of thee, O Sovereign Lady, and enlighten and instruct me.

*Troparion:*

Thou hast blunted the sting of death and the sin of the world, O Maiden, having given birth unto the True Life; therefore, O Sovereign Lady, swiftly blunt the sharp arrows of my passions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Shown forth from the ages as alone undefiled by virginal hands, the fig-leaf garment of Adam [f. 130rA] *Troparion:*

...thou hast torn apart. Therefore clothe me now with the garment of chastity through thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Wealth and divine glory did many daughters visibly acquire beforehand, but beyond comparison, O Sovereign Lady, thou hast exalted thyself above all; therefore enrich me now with heavenly and divine graces.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupported weight: establish Thy Church upon the immovable Rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, Who alone art Good and the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

The depth and height known in thee, O all-pure Maiden, born from thy womb by the unsearchable wisdom of God: deliver my heart from the deep serpent-like cunning of that wisdom.

*Troparion:*

Thou Who of old by Thy Word didst form the winged creatures and reptiles from the waters, which were not before: Thou again, O Christ, from the blood of the Pure Virgin, by Thy divine Incarnation, hast fashioned a strange garment for Thyself.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thou alone hast appeared as purification for nature, O Most Pure One, into whom the divine Fire dwelt unconsumed; cleanse this openly: for thou indeed from sins and my passions [f. 130vA] *Troparion:*

...cleanse the defilement of my sins and passions, and enlighten me by thy prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Knowing thee, O Most Pure One, to be the chalice of the offspring of the new vine, manifestly giving drink unto the faithful for the remission of transgressions, I pray thee: with the outpouring of divine water give drink unto my heart.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Discerning Thy divine self-emptying, farsighted Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.

*Troparion:*

Thou hast appeared as an unplowed furrow, having budded forth the unsown divine Ear of wheat: wherewith nourish me who hunger, with divine gifts and graces.

*Troparion:*

I am sorely and mortally sick, and held fast by the fire of passions of the soul, O Sovereign Lady; give me drink with the water of thy prayers, and swiftly raise me up.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

As thou art the animate City of God, whose streams the spiritual rivers make glad,[^83] by the pillar of thy prayers establish the dwelling of my soul.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Knowing thee to be the Cloud that dropped down true righteousness,[^84] I pray thee, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady: swiftly deliver thy servant from all who wrong me.

[f. 131rA] *Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.

*Troparion:*

Utterly calm the turmoil and the wave of sin and of my passions, O Virgin Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto the Cause of dispassion.

*Troparion:*

Christ, Who from on high appeared as the instrument of the divine garment of majesty: O Pure One, clothe my stripped soul with the garment of virtues.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Grant me forgiveness of transgressions by thy supplications, O Pure Virgin, who didst give birth unto Christ and the Lord, our divine cleansing.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

By the ministry of thy prayers, O Virgin, consume the stripes, wounds, and festering sores of sin for me, and grant strength unto thy servant.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Calm the sea of passions, Master Christ, fiercely raging with a soul-destroying storm, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art merciful.

*Troparion:*

Thou who didst give birth unto the Light, the Maker of luminaries: enlighten my soul now, and deliver me from the darkening of passions, O All-Hymned One.

[f. 131vA] *Troparion:*

Entreat thy Son, O Birthgiver of God, Who of old sweetened the waters of Marah,[^85] swiftly to deliver me from grievous passion and bitterness.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

A torrent of passions troubles my soul, O Most Pure One; but dry it up by thy prayers, and consume the evil thoughts.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Delivering mankind, Christ came forth from Zion,[^86] and from thy womb, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady; through Him deliver me also from adversities and sorrows.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^87]

Desponding in every way, O passionate soul, hast thou forgotten thy God Who has mercy upon thee, and despising His commandments, dost thou end thy life in prodigality with a harmed mind? Depart from evil, crying aloud unto the Birthgiver of God: Have mercy upon my despairing soul!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Lord God of the fathers, Who is exalted above all, quenched the flame and bedewed the children who sang in harmony: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:*

The demonic hosts are terrified and tremble at the invocation of thy name, O Most Pure One; delivering me from them, save and preserve me, sheltering me from every harm.

[f. 132rA] *Troparion:*

Ineffable is thy glory, O Virgin, for thou didst give birth unto the Lord of Glory; therefore vouchsafe me the glory of thy Son and my God, through thy prayers.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Incline Thyself unto the supplications of thy servant, O Sovereign Lady, and swiftly deliver me from misfortunes and sorrows, and from all temptations visible and invisible.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having been wholly muddied and defiled by the passions of shame, O Most Pure One, wash me with purity, and make me bright, O Virgin, by the sprinklings of thy prayers.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Unto the Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven an all-beauteous choir, sang: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Make the barren womb of my soul barren and dried up to the bearing of passions, and rain down upon me the divine dew, O light cloud,[^88] that I may bear the fruits of repentance.

*Troparion:*

Calm the wave of passionate thoughts and the storm, O Most Pure One, by thy prayers, and guide me unto the course of dispassion: that I may warmly glorify thee unto all ages.

*Glory...*

[f. 132vA] *Troparion:*

Spiritual Door, and closed gate through which God alone passed:[^89] shut and bar the doors of my passions, and open unto me the gates of hope.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Take from me the burden of transgressions, O Virgin Mother: who alone hast ineffably given birth unto the Lamb of God and the Word,[^90] Who takes away all the sin of the whole world.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Dance, O Isaiah! For the Virgin held in her womb and brought forth a Son, Emmanuel: both God and Man, the Orient is His name:[^91] Whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Having defiled my soul as a man with many transgressions, and having been muddied with carnal passions, I now entreat thee earnestly and implore thee, O Virgin: cleanse me, O Pure One, by thy prayers, from every malice.

*Troparion:*

Thou hast given birth unto the God of mercy and compassions, the Lover of mankind, the only Good, Long-suffering, and Tenderhearted: show Him propitious unto me in good season, O Pure One, by thy supplications, and grant remission of transgressions.

*Glory...*

[f. 133rA] *Troparion:*

Raise up thy servant, O Sovereign Lady, who am now held fast by the slumber of despondency and asleep in pleasures, unto the virtues of good deeds, by thine unsleeping maternal prayers.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

With blessings I bless thee, O Most Pure One, and warmly magnify thee: bless therefore me, thy singer, and deliver me from all grievous and sorrowful things, and preserve me unconquered by thy hands.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Large architectural engraving of the Annunciation. The Archangel Gabriel, holding a staff or flowering branch, points upward to the Holy Spirit descending in rays of light, with the Cyrillic inscription "Гаврїилъ". The Most Holy Virgin Mary stands at right before a lectern beneath an arched baldachin canopy, with decorative floral borders and stars)*

[f. 133vA] *(Woodcut: Full-page engraving of the Mother of God Hodegetria crowned with the Christ Child holding an orb and sceptre, flanked by the Archangel Michael and the Archangel Gabriel, with the Cyrillic inscriptions "Архистратигъ Михайлъ", "Архистратигъ Гаврїилъ", and "Радуйся Невѣсто Неневѣстная", and a bottom rhymed distich: "Blessed Virgin Mary and Mother, preserve Christians by thy grace")*

[f. 134rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece depicting the Holy Trinity enthroned amid clouds and celestial choirs, flanked by floral vines and birds)*

---

# TONE 6

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

*On Saturday at Compline,*[^92]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

When Israel walked upon the dry land through the abyss with their footsteps, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh engulfed, they cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Having inexhaustible grace as divine, O unwedded Mother: despise not those who flee unto thee, ever saving them from misfortunes and sorrows.

*Troparion:*

Lo, a time of despondency has come upon me; arise, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, and give me a helping hand: for thou hast filled the world with divine gladness.

*Glory...*

[f. 134vA] *Troparion:*

Grant me swiftly thy mighty protection, O Birthgiver of God, as thou art our Protectress, who am held fast by afflictions, and preserve me unharmed from them.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Death hastens unto my soul, and the temptations of those who hate me encompass me like asps to destroy me; but do thou thyself save me.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^93]

*Troparion:*

As thou didst give birth unto Life, O Birthgiver of God, make alive my soul that is slain by transgressions.

*Troparion:*

Preserve from every temptation those who flee unto thee, O Birthgiver of God, our Hope.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

From the adversities that rise up bitterly against me, save me, O all-immaculate Mother of my Lord.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

O thou who didst grant salvation unto the world through thy divine Childbirth: deliver me from misfortunes.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

The Prophet Habakkuk heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, that Thou wast about to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard the report of Thee and was afraid: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^94]

[f. 135rA] *Troparion:*

Through poverty the strength of my soul has grown faint, and a grievous darkness has fallen upon me, arising from the onset of transgressions: look down, O Birthgiver of God, as the light-bearing Cloud of God, and enlighten me, I pray.

*Troparion:*

Turn the storm of my evils and the turmoil of my transgressions into the calm of salvation, as thou art a harbor, O all-immaculate one, and deliver me from their destruction, I pray: for roaring as lions they seek to swallow me up.[^95]

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

By night and by day, on earth and at sea, and in every place, being a firm salvation, O Birthgiver of God, invincible Protection, save me: for after God we Christians truly place our hope in thee.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

From great and diverse transgressions and adversities thou ever savest me. Therefore, knowing Thee to have given birth unto the Lord, I pray, and unto Thee I flee, the invincible Help of the afflicted: lead me out from misfortunes by thy supplications.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Christ Who didst make light shine upon all the world: enlighten my heart, crying aloud unto Thee from the night, and save me.

*Troparion:*

We glorify thee as the protection of our salvation, O immaculate Mother of the Word, not fearing the snares of men.

[f. 135vA] *Troparion:*

Having thee as an insurmountable wall, we are delivered from temptations and from the uprising of sorrows, O Most Pure One.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Deliver me from the evil tongues of men, O Pure One: for as a razor have they been sharpened,[^96] bitterly seeking to destroy my soul.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Earnestly falling down before thee, I pray: as thou art the Mother of my Creator, set me free from the misfortunes that beset me.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Swallowed up by the sins of this life, I cry aloud unto Thee, O Christ: As Jonah from corruption, set me free!

*Troparion:*

Having tasted bitterness, I am estranged from divine sweetness, O Most Pure One; therefore I cry unto thee: grant me help.

*Troparion:*

The darkening of passions has made me a slave of corruption; therefore, O thou who didst give birth unto the Light, set me free, O Sovereign Lady.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

In harmony and with faith I confess thee, O Most Pure One, and offer thee a sacrifice of praise, having been delivered through thee from sorrows.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Mouths of iniquity have opened against me, O Sovereign Lady; therefore, as our Protectress, swiftly set me free from them.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*[^97]

Steadfast Protectress of Christians, unfailing Mediatress before the Creator: despise not the voices of sinners in prayer, but in thy goodness anticipate us for our help, who in faith cry aloud unto thee: hasten unto prayer and make speed unto intercession, ever protecting those who honor thee, O Birthgiver of God.

[f. 136rA] *Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Angel made the furnace drop dew for the holy children, but the command of God burned the Chaldeans, compelling the tyrant to cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^98]

*Troparion:*

Temptations encompass my soul now like fat bulls, O Most Pure Virgin;[^99] do thou thyself set me free from them.

*Troparion:*

Diligently defending those who are in misfortune and in sorrow, ever grant joy, O Birthgiver of God.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Surround me with defense, O Virgin, who am wholly condemned by despair of my deeds and by sorrows: for thou art the indestructible protection of all.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

By thy representation we are delivered from misfortunes and sorrows, and we find great and inexhaustible riches.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Out of the flame Thou didst pour dew for the saints, and the righteous man's sacrifice Thou didst burn up with water:[^100] for Thou, O Christ, doest all things whatsoever Thou wilt: Thee we exalt above all unto the ages!

[f. 136vA] *Troparion:*

I am consumed by the malicious afflictions of men, and I pray thee, O Birthgiver of God: deliver me from their vain counsel.[^101]

*Troparion:*

I have been overcome in sorrows by my temptations, and I pray thee, O Sovereign Lady: save me unharmed from them.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Save me, O Pure One, from the deceitful man, and from the tongue and lips, and from tyranny and every constraint.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Seduced in my disposition, being without defense, I cry unto the Birthgiver of God: Deliver me from all evils!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Thou who didst receive joy through the Angel, and didst give birth unto thy Creator: O Virgin, save those who magnify thee!

*Troparion:*

Be a prayer for us who are in the sorrow of this life, O Birthgiver of God, and save from misfortunes those who now flee unto thee.

*Troparion:*

Truly on earth and at sea thou alone hast appeared, O Pure One, as an indestructible protection unto those who flee unto thee with a humble-minded soul.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Unto me who have foolishly enslaved myself to all manner of grievous falls, grant freedom through thy supplications, O All-Hymned One.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having acquired thee as hope and firm protection, O pure one, magnifying thee as the Birthgiver of God, I finish the song in faith.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after the Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 137rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece with floral arabesques)*

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

*On Sunday at Compline,*[^102]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

When Israel walked upon the dry land through the abyss with their footsteps, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh engulfed, they cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God!

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Drive away the cloud of passions from my mind, O Pure One, and make the Light shine upon me, O Most Pure One who didst give birth unto the Son, the Radiance of the Father,[^103] that I may sing of thee.

*Troparion:*

As Gabriel unto the Virgin, come, ye faithful, let us also cry aloud with soul-felt reverence: Truly adorned one, who didst give birth unto Joy, Rejoice, O Most Pure One![^104]

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Carrying in thy womb in the last days the Son, Who before the ages was impassibly begotten of the Father, O Most Pure One, thou hast brought forth Him twofold, God and Man.[^105]

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Before thy Childbirth thou didst give birth unto the Only-begotten without corruption, and a Virgin thou didst remain after...

[f. 137vA] *Theotokion:*

...Childbirth, O Most Pure One: therefore we the faithful cry aloud unto thee: Rejoice!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.

*Troparion:*

There is none undefiled as thou, O Most Pure Birthgiver of God, who didst give birth unto the Creator and Sovereign Lord of all. Therefore unto thee all we faithful thankfully cry: Rejoice!

*Troparion:*

A fierce surging of passions and the bitterness of many falls trouble me, casting me truly into the depth of my evils; give me a hand, O Pure One, and save me.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Dispel the darkness of my transgressions, O Virgin, and make radiant the light of dispassion, O Pure One, who hast ineffably brought forth Life, the unapproachable Sun.[^106]

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Having cleansed me from transgressions and defilement by the fire of the fear of God, O Maiden, clothe me with the radiant garment of virtues, and unite me with the Choir of the saints.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church cries aloud in godly manner, celebrating in the Lord with a pure mind.[^107]

[f. 138rA] *Troparion:*

O thou who didst give birth unto the Light, dispel the darkness of my many transgressions by thy radiance, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, who hast made the Sun of Righteousness shine upon the faithful.[^108]

*Troparion:*

Unto thee, O Most Pure One, I now offer the hope of my infirmity: that from thee I may go away unailing, and having received salvation, I may sing: Rejoice!

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Grant us thy rich compassions, O Most Pure One, who run with faith and love into thy Temple, and deliver us from all temptations and sorrows.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The Word dwelt within thee, O Most Pure One, delivering the world from the fury of passions, O Sovereign Lady, and vouchsafing the Kingdom of Heaven unto those who sing of thee.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

By Thy divine light, O Good One, enlighten with love the souls of those who rise early at dawn unto Thee, I pray: to see Thee, O Word of God, the true God, calling them back from the darkness of sin.[^109]

*Troparion:*

By the divine radiance of Him Who shone forth from thy loins, enlighten my soul sleeping in darkness, O Virgin, and drive away the mist of sin, illuminating me with thy light.

*Troparion:*

Heal my soul that has fallen among thieves, O Good One,[^110] mercifully dropping the oil and wine of thy tenderheartedness, unstintingly granting it unto me.

*Glory...*

[f. 138vA] *Troparion:*

Heal our bodily infirmities and those of the soul, who flee unto thee, O All-Hymned One: for thou art the strength of those who run to the protection of thy prayer, O Birthgiver of God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

The divine Holy Spirit, having dwelt within thy womb, O Most Holy and Pure One, and overshadowed thee, showed thee forth as the House of the Trinity, by the Father's good pleasure conceiving the Word and bringing Him forth.[^111]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Beholding the sea of this life surging with the storm of temptations, and fleeing to Thy calm haven, I cry aloud unto Thee: Bring up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One![^112]

*Troparion:*

Dispel the darkness of my wretched soul, and enlighten me, O Most Pure One, with the Light that shone forth from thy womb and illuminated all human nature.

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Pure One, from the snare of enemies visible and invisible, and grant me the shelter of thy mighty protection: for unto thee, O Virgin, have I now fled.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

He Who created all things was incarnate in the last days from thy holy womb, and having restored human nature that had fallen grievously from Adam, He renewed the world.[^113]

*Both now...*

[f. 139rA] *Theotokion:*

The majesty of the holy Angels sings of thee, and I pray thee, O all-immaculate one: take away from me the unfitting fantasies of demons, preserving my heart in stillness.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

The assaults of the adversaries wage war against thine inheritance, O Pure Sovereign Lady; destroy their craftiness, and cast down to the earth their haughty insolence: for thou art the Protectress of those who faithfully pray unto thee.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Angel made the furnace drop dew for the holy children, but the command of God burned the Chaldeans, compelling the tyrant to cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

Having enlightened me who was in the darkness of ignorance with unapproachable Light, O Most Pure One, guide me unto the light to cry aloud faithfully and piously unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

Drive away the dark cloud from my soul, O Birthgiver of God, illuminating me with the light of the Creator of all: for thou art the receptacle of the Co-eternal Light, Who shone forth impassibly from the Father.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Incline Thyself unto our supplications, O Pure Birthgiver of God, being moved mercifully by prayer unto God, and make Him merciful unto us, who ever hope in thee, O Good One.

*Both now...*

[f. 139vA] *Theotokion:*

Unto me who am defiled by the passions of wicked deeds, grant a place of cleansing, O Pure One: drops of tears, washing away the fountain of mire of the shame of my wicked sin.[^114]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Out of the flame Thou didst pour dew for the saints, and the righteous man's sacrifice Thou didst burn up with water: for Thou, O Christ, doest all things whatsoever Thou wilt: Thee, the Lord, we exalt above all unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

O thou who didst in the flesh bring forth the Master of all: snatch me, O Maiden, from the assaults of passions, who sing with perpetual love unto the Creator of all unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

As I lie upon a bed of pain, raise me up by thy visitation, O Maiden, and grant me health of soul and body, who sing of Christ unto the ages.

*Troparion:*

Quench the flame of my passions by the dew of thy prayers, O Sovereign Lady, and save me, even as of old thy Son delivered the Children who exalted Him above all unto the ages.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

I am not terrified by the barking of the adversary,[^115] having my hope and expectation in thee, O Good One: for thou ever protectest, saving from misfortunes those who praise thee.

[f. 140rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O Most Pure One, the incarnate Word was manifested unto us: Whom magnifying, with the heavenly hosts we call thee blessed.[^116]

*Troparion:*

Deliver him who flees unto thee in faith from all evils, O Most Pure Virgin, and grant health unto me who have fled beneath thy protection, O Maiden, and deliverance from grievous ills: that with pious voice I may ever glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

Thou hast dissolved the curse of sorrow, O Pure One,[^117] and hast poured forth joy upon the world by thy Childbirth, having brought forth the Fountain of blessing; therefore praising thee, the All-Hymned, we all, the faithful, ever call thee blessed in piety.

*Glory...*

*Troparion:*

Thy womb became a receptacle for the Light, O All-Hymned Birthgiver of God, Who shone forth from the Father before all ages; therefore, all of us glorifying thee as the Birthgiver of God: Him we magnify as thy Son and God.

*Both now...*

*Theotokion:*

Thou alone, O Pure One, hast brought forth in time the timeless Son Who shone forth from the Father,[^118] through the overshadowing of the divine Holy Spirit. Therefore, O Most Pure One, as the unwedded Birthgiver of God, all we faithful confess thee with tongue and heart.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

[f. 140vA] *On Monday at Small Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, and beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, Israel cried out: Let us sing unto God a song of victory![^119]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Bowed down by a multitude of temptations and grievous deeds, I bend the neck of my soul and body unto thee, O Pure One, and earnestly cry out unto thee: Do thou set me upright!

*Troparion:*

All-immaculate Maiden, mighty help of the faithful and hope of Christians: deliver me from bodily pleasures and passions that war against me.

*Glory:*

O Purest One, who gavest birth to the noetic Light, the Guide of those who are in darkness: enlighten my soul and illuminate my mind, dispelling the gloom of passions and transgressions.

*Both now:*

[f. 141rA] *Theotokion:*

Unto my darkened soul, beset by improper thoughts, dispel the gloom, O Theotokos, Sovereign Lady, by the light of the holy commandments of Him Who was incarnate from thee.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thine acknowledgment.[^120]

*Troparion:*

Having hoped in thee, O Pure and Most Pure One, may I not fall away from expectation; but as the merciful Mother of the God Who loves mankind, deliver me from the snares of the enemy.

*Troparion:*

O Mary, Mother of God, gate of salvation through which the Creator of all alone did pass: open unto me the divine gates of repentance, O Most Pure One.

*Glory:*

Be thou a haven and guardian unto me, O Pure One, who am ever tempest-tossed by the grievous surges of passions in the sea of life, O only Ever-Virgin.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

O Mary, Mother of God, enlighten my soul, which is grievously darkened by many sins, wounded by the arrow of the evil one, and grown faint.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

"Christ is my power, God and Lord," the venerable Church in godly manner calls out, crying: feasting in the Lord from a pure understanding.

[f. 141vA] *Troparion:*

Being my strength, O Most Pure One, and refuge, and impregnable rampart, and intercessor before God: deliver me from the everlasting flame and from Gehenna.

*Troparion:*

O All-honorable Mary, dispel the turmoil of passions from my mind and the storm of temptations; for thou hast given birth to the Fountain of dispassion, O Mother, Ever-Virgin.

*Glory:*

O Pure and All-immaculate One, who becamest a spacious receptacle for the Divine indwelling of Purity: consume the defilement and uncleanness of my soul.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Thee, the only Most Pure and only undefiled One, do I implore, O Maiden: cleanse with thy prayers me who am defiled by pleasurable passions and have mired my soul in misery.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

By Thy divine light, O Good One, lovingly illumine, I pray, the souls of those who awake early unto Thee, that they may see Thee, O Word of God, as true God, calling them forth from the gloom of sins.[^121]

*Troparion:*

Defiled by thoughts and mired by many falls, O Mother of God, I implore thee, the only undefiled One: take pity and save me by thine intercession.

*Troparion:*

With thy divine radiance, O Good One, enlighten my soul darkened by pleasures, and direct it unto the paths of salvation, O thou who alone hast given birth to Christ the Savior.

*Glory:*

[f. 142rA] *Troparion:*

Tear asunder the fetters of my sins by thine intercession and protection, O Virgin, and fill my miserable soul with the joy of divine stillness, and deliver it from darkness.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Having now acquired thee alone as a divine refuge, O Good One, I faithfully fall down before thee and cry: Be thou my protection and shelter of salvation, O Sovereign Lady of the world, and save me.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, I run to Thy calm haven and cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One![^122]

*Troparion:*

Guilty of sins and grievously wounded in my wretchedness, fleeing unto thee, most merciful Mother of God, I pray: Destroy the assaults of my transgressions.

*Troparion:*

O gracious Gate, who hast opened the heavenly gate unto the faithful: open unto me the radiant gate of repentance, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me from the gates of death.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Establish me who am bowed down by passions from the assaults of the adversary, O Mother of God, as thou hast given birth to the Fountain of dispassion; for unto thee have I fled, the Consoler of my miserable soul.

*Both now:*

[f. 142vA] *Theotokion:*

Look upon me the humble one, O Sovereign Lady, and save me who am without hope; for thou art my expectation and protection, my life and the light of my heart, O Theotokos, and my confirmation.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

Truly thou art the strength of the helpless, O Most Pure Virgin Mother; therefore, we the humble are exalted through thee, in thee we hold our head high; unto all thou art a Protection, and an intercessor before God.[^123]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

An Angel made the furnace pour forth dew for the holy Children, while the command of God consumed the Chaldeans; and it persuaded the tyrant to cry out: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^124]

*Troparion:*

We ever have thee as our cleansing before God, O All-immaculate One. Therefore, O Most Pure One, entreat Him to deliver from fearful torments yonder all who truly confess thee to be the Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

Raise me up who am held fast by sloth unto the doing of divine deeds, O Most Pure One, strengthening me against the enemies who ever sorely battle against me and beguile me with contrary thoughts.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Leave me not to perish, O Virgin, nor suffer me to become food for the fierce serpent who ever thirsts for my destruction; but grant me thy rich mercy.

*Both now:*

[f. 143rA] *Theotokion:*

Cease not to pray to our God Who loves mankind, O All-immaculate One, that we may receive complete forgiveness, O Pure One, and may attain the prepared heavenly good things and joy that cannot be taken away.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and with water Thou didst burn up the sacrifice of the righteous one; for Thou, O Christ, dost accomplish all things whatsoever Thou willest: therefore we exalt Thee, the Lord, unto all ages.[^125]

*Troparion:*

Through thy childbearing, O All-honorable One, show my barren heart to be fruitful in good works, by entreating God.

*Troparion:*

Bring a supplication unto God, O Virgin, to save our souls, O full of the grace of God, and deliver us from the reception of passions and the evil temptation of demons.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Unto thy protection do I flee, O All-immaculate One, and I now present thee as the Protectress of my life: deliver me, O Maiden, from the fearful judgment, the trial, and the eternal fire.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Establish my soul which hath been shaken by the malice of the enemy, O Pure Virgin, and rescue me from the fiery torment and the painful portion of the goats.[^126]

[f. 143vA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O Most Pure One, the incarnate Word appeared unto us, Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we bless thee.[^127]

*Troparion:*

The surging of passions troubles me, and with pleasures drowns me, O all-immaculate Virgin, who didst bear the Helmsman, Christ: stretch forth a hand of help unto me and save me, the only salvation of those who with faith bless thee.

*Troparion:*

O Bridal Chamber and Throne of the Reigning One, Mountain of God, and elect City, and Paradise, all-radiant Cloud of the Sun: enlighten my soul, driving away the cloud of my sins, O full of divine grace.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

O Pure Virgin, graced of God, Gate of Light: open the gates of my soul, locking out the entrance of sin, lest the hand of the deceiver seize me, leading me grievously unto all-ruinous and fearful torment.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Arouse thyself, my soul, and watch in prayers and in all-good cares, driving away the sleep of despondency, having ever as an alert protectress the Pure Mother of God, the all-bountiful.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

[f. 144rA] *On Tuesday at Small Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, and beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, Israel cried out: Let us sing unto God a song of victory![^128]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thus I lament over my defiled life and the multitude of my measureless evils; what to confess I am at a loss to say and am terrified! Help me, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

Whence shall I begin to speak of my wicked and grievous falls, passionate as I am? Woe is me, what shall become of me hereafter! But O Sovereign Lady, before the end have mercy on me.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

The hour of death and the fearful Judgment Seat I ever contemplate, O Most Pure One, and being convicted, I tremble sorely; but help me.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Beholding me now stripped of divine virtues, and far departed and estranged from God, the destroyer of good things rushes to swallow me up; hasten, O Sovereign Lady!

[f. 144vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thine acknowledgment.[^129]

*Troparion:*

Filthily have I defiled my soul with my measureless evils, passionate that I am, O Theotokos, Sovereign Lady; and whither shall I go hereafter, being utterly possessed by despair?

*Troparion:*

Slain by the snares and huntings of the deceitful enemy, O All-immaculate Sovereign Lady, quicken me; for thou, O Theotokos, didst bear the hypostatic Life of all, that I, thine unworthy servant, may piously hymn thee, the all-praiseworthy.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

There is no other among men who has committed such improper deeds, nor was any born in the world as I, O Good One, darkened in mind, who have also defiled divine Baptism.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

I have reached the extremity of evils, O Most Holy Virgin; help me quickly! Heaven and earth cry bitterly because of my improper, unbecoming deeds.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

"Christ is my power, God and Lord," the venerable Church in godly manner calls out, crying: feasting in the Lord from a pure understanding.

*Troparion:*

The ranks of Angels and the hosts of the Heavenly Powers are in awe of the dominion of thy Son, O Pure One; yet now I, despairing, am possessed by fearless impudence.

[f. 145rA] *Troparion:*

All creation was greatly amazed and terrified, beholding me doing grievous, evil, and improper things, and it marvels at the great mercy of thy Son.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Grievously have I defiled the bodily church, and into the temple of the Lord, wherein men enter with trembling, I the prodigal enter without shame; woe is me!

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Show me not, O Sovereign Lady, show me not as an alien, in awe of the Blood of thy Son, me who am altogether unworthy; but wash me clean from the defilement of my transgressions.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

By Thy divine light, O Good One, lovingly illumine, I pray, the souls of those who awake early unto Thee, that they may see Thee, O Word of God, as true God, calling them forth from the gloom of sins.

*Troparion:*

By thy divine radiance, O Good One, heal the passions of my soul which the corrupter sowed in me, and deliver me from this bitter captivity; for he mocks, seeing me without protection.

*Troparion:*

Adam indeed transgressed a single commandment of thy Son, O Virgin, and fell into exile; how then shall I, the transgressor and apostate from Him, escape the abyss of my transgressions?

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

A murderer and fratricide indeed Cain showed himself of old, and was cursed by God; what then shall I do [f. 145vA] I the all-audacious? Having slain my soul now, yet I am not ashamed!

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

I have emulated fierce Esau in belly-madness and gluttony; I have defiled my soul with drunkenness and my life with self-indulgence, and who will not weep over me, passionate as I am? Woe is me![^130]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, I run to Thy calm haven and cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One!

*Troparion:*

Prodigal is my life, defiled is my soul, and my whole life is mired in misery; my body I have grievously mired in evils. Therefore make haste, O Maiden, help me!

*Troparion:*

The end is at hand, and I cannot endure it, O Good One; my conscience convicts me, for my wicked deeds and prodigal life stand before me, and I dread the Judgment Seat of thy Son, O Pure One.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

For the inflammation of my flesh, a fearful and unquenchable river of fire truly awaits me, and the sleepless worm; but quench these for me by thy prayers, O Most Pure One.[^131]

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Now am I possessed with trembling, O Good One, and I am terrified by the snare of the evil one; for before the end [f. 146rA] the corrupter wishes to slay me, holding me altogether captive and stripped of virtues.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

Hope, and Rampart, and Haven of thy people, O Virgin, from whom the Savior was dispassionately born for all mankind, and saved those who hope in thee: Him, thy Son, Whom thou didst weep for at the Cross, entreat now to deliver from corruption all who praise thee.[^132]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

An Angel made the furnace pour forth dew for the holy Children, while the command of God consumed the Chaldeans; and it persuaded the tyrant to cry out: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

Sevenfold has the evil one kindled the flame with passions for me, and by fornication he ever puts my heart to death. Therefore quench it with the streams of my tears, O Mother of God, and save me.[^133]

*Troparion:*

Being mired in the filth of my transgressions, cast me not away, O Sovereign Lady, Good One; for seeing me in despair, the all-wicked enemy laughs; but do thou thyself raise me up by thy sovereign hand.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Fearful indeed is the Judgment Seat, O my passionate and unfeeling soul, and unending and dreadful is the torment; nevertheless, fall down now before the Mother of thy Judge and God, and despair not of thyself.

*Both now:*

[f. 146vA] *Theotokion:*

Darkened am I, passionate as I am, by a multitude of measureless evils, and I have defiled soul, body, and mind. Therefore, O Most Pure One, by the radiance of thy light, lead me up quickly unto the sweetness of dispassion.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and with water Thou didst burn up the sacrifice of the righteous one; for Thou, O Christ, dost accomplish all things whatsoever Thou willest: therefore we exalt Thee unto all ages.[^134]

*Troparion:*

O Virgin Mother, who didst bear and hold in thine arms One of the Trinity, God: quench the fiery furnace of passions, and wash my soul with streams of tears.

*Troparion:*

I dread the coming of death, O Most Pure One, and now I am utterly afraid of that Judgment; yet doing evil deeds, I am not at all ashamed! Have pity on me before the end by thy prayers, O Virgin.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Unceasing sighings grant unto me, O Sovereign Lady, and give a shower of tears, that I may wash away my many transgressions and incurable wounds, so that I may attain eternal Life.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

The multitude of my evils have I confessed unto thee, O Sovereign Lady, that no other in the world has so provoked to anger God, thy Son and Lord; quickly incline Him to mercy toward me, O Virgin.

[f. 147rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O Most Pure One, the incarnate Word appeared unto men, Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we bless thee.[^135]

*Troparion:*

Lo, I come unto thee, O Most Pure One, with much fear and love, as thy servant who firmly knows thy many prayers; for truly a mother's prayer unto the Son can do much, O Sovereign Lady, for His heart is inclined to compassion.[^136]

*Troparion:*

Take the choirs of Archangels and the hosts of the heavenly armies of my Creator, the assemblies of the Apostles and Prophets, the Martyrs, the Venerable, and the Hieromartyrs, and make supplication, O Pure One, unto God for us.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Both now and then may I find thy help, O Pure One, in the hour wherein my spirit shall depart, attending quickly, deliver me from the torment of demons, O All-immaculate One; and leave me not, O Good One, to be delivered over unto them.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

A bountiful and man-befriending Judge do I expect in thy Son, O Pure One; despise me not, but make Him favorably inclined toward me, that He may place me on the right hand then, O All-immaculate One, at the Throne of His Judgment Seat; for in thee have I placed my hope.[^137]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion: after Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

[f. 147vA] *On Wednesday at Small Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

He was a helper and a protector unto me for salvation; He is my God, and I will glorify Him, the God of my father, and I will exalt Him; for He is gloriously glorified.[^138]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

How shall I approach thee, O Pure One, despairing and wretched as I am, having become utterly passionate with defilements? But taking pity, save my wretched soul, O Mother of God, Most Pure One.

*Troparion:*

Having arisen from the depth of dishonor, O humble soul, cry warmly unto the only Pure One: Have mercy on me, O Maiden, All-immaculate and Pure, and deliver me from passions.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Drowned in sins by the tempest of temptations, stretch forth now unto me a hand of help, O Pure One, and deliver me from the dishonor of passions, that I may ever glorify thee.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Deliver me from the grievous tempest of sin, O Mother of God, by which I am wholly defiled. Therefore I flee unto thee: lighten now for me the burden of my transgressions.

[f. 148rA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish, O Lord, upon the rock of Thy commandments my shaken heart, for Thou alone art Holy and Righteous.[^139]

*Troparion:*

Make youthful me who have grown old through the shameful passions of sin, O All-immaculate One, lightening the bonds of transgressions.

*Troparion:*

Cleanse me from clay and the mire of sin, and make me a divine temple, O Theotokos, who purely flee unto thee.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Arouse thyself, O wretched soul, from the sleep of despondency; and with thine eyes look up unto the only Maiden, the Bride of God, who saves thee.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

I come, O Sovereign Lady, despairing and contrite, and I run unto thy fervent prayer: save me, O Pure One, thy servant.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

The Prophet heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, that Thou wast about to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard the report of Thee and was afraid: glory to Thy power, O Lord![^140]

*Troparion:*

In truth I am about to be judged for deeds and matters, O Mother of God, before thy Son, O All-immaculate One: may I find thee then as a refuge and most sovereign protection, delivering me from all torments.

[f. 148vA] *Troparion:*

Wretched is my life, and my disposition is defiled with sins; I am wholly passionate, O Pure One: cleanse me from the defilement of transgressions, and make me whiter than snow by thine intercession before the compassionate God.[^141]

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Bound all around by the fetters of my transgressions, unable to turn aside toward the paths of repentance: stretch forth thy hand unto me, O All-immaculate One, and set straight my pathway, guiding me unto the way of salvation.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Deliver me from grievous torments, and outer darkness, and Gehenna by thy prayers, O Virgin; for thou hast the will and the power, having given birth to the only all-good Lord.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Waking early from the night, O Lover of mankind, enlighten me, I pray, and guide me in Thy commandments, and teach me, O Savior, to do Thy will.[^142]

*Troparion:*

In the day of Judgment appear, O Sovereign Lady, and stand by me, delivering me from torment and fire, that having been saved, I may sing of thy invincible grace.

*Troparion:*

Seeing me stripped of thy protection, O Sovereign Lady, the enemy plunged my soul into the depth of transgressions, and beholding me he laughs; but help me!

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Cause my wretched and passionate soul to arise, O Pure One, I pray thee, O Immaculate One [f. 149rA] and take away from it the heavy burden of many sins.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

A supplication do I bring unto thee, O All-immaculate One, from my defiled, wretched, and unclean lips; take pity on me through thy prayer, I beseech thee.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I cried with my whole heart unto the merciful God, and He heard me from the lowest hell, and brought up my life from corruption.[^143]

*Troparion:*

Receive me, the prodigal and most wretched one, O Master Christ, and deliver me from torment by the entreaties of her who bore Thee, and set me free from the left-hand portion.[^144]

*Troparion:*

The dark fear of hades grips me, O Pure One, and the portion of the goats troubles me: deliver me from it, O Most Pure Theotokos, I pray thee.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Having put to death the passion of my flesh, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos, quicken, O Pure One, my most wretched soul, and guide me onto the right way.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

A servant's supplication do I offer unto thee, senseless as I am, and I flee unto thy tender-hearted mercy: turn me not away put to shame, O Pure One.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

A sighing from the heart and from the depths of my being do I bring unto thee, O All-immaculate One, asking for thy abiding [f. 149vA] protection: have mercy on my all-passionate soul, and entreat the greatly merciful God to deliver me from the Judgment and the lake of fire, O only blessed one.[^145]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

We have sinned, we have transgressed, we have done unrighteously before Thee, neither have we kept nor done as Thou hast commanded us: yet betray us not utterly, O God of the fathers![^146]

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be merciful, as the godly-adorned Virgin Mother, knowing thee to be wholly pure, I the wretched one run unto thee: turn me not away put to shame from thee, O All-immaculate One.

*Troparion:*

Arouse thyself, O passionate soul, and arise, O wretched one; smite thy breast from the depths and pour forth fountains of tears, that the Mother of the compassion of Christ and God may have mercy on thee in thy misery.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Having become a temple of God of old through divine Baptism, I the wretched one have wholly defiled myself, and lie miserably in a terrible fall: but do thou raise me up, O All-immaculate One, hope of the despairing.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

A heavy cloud has covered my most wretched heart; darkness and gloom have come upon my passionate soul, O Bride of God: enlighten me with the rays of repentance, that I may glorify thee.

[f. 150rA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify, and before Whom the Cherubim and Seraphim tremble: let every breath and creation sing, bless, and exalt Him unto all ages.[^147]

*Troparion:*

The river of fire confounds me, and the lightless gloom utterly shakes me, the worm terrifies me, and the gnashing of teeth: may I find thee then, O Virgin, as a sovereign protectress.[^148]

*Troparion:*

Let me not become fuel for the fire, O Lord, nor let me hear the voice of Thy threat, having Thy pure Mother praying unto Thee: by her entreaties have mercy on me, as I cry unto Thee.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Vouchsafe me to hear the divine voice, and show me, O Christ, to be a partaker among the elect of the Lord: let not the place of weeping receive me, having the Theotokos praying unto Thee for this.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Send down upon me now streams of tears, and create within me sighings from the soul, O Pure One, ever to fall down before thy protection: that through thy prayer I may find absolution of my sins.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Inconceivable is the Childbearing of a seedless conception; incorrupt is the Birthgiving of a husbandless Mother: for the Birthgiving of God[^149] [f. 150vA] renews natures; therefore do all generations orthodoxly magnify thee as the Bride of God and Mother.[^150]

*Troparion:*

The fearful day of judgment confounds me, O Virgin, the gnashing of teeth, my heart melts, the standing of the goats terrifies me; but may I find thee as a helper in grievous perils, O Theotokos, when I am about to be judged.

*Troparion:*

Now trembling holds me fast, and fear of Gehenna confounds me, and what to do I know not, wretched and condemned as I am! Therefore I flee unto thee, and with fervent compunction cry unto thee: Despise me not, O Most Pure One.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Beholding the judgment seat, and contemplating Thy judgment, O my Christ, wherein I am about to be righteously judged, condemned for my deeds: yet then I have as an intercessor praying unto Thy manifold tender mercies, Thy Mother, the Bride of God.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Mentally touching thy footsteps, O All-immaculate One, I pray thee: receive my weeping, give ear unto my singing, and grant me remission of transgressions as I cry unto thee, through whatever prayers thou knowest, O Virgin, that with love I may magnify thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after Our Father: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 151rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece with floral foliage and central blossom)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

*On Thursday at Small Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Of which the Acrostic is: Grant me, O Virgin, tears of repentance.*[^151]

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, and beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, Israel cried out: Let us sing unto God a song of victory![^152]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Receive, O Virgin, my supplication from unworthy and defiled lips, me who have dared boldly to offer it; and do thou, O Pure One, shine the light of compunction upon thy servant.

*Troparion:*

Grant me a shower of tears by thy prayers, O Virgin Mother Pure: that I may weep fervently over what I have committed on earth, and through thee escape eternal torment.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Cast not away my weeping, O thou who didst give birth to the Fountain of tender mercy; but graciously, O Good One, with thy merciful eye, O Theotokos, heal the passions of my soul.

*Both now:*

[f. 151vA] *Theotokion:*

With sighing weep, O my soul, and wail from within thyself, bowing down before the Mother of God, and saying: Deliver me, the guilty one, from fearful torment.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^153]

*Troparion:*

There is no other who has transgressed as I, O Most Holy Pure One, enslaved by the wickednesses of measureless thoughts; but placing my hope in thee, I pray: snatch me from them.

*Troparion:*

Lo, all the enemy's evils have come upon me as wounds, and growing heavy, they have stank exceedingly, and lead my soul into every downfall.[^154]

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Manifold is the multitude of my transgressions. Therefore, O Most Holy One, falling down in faith, I, the lowly one, cry unto thee: in the fearful hour, entreat Him Whom thou didst incarnate.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Holy Theotokos, rescue me who am guilty of every condemnation, as having maternal boldness toward God, and number me among the guests of His Bridal Chamber.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church in godly manner calls out, crying: feasting in the Lord from a pure understanding.[^155]

[f. 152rA] *Troparion:*

Grant me a fountain of tears, O Sovereign Lady, that I may wash away the filth of my lowly soul, and find the beauty which I lost through the counsel of the serpent.[^156]

*Troparion:*

O my merciful God, O God Lover of mankind, by the prayers of her who bore Thee, now pour out Thy mercy upon me: Thy Mother, my hope and protectress, entreats Thee.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Me the prodigal, me the dreadful one, who have sinned much toward thee senselessly and shamelessly, O All-immaculate One, take pity and save, and deliver from Gehenna.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst destroy the sting of death, having given birth to the Life of all. Therefore I also cry unto thee: I have sinned, save me, O Pure One, by thy maternal supplications.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

By Thy divine light, O Good One, lovingly illumine, I pray, the souls of those who awake early unto Thee: that they may see Thee, O Word of God, as true God, calling them forth from the gloom of sins.[^157]

*Troparion:*

Have mercy on me, O Pure One, who have placed all my hope in thee, and take pity on me who call upon thee in faith: look past all the grievous passions of my soul.

*Troparion:*

Save me from wicked passions, afflictions, and the harm of adversaries, and deliver my lowly soul from their barking, [f. 152vA] O Virgin, lest they say: We have prevailed against him.[^158]

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Appearing pure in body and soul, O Most Pure One, free my soul from defilement, granting me to live purely, and guide me to do the divine will of the Lord.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Thee alone do I have as refuge and reconciliation with God, I who have spent my whole life in manifold transgressions. Therefore do thou thyself take pity on me, O Theotokos.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, I run to Thy calm haven and cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One![^159]

*Troparion:*

Set upright, O Sovereign Lady, my slight sighing and the lifting up of my hands as a sweet-smelling sacrifice, and vouchsafe me to behold thy countenance with a pure conscience.

*Troparion:*

Despise not the supplication of the words of my prayer, O Good Sovereign Lady, Mother of the Merciful God Who shone forth from thy womb: but grant me divine remission before the end.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

I have bowed down to passions, prodigal and utterly wretched as I am, and have departed far from God; entreat Him to save me, [f. 153rA] O Good One; for I have fled unto thee, and adorn me with the former garments.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Thy Son Jesus came forth from thee in the flesh without consuming the fire of thy womb: entreat Him, O Pure One, to deliver from the fire of eternal torment those who praise thee in faith.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

When the Pure and glorious Virgin, the glory of Angels, the only Blessed One, stood by the Cross of her Son and God, unable to endure the malice of enemies, she cried aloud weeping with sighings: How does the Lover of mankind endure all these things brought upon Him?[^160]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Children in Babylon feared not the flame of the furnace, but cast into the midst of the fire and refreshed with dew, they cried: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers![^161]

*Troparion:*

Arise, O my soul, from the evils which thou hast committed: why sleepest thou further? Why liest thou prostrate in the sleep of despondency? Cry aloud unto the Theotokos: Most Holy One, help me!

*Troparion:*

Beholding the honor of thine image, I dare not gaze upon thee, O Most Pure One: praying to receive forgiveness of debts, and purely to look upon thy most pure Icon.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Unto thy protection do I flee, O Mother of God, and fall down to receive forgiveness: despise me not, [f. 153vA] O Sovereign Lady, but take pity and save me.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

With manifold evils of the soul-corrupter have I defiled my soul and mind: I pray thee, O Most Pure One, look down, and snatch thy servant from his temptations.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Trained in the ancestral laws, the all-blessed youths in Babylon, braving danger, disregarded the senseless decree of the ruler; and united together in the fire wherein they were not consumed, they cried unto the Almighty a worthy song: Praise the Lord, all ye works, and exalt Him above all unto all ages![^162]

*Troparion:*

Measurelessly have I sinned, and defiled my body in sin, senseless that I am: I contemplate the day of unerring judgment, and in perplexity I fear, and am terrified and tremble. Therefore do I fall down before thy tender-heartedness, O Sovereign Lady: cast me not away, but see my affliction, and deliver me from every condemnation and manifold torments.

*Troparion:*

Having despised the divine law, I lie wholly in slothfulness, wretched as I am: look upon me, O Most Pure One, raise me up quickly, and make me to receive the portion of the saved, that rejoicing I may cry unto thee: Rejoice, Joy of the world, only Protectress of those who call in faith upon thy firm protection.

*Glory:*

[f. 154rA] *Troparion:*

Woe is me, O my soul! How shall we stand then in fear and trembling before the fearful judgment seat, when the Angels shall stand in fear and trembling! Having the Theotokos as a good protectress, bend thy knees and lift up thy hands on high, crying: Look down and spare me then with thy prayerful gaze, O Virgin.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Show forth now the unashamed strength of thy protection: for as One indebted, thy Son is inclined by maternal prayers, O Virgin: anticipate then now in this urgent hour, that I may not depart unprepared, but may live yet longer, O All-hymned Theotokos Pure.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Lament Me not, O Mother, beholding in the tomb the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the womb without seed: for I shall arise and be glorified, and as God I shall unceasingly exalt in glory those who magnify thee in faith and love.[^163]

*Troparion:*

That I may obtain heavenly glory, O Virgin, I pray thee: forgive me, and deliver me from the debts of darkness, from all transgressions, O Most Pure One, committed in ignorance and in knowledge, both by day and by night: that I may offer unto thee the thanksgiving song: Rejoice!

*Troparion:*

From great afflictions snatch me, O Maiden, and establish my feet upon the rock of divine [f. 154vA] salvation: for having acquired thee now as an invincible protectress, I hope to pass through the wall and barrier which I built by disobedience regarding the tree.[^164]

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

I pray thee who didst give birth to the merciful Judge and Master: receive the boldness of defiled lips, and my poor singing, and loathe me not, who am more sinful than all men: after God I, thy servant, have thee as protectress.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Being higher than all creations, O Theotokos, thou didst hold in thy womb God incarnate: fervently entreat Him, O Pure One, to release thy servant from every tribulation, that with free voice I also may glorify thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Tailpiece vignette depicting Christ in glory / angelic vision)*

[f. 155rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece depicting the Mother of God and Christ Child medallion surrounded by floral scrollwork)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 6)

*On Friday at Small Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

He was a helper and a protector unto me for salvation; He is my God, and I will glorify Him, the God of my father, and I will exalt Him: for He is gloriously glorified.[^165]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Cast not away, O Good One, loathe me not, despise me not, who have fervently fled beneath thy tender mercy, O Pure One: but grant me to be fed with the grace that is in thee.

*Troparion:*

O Good Theotokos, Protectress of the sorrowful, receive my sighing from the soul, and deliver me from all the measureless evils which I have grievously committed.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Unto thee do I fall down, O Good One, sorrowful Protectress: deliver me from the everlasting fire, and darkness, and the abyss, me who have lived wickedly throughout my whole life.

*Both now:*

[f. 155vA] *Theotokion:*

Woe is me, how shall I entreat Thee, O Redeemer, my Jesus, having measurelessly sinned against Thee! Therefore do I offer unto Thee as intercessor her who bore Thee, the Pure One: have mercy and save me.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Establish, O Lord, upon the rock of Thy commandments my shaken heart, for Thou alone art Holy and Righteous.[^166]

*Troparion:*

I fall down before thee, O Mother of the Word; receive me through thy tender mercies: and grant forgiveness of transgressions unto me who ask, through thy fervent prayers.

*Troparion:*

Have mercy on me, O Sovereign Lady, have mercy, and deliver me from every malice, and demonic affliction, and eternal torment.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Subdue, O Sovereign Lady, the bitter drunkenness of my pleasures, granting me the divine sobriety of repentance and saving conversion.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Having ineffably given birth to the Lord and Sustainer of all, O Mother of God, entreat Him: that He may save thy flock.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

The Prophet heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, that Thou wast about to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard the report of Thee and was afraid: glory to Thy power, O Lord![^167]

[f. 156rA] *Troparion:*

The wounds of my soul I unceasingly scratch with voluptuous pleasures, and pains have been added unto my sores, and I remain insensible and incurable. Have mercy, O Mother of God, and heal, and save me through whatever prayers thou knowest.

*Troparion:*

The darkness of sin has covered my soul, O Mother of God: thus I walk in light as in the night, ignorant of Christ's commandments. O thou who didst give birth to the Divine Light, have mercy on me, and enlighten me, I pray.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

The Word of the living God descended into thy womb, O Virgin Mother, and taking from thy most pure blood all my compound nature, He came forth twofold in one hypostasis: entreat Him to save our souls.[^168]

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

I have diligently pursued bodily desires and all pleasures, and have become utterly corrupt, defiled, and abominable; O Most Pure Mother of God, have mercy, and save me by thy tender mercy.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Waking early from the night, O Lover of mankind, enlighten me, I pray, and guide me in Thy commandments, and teach me, O Savior, to do Thy will.[^169]

*Troparion:*

I have kindled for myself the fire of Gehenna by unbecoming deeds, and have grievously drawn down the wrath of God: help me, O Pure One, and forsake me not.

[f. 156vA] I continually ask for remission of transgressions, yet unceasingly commit most lawless things, and embitter thee, O Most Pure One: O Cleansing of the faithful, take pity on me.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Him Whom thou didst bear of thy blood, O Pure One, perfect Man and true God, Jesus: entreat Him that we may be delivered from eternal fire.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

O Gate impassable, I pray: open unto me the gates of true repentance, and show me the pathways unto salvation, O Pure One, the Directress of all.[^170]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
