I cried with my whole heart unto the merciful God, and He heard me from the lowest hell, and brought up my life from corruption.[^171]

*Troparion:*

The waves of unbecoming thoughts engulf me now: but do thou, O Sovereign Lady, guide me by thy tender mercy unto the calm haven of true repentance.

*Troparion:*

Having acquired thee as hope and unconquerable rampart, and firm protectress, O Maiden, we are delivered from transgressions, grievous passions, and every harm.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

I fall down before thee, O Pure Virgin, and cry with weeping, O Theotokos: deliver my wretched soul from the future judgment and the eternal fire.

*Both now:*

[f. 157rA] *Theotokion:*

Thou art strength, O Most Pure One, unto those weak in soul through thy prayers: therefore despise me not who am sick in soul, but heal me.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

Through the tender mercy of Thy compassions, O Christ, Thou didst descend upon earth, and being incarnate of the Virgin, Thou didst sanctify all: all those who are of earth Thou didst draw up unto Heaven. Therefore, hoping in Thee, we shall never err, but are delivered through Thee in this present age: for Thou art our Savior, O Creator, our God.[^172]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

We have sinned, we have transgressed, and have not been justified before Thee, neither have we kept nor done as Thou hast commanded us: yet betray us not utterly, O God of the fathers![^173]

*Troparion:*

When my wretched soul must endure separation from the body, and there shall be none to deliver or comfort it: stand by me then, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me from the malice of demons.

*Troparion:*

I fall down before thee, and offer fervent drops of tears: I know thy loving long-suffering and meekness: do thou have mercy on me now, O Pure One, forgive and save me.

*Glory:*

[f. 157vA] *Troparion:*

Have mercy, O Virgin, on my estranged and wretched soul; see also the unstable burning of my flesh, and grant me saving help and deliverance.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

The Son and Word, co-unoriginate and consubstantial with the Father, took from thee our flesh immutable, endowed with mind and animated by a soul, as He Himself knows, and in it renewed our nature unto what is better, O Pure Virgin.[^174]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify, and before Whom the Cherubim and Seraphim tremble: let every breath and creation sing, bless, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^175]

*Troparion:*

I am at a loss contemplating my deeds, and tremble before the Judge of the fearful judgment seat: what answer shall I, the wretched one, bring then! O Sovereign Lady of the world, be thou my Protectress.

*Troparion:*

Turn me not away, O Sovereign Lady, from thy face when I look upon thine image; but be merciful unto me, and deliver me from the condemnation that is at hand.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

O Bride of God, Mary unwedded, deliver me, thy servant, from every malicious harm, I cry unto thee: and in the age to come stand by me, only Protectress of Christians.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

To show me, being perfectly cleansed, a partaker of His divine Kingdom, that He might deify mankind, thy Son, O Sovereign Lady, appeared from thee as perfect Man:

[f. 158rA] Him do thou entreat, O Pure One.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

O thou who didst receive the Joy of the Angels and didst give birth to thy Creator: O Virgin, save those who magnify thee.[^176]

*Troparion:*

Thou alone art the Protectress of men, O Most Pure One, thou art the rampart of Christians, O Pure One; thee do I now set forth as Mediatress before Christ for me the lowly one: that through thy prayers He may have mercy on me, the wretched one.

*Troparion:*

Nightly works have I committed, and the night of torment is about righteously to cover me who am stripped of light, and to receive every torment of hades: but O Pure Virgin, who didst give birth to the Judge and God, deliver me from every torment.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

The time of my life have I spent in evil deeds, and have drawn near to the gates of hades, and go thither utterly unprepared: help me, O Good Theotokos, for in thee have I placed my hope.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

O divinely magnificent Sovereign Lady, Adornment of Angels, Glory of Martyrs: pray together with them that we may find mercy and remission of debts, and that all who perform divine works may be enabled well to finish the course of our life.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 158vA] *(Woodcut: Full-page engraving of the Most Holy Theotokos standing at full length upon clouds, holding the pre-eternal Christ Child, with the titles "MP ΘV" and "IC XC". Inscribed at the top in red cinnabar: "Rejoice, thou who didst bear Him Who bears all things!" At the foot, a four-line devotional poem:)*[^177]

Even as here, O Virgin, thou standest together with thy Son,

So also in Heaven near Him thou sittest upon the Throne:

Grant in Thy mercy that we all may stand near thee in Heaven,

Together with thy Son everlastingly to glorify thee.

[f. 159rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece depicting the medallion of the Mother of God and Christ Child surrounded by acanthus scrollwork)*

---

# TONE 7

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Saturday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

Unto Him Who shattered wars by His arm, and engulfed the captains and horsemen: let us sing unto Him, as unto our Redeemer and God, for He is glorified![^178]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

As an adornment of song, and a hymn worthy of God, the gifts of thanksgiving do we offer unto thee, crying: Rejoice! now, O Pure One: for from sorrow thou hast bestowed joy upon us.

*Troparion:*

The grace of thy mercy, and the strength of thy protection, we shall not pass over in silence, O Most Pure Virgin, but proclaim it now: that from grievous perils thou hast saved us.

*Glory:*

[f. 159vA] *Troparion:*

...being delivered from diverse temptations and sorrows by thy maternal prayers, O Most Pure One: harmoniously and fervently singing all voices of thanksgiving unto thee, we cry aloud a song of victory.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Unbrokenly adorned with diverse virtues as with golden ornaments and the graces of the Spirit, O Pure One, as the Bride of the Father, thou didst truly appear as the Mother of God.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

O Thou Who didst establish the Heavens by Thy word, and founded the base of the earth upon many waters: establish my mind unto Thy will, O only Lover of mankind.[^179]

*Troparion:*

The majesty of divine joy, as our rejoicing from the beginning, do we offer with voices of thanksgiving, fervently honoring thee as our Protectress.

*Troparion:*

Being delivered from afflictions through thee, and having obtained joy for thy sake, O Unwedded Mother: we all glorify thee as a good bestower and all-praised protectress.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Receiving relief from transgressions and trials by thy divine prayer, O Mother of Christ God, we faithfully sing of Thee with voices of thanksgiving as the cause of all good things.

*Both now:*

[f. 160rA] *Theotokion:*

She who is the spring of immortal joy continually pours forth streams of gladness and herself saves all: as our ever-living benefaction, the Mother of Christ God.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

The virtue of Thine ineffable wisdom, O Christ God, covered the Heavens by Thy dispensation, O Lover of mankind.[^180]

*Troparion:*

A worthy song, Rejoice, O Pure Virgin, do we offer rejoicing, having been delivered from afflictions by thy prayers.

*Troparion:*

Gratefully unto thee, O Pure Virgin, with the hands of the soul, we offer singing, leaping with divine songs, having been delivered from many sorrows.

*Glory:*

*Troparion:*

Many troubles and passionate sins have risen up against us: but by thy divine protection, O Pure One, snatch us away.

*Both now:*

*Theotokion:*

Blessed truly are they that honor thee, O Most Pure Theotokos: for through thee have we been delivered from sin and sorrow.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Thou Who didst drive away the night of passions, cause the light of understanding to shine upon me: O Thou Who didst dispel the primordial darkness of the deep, bringing forth the first-created light unto the world, O Creator of all.[^181]

*Troparion:*

Thou didst bear Him Who takes away the sins of the faithful, O Virgin, through Whom the world was saved from trials and sicknesses. Therefore [f. 160vA] we also cry unto thee: Rejoice, having been delivered from sorrows.

*Troparion:*

Encompassed by diverse temptations, O Pure Sovereign Lady, by sorrow and affliction and grievous distress, and bereft of gladness, I have found thee as my hope. Glory:

*Troparion:*

As a treasury of salvation for us, thy faithful servants, O Pure One, thou drivest away temptations, and keepest us unharmed. Therefore, partakers of thy many good things, we thank thee with songs. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

We thank thee, having been delivered through thee from many sins, and weaknesses, and sicknesses, and grievous diseases, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady: for thou art a firm hope unto thy faithful servants.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I fell into the depth of transgressions, O Good One; as Jonah from the whale, I cry unto Thee: Bring up my life from corruption, and save me, O Lover of mankind.[^182]

*Troparion:*

Angelic tongues cannot worthily sing thy praises, O Pure One; yet we now, undertaking it as servants, offer unto thee Gabriel's Rejoice!

*Troparion:*

From the depth of sorrow and distress into which we fell through our sins, we have been delivered through thee from want and temptations, O Virgin Theotokos Pure. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thou hast made the whole world, O Pure One, rightly to give thanks, and praise, and glorify thy grace with piety:

[f. 161rA] for through thee have we been delivered from troubles and sorrows. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

By night and by day, openly and secretly, we flee beneath thy protection, O Most Pure Maiden, faithfully glorifying thee.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

O Lord, we are Thy people, and the sheep of Thy pasture: as Shepherd, turn us back who have strayed; gather us who are scattered by corruption; have mercy on Thy flock, O Lover of mankind; have compassion on Thy people, through the prayers of the Theotokos, O only Sinless One.[^183]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Cast into the fiery furnace, the holy youths changed the fire into dew, and singing thus they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers![^184]

*Troparion:*

A thanksgiving rejoicing do we offer unto thee, O Mother of God: for truly delivered through thee from every evil undertaking, we harmoniously sing unto thee: Blessed art thou!

*Troparion:*

In the evening weeping lodged with us amid sorrows and expectation of evils: but through thy glorious protection, O Virgin, being deified, we found joy in the morning; for thou hast saved us.[^185] Glory:

[f. 161vA] *Troparion:*

Having all acquired thy Protection before God as a divine refuge, in trials and persecutions and sins we flee unto thee, and through thee receive relief, O Most Pure One. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The grace of thy prayer, O Pure One, we proclaim with lips and spirit, O glorious Lady: for for thy sake are we all delivered from temptations and storm and heavy sorrows, and from the passion of sin.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who is fearful unto the Cherubim, and marvelous unto the Seraphim, and Creator of the world: ye priests, servants, and spirits of the righteous, praise the Lord![^186]

*Troparion:*

Having been delivered from the storm of sin, and from passions and trials by thy prayers, O Good Theotokos, with thanksgiving voice we cry unto thee: Rejoice! as having passed through thee from sorrow into joy.

*Troparion:*

Despise not those held fast in sickness and temptations, O Good One: but hearing our poor supplication, liberate us from great afflictions, that we may faithfully sing of thy prayer, O Pure One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Cause of the remission of transgressions, raise us up now from sorrows, trials, and passions of men, and unseemly temptation; and through thy divine supplications, O Theotokos, deliver us from them most gloriously. Both now:

[f. 162rA] *Theotokion:*

Thy compassions are ever sent down upon all, O Christ, truly by the grace and supplications of her who bore Thee: for from Thee do we Christians receive Thy mercy, O Compassionate Savior.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

A Mother above custom, and a Virgin beyond custom, the only blessed among women: with hymns, ye faithful, let us worthily magnify her.[^187]

*Troparion:*

A rejoicing according to worth, with a voice of acclamation, do we offer unto thee now, O Virgin, with the Angel Gabriel, having been delivered from diverse temptations by thy prayers, O Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

Joy and gladness and divine rejoicing, O Virgin who knewest not man, God's own joy hath multiplied for us: for behold, we who were weeping bitterly now rejoice through thy prayers. Glory:

*Troparion:*

I will sacrifice a sacrifice of praise, and with tongue and voice fervently render unto thee a song of thanksgiving, O Virgin: so that praying unto thee in the day of sorrow, I am delivered through thee. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

We rejoice together with thy divine Nativity with piety, O Most Pure One: for it poured forth joy unto us from trials and sorrows. Therefore do we also thankfully offer a song unto thee, faithfully singing thy praise.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 162vA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece depicting the Nativity of the Most Holy Theotokos, with Saint Anne reclining and maidservants washing the newborn Virgin)*

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Sunday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

At Thy nod, the nature of water, which before was poured forth, was changed into an earthly form, O Lord; therefore, traversing dryshod, Israel sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^188]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

The ranks of the Angels truly honor thee as Mother of the God of all and their strength, O Most Pure One: but even now from my clay lips, receive a song of supplication.

*Troparion:*

Put to death the malice of my flesh which is still alive, O Most Pure One who didst give birth to the true Life, and unto thy servant grant deliverance from passions and transgressions, O Pure One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The timeless Word, Sun from Sun, which shone forth incomprehensibly, shone forth in time: by His rays, O Virgin, enlighten my soul and give it understanding. Both now:

[f. 163rA] *Theotokion:*

A word of praise ever operative is fitting for thee, O Theotokos: yet we pray thee, O Pure One, save those who flee unto thee from every worldly sorrow.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

In the beginning, O Lord Savior, Who didst establish the Heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all their host by the all-creating and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession.[^189]

*Troparion:*

Paying my debts, as befits a servant I have acknowledged thy graces, O Sovereign Lady, and fervently sing of thee, O Most Pure Mother of the Word and Redeemer of all: but even now deliver me from worldly distresses.

*Troparion:*

Adam who was slain of old by transgression, hast thou visibly accounted worthy of a better life, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth to the Life-originating Word: but even now liberate me from mortal sin. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The law of passions has grown hardened in my flesh, and now grieves the spiritual law, and unites my mind unto most grievous lusts, O Most Pure One: but grant me swiftly the peace of dispassion through thy prayers. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Ineffably having conceived in thy womb Christ, the Sustainer of all, O Most Pure One, [f. 163vA] thou hast loosed the substance of the earthborn from the gates of hades: but even now tear asunder the much-entangled bonds that corrupt me.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Not leaving the Father's Heavens, Thou didst descend upon the earth, O Christ God; I heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^190]

*Troparion:*

By night I wrestle with the darkness of grievous things, having darkened my mind; O Gate of Light, illuminate me by thy rays, and deliver me swiftly from bitterness and sorrow.

*Troparion:*

Constrained by a multitude of sins, and greatly bound by the cords of temptations, I call upon thy help now, O Sovereign Lady, that I may be delivered from every torment. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Destroy the counsels of the gatherings of evil, fierce, envious, and crafty men, O All-immaculate One: and deliver me from every sorrow of this life, and affliction. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Cut down with the sword of thy prayers the turnaround of deceitful words and workings against me, O Sovereign Lady, and destroy them swiftly, and deliver me from every sorrow.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Night is without light, O Christ, unto unbelievers, but unto the faithful there is enlightenment in the sweetness of Thy words: therefore early do I rise unto Thee, and sing of Thy Divinity.[^191]

[f. 164rA] *Troparion:*

Being an invincible Wall unto the faithful, be a protection unto thy servant, O Virgin, and deliver me from every dire sorrow and affliction.

*Troparion:*

I am exceedingly sick, grievously afflicted on both sides, O Most Pure One: heal by thy prayers the weakness of soul and body which I have undergone. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Into the deep of thy compassions, O Virgin, I, thy servant, flee now, that I may be delivered from the hostile counsel holding me fast, and from affliction. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Exalted is the glory of thy prayers everywhere, O Virgin Mother Most Pure: through them deliver me now from every assault of this life.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Sailing upon the storm of life's billows, sinking with the ship through sins, and cast unto the soul-corrupting beast: as Jonah, O Christ, I cry unto Thee: Bring me up from the death-bearing depth![^192]

*Troparion:*

The sayings and words of all faithfully praise thee, O Pure One, for the debt of all men was visibly loosed by thy Nativity. But even now receive the prayer of thy singers, and deliver us from temptations and sorrows.

*Troparion:*

Sick as I am, and pricked by the passionate and sinful thorns, seeking healing from thee, O Most Pure One: deliver me from every sickness and affliction, and save me from temptations and sorrows. Glory:

[f. 164vA] *Troparion:*

Having glorified the nature of the earthborn, thou didst lead it up to Heaven, O Theotokos, having become the Palace of the King of Glory. Therefore bring me up from the abyss of many transgressions, and temptations, and passions. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst give birth to the Word incarnate beyond mind and word, O Pure Virgin, Who delivered us from all irrationality. Therefore with divine words do we unceasingly sing of thee and faithfully glorify thee.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

O Sovereign Lady, thy Son is angered against thy sinful servants, for we have moved the Compassionate One unto wrath: but turn Him unto mercy, O Pure One; take compassion, O Mother of God, entreat the Tender-hearted One by thine unceasing supplications, and deliver the captives.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Cast into the fiery furnace, the holy youths changed the fire into dew, and singing thus they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers![^193]

*Troparion:*

Adorned with fringes of purity, O Most Pure Maiden: turn my soul now from defiled malice unto divine beauty, O Blessed Sovereign Lady, Protectress of our race.

*Troparion:*

Demonic malice holds me fast exceedingly now, and they kindle within me the fire of passions; but rather by thy rays burn them utterly by thy compassions, O Protectress of our race. Glory:

[f. 165rA] *Troparion:*

In praying unto thy Son for us, O Most Pure Mother of God, cease not at all by thy prayers to snatch thy servants from the trial of human sorrows. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The Transubstantial Son immutably took flesh from thee, O Virgin, and became Man, that He might save those who sing: Blessed, O Most Pure One, is the Fruit of thy womb!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Refreshed in the furnace, the youths cry out: Let all creation bless the Word Who created all things, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^194]

*Troparion:*

Passionate pastures and manifold troubles truly encompass thy servant, O Sovereign Lady: but deliver me from every temptation thereof.

*Troparion:*

May thy mighty hand come upon us now, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver us from the expected human afflictions. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Break the snares of crafty and evil-counseling men, O Pure Birthgiver of God, and deliver thy servant from every dire necessity. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O only divine haven existing for the faithful, O Virgin Pure: I pray thee, deliver me who am in the sea of life from the bitter storm.

[f. 165vA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Rejoice, radiant Bride, Mother of the Giver of Light; Rejoice, thou who didst contain the Incontainable in thy womb; Rejoice, thou who art more honorable than the Cherubim themselves, having given birth to the Savior of our souls.[^195]

*Troparion:*

I have pranced like an unbroken steed away from the Master's commandments; by the bridles of thy prayers, O Most Pure One, may I be restrained unto the path of swift ordering.

*Troparion:*

I have been dragged down exceedingly by senseless stumbling unto the working of my crafty sin: deliver me, O Theotokos, from a worse change. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O thou who didst appear as the first healing of human nature, heal the crushing of Adam and Eve: heal me also, O Theotokos, through thy supplications. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Grant me a chaste mind, a pure heart, and a vigilant understanding, that I may keep, O Sovereign Lady, the most pure commandments of my God.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Vignette depicting the Virgin and Child standing upon the crescent moon under stars, surrounded by stylized typographical floral crosses)*

[f. 166rA] *(Woodcut: Decorative headpiece depicting a winged cherub head surrounded by floral branches)*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Monday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

Unto Him Who shattered wars by His arm, and engulfed the captains and horsemen: let us sing unto Him, as unto our Redeemer and God, for He is glorified![^196]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thee, the cause of our salvation and divine redemption, do we entreat, O Most Pure One: pray that we may be saved.

*Troparion:*

I am wholly in grievous temptations, in troubles and transgressions; I cry unto thee, O Most Pure One: save me, thy servant. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thee, the pure Virgin and Mother of the Creator, do we entreat: deliver us, the faithful, from every affliction. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O thou who beyond word didst give birth in the flesh to the Word, Christ our God, the Author of life: entreat Him that we may be saved.

[f. 166vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

The Church of Christ is established in faith, which unceasingly with hymns sings, crying: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls![^197]

*Troparion:*

Having walked the path of all-ruinous malice, I found not the way of my salvation: guide me unto it, O All-immaculate Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

My strength and establishment, and help, and only hope do I inscribe thee, O All-immaculate One: be thou my Protectress in the day of my departure. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Ever praying unto thee, O All-immaculate One: stand by me at the hour of my death, and deliver thy servant from torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Incomprehensibly born of the Virgin, our Savior appeared as He willed, and Thou hast renewed the world, O Lord.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard the report of Thee and was afraid; I understood Thy works and was amazed, O Lord.[^198]

*Troparion:*

Having transgressed the commandments of my Master, I have appeared an easy prey unto enemies: deliver me, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

At the hour of death deliver and save my passionate soul from the demons, O Most Pure One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Loose the bonds of my transgressions, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, and be Mediatress unto eternal life for me through thy prayers. Both now:

[f. 167rA] *Theotokion:*

We sing of thee as Virgin even after childbearing, and glorify thee, O Most Pure One, as Mother of our God.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Rising early unto Thy glory and praise, O Christ, we ever praise the sign of Thy Cross, which Thou hast given us as a weapon of help.[^199]

*Troparion:*

Haven unto those grievously tossed in the storm, Gate of salvation unto those saved through faith: O Good Virgin, save me, thy servant.

*Troparion:*

Show forth thy manifold compassions richly upon me, thy servant, O Virgin Theotokos, delivering me from the fearful future judgment. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The unstable night of passions holds fast my soul and sends it into the ruinous abyss; with the light of thy prayer, O Theotokos, illuminate and save me, thy servant. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Virgin Full of Grace, who in time beyond word didst give birth to the Word: entreat Him to save our souls.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Jonah cried from the belly of hades: Liberate my life from corruption! And we cry unto Thee: O Almighty Savior, have mercy on us![^200]

*Troparion:*

In temptations we ever have thee as a firm protectress, in afflictions strong salvation, rest in sorrow, and help in troubles, O Virgin.

[f. 167vA] *Troparion:*

Appear unto me as guardian in this life, O Most Pure One; deliver me from demons at the hour of death, and give me rest after death. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thou art the beauty of the honorable Holy Angels, the rejoicing of all men, O Theotokos Virgin: guide me unto life. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Heal my wretched soul, O All-immaculate One, which is incurably sick with the deceits of this life and the closing of many doors.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

In the tender compassion of Thy mercy, O Christ God, Thou wast pleased to become incarnate of the Holy Virgin: for her sake preserve our life, as Lover of mankind.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Thou didst cool the burning furnace, O Savior, and saved the youths who cried aloud and said: Blessed art Thou unto ages, O Lord God of our fathers![^201]

*Troparion:*

Calling to mind the multitude of my transgressions, I fall into despair, O All-immaculate Virgin. Therefore do I cry unto thee: Help me, that I may not perish utterly.

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be the Mother of Life, O Pure One, I cry unto thee: Deliver me from the death of the soul, and account me worthy of eternal life. Glory:

[f. 168rA] *Troparion:*

Deliver from troubles, passions, sicknesses, affliction, and the sins of this life, and from the unquenchable eternal fire, those who faithfully honor thee with hymns, O All-immaculate One. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Every tongue glorifies thee, O Pure One, who art the glory and praise of our race, and Guide of the strayed, O all-hymned Theotokos.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who is fearful unto the Cherubim, and marvelous unto the Seraphim, and Creator of the world: ye priests, servants, and spirits of the righteous, praise the Lord![^202]

*Troparion:*

Behold, I, most wretched one, have spent my life in slothfulness: and now I am in perplexity and have drawn near unto the end; help me, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

Refuge of sinners, uprighting of the fallen art thou, O Sovereign Lady: therefore do I flee unto thy protection; save me. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Possessing unslumbering prayer, O All-immaculate Sovereign Lady of the world, deliver from the future judgment those who in faith venerate thine icon. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Every tongue sings of thee, glorifying thee: for thou, O Virgin Bride of God, didst give birth to the All-hymned God; entreat Him unceasingly to save the souls of those who sing of thee.

[f. 168vA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

A Mother above nature, and a Virgin in nature, the only blessed among women: with divinely inspired hymns, ye faithful, let us magnify her.[^203]

*Troparion:*

All who flee in faith unto thy tender mercy are delivered from the sorrowful troubles of this life. Therefore I also have fled unto thy protection, O Theotokos.

*Troparion:*

Mighty Protectress, invincible wall unto the sorrowful, O Pure One: save me from the passions of sins, and from the everlasting fire. Glory:

*Troparion:*

With the radiant beams of the Word Who shone forth from thee, O Pure Virgin, illuminate me and save me as thou art Good, and snatch me from torments. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou bearest in thy holy hands Him Who bears all things: entreat Him, O Pure One, to save us who are not held fast by the adversary's wickedness.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Vignette depicting Christ the Great High Priest blessing from clouds, flanked by the Mother of God and Saint John the Baptist in intercession, surrounded by typographical floral crosses)*

[f. 169rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate three-medallion headpiece depicting the Mother of God, Christ Emmanuel, and Saint John the Baptist surrounded by strapwork and acanthus)*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Tuesday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

At Thy nod, the nature of water, which before was poured forth, was changed into an earthly form, O Lord; traversing wholly dryshod, Israel sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^204]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Still the storm of my pleasures and passions by the calm of thy divine supplication, O All-immaculate One, that with a tranquil heart I may glorify thine ineffable Childbearing.

*Troparion:*

Behold, thy Childbearing became the desire and sweetness of salvation, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos: drive away from thy servant every bitterness arising from the passions of transgressions. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Most Pure One, from demonic deception, and affliction, and harm, and temptation: that in faith I may glorify thee, who after God art my protectress and protection. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst appear unto Moses at the bush, O Word of God, as a fire that burns and yet consumes not at all:

[f. 169vA] prefiguring Thy Childbearing of the Virgin, for whose sake Thou didst fashion man.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

In the beginning, O Lord Savior, Who didst establish the Heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all their host by the all-creating and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession.[^205]

*Troparion:*

Entreat fervently the King Who was lifted up upon the Cross and cast down hades, on behalf of those who ever faithfully sing of thee with sacred voices and hymns, O Hope of the world and Defense of the faithful.

*Troparion:*

Higher than the Heavens, and more glorious than the Cherubim, having conceived thou didst give birth to God: therefore entreat Him to deliver me from transgressions and fearful condemnation, by thy merciful supplication and thy power beyond word. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O thou who through thy Childbearing hast changed men from the corruption of death, O Sovereign Lady: deliver me from the corruption of passions and sicknesses, and grievous transgressions, and grant me unaging life through thy divine intercession. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Immutably made like unto men in all things, O Word of God, Thou didst visibly come forth from the Pure Virgin, and unto all didst reveal the all-radiant Godhead, immutable in hypostases in one undifferentiated Essence.[^206]

[f. 170rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Not leaving the Father's Heavens, Thou didst descend upon the earth, O Christ God; I heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^207]

*Troparion:*

Stretch forth thy mercy and compassion upon thy servants, O Pure One, who ever pray unto thee, delivering them from fearful torment through thy prayers.

*Troparion:*

With thy pure prayer and fervent faith I flee unto thee, O All-immaculate One: be thou the cleansing-place of my many transgressions, delivering and saving me. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Mediatress of our joy hast thou appeared by thy Childbearing, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady: entreat Him, O All-immaculate One, to deliver me from temptations, and to account me worthy of eternal life. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Not parting from the Father's Bosom, yet incarnate of the Virgin, O Christ God: preserve the flock of Thy dispensation, which worships in godly manner.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Night is without light, O Christ, unto unbelievers, but unto the faithful there is enlightenment in the sweetness of Thy words: therefore early do I rise unto Thee, and sing of Thy Divinity.[^208]

*Troparion:*

I pray thee, O Pure Virgin, deliver my soul from dark sin, and illuminate it with divine [f. 170vA] beam of repentance, through the mercy of thy supplication.

*Troparion:*

Being in the cruel abyss of tribulations and oppressive passions, O Most Pure Consolation: by thy prayers and protection deliver and save those who praise thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having conceived God in a virginal womb and given birth, O Most Pure Theotokos: deliver those who sing of thee from all eternal condemnation. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Knowing thee to be the Mother of God, O Pure One, and our Protectress: we present thee as the Mediatress of our salvation unto the bountiful Lord.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I cried unto the Lord in my affliction, and unto Thee, and Thou hast brought up my life from corruption, O Most Merciful One.[^209]

*Troparion:*

O Sovereign Lady, implore thy Son Who was lifted up upon the Cross, and delivered men from corruption, that we may be saved.

*Troparion:*

O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, forsake me not who trust in thee, but make haste with thy help. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Deliver from sorrow those who call upon thy Holy Name, and set them free from fearful torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, firm hope: preserve them who in love sing of thee as the true Theotokos.

*Lord, have mercy (3). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

[f. 171rA] Unceasingly pray Christ God, Who was crucified for our sake and cast down the power of death, O Virgin Theotokos: that He may save our souls.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Of old the children showed the burning furnace to be cooling, singing unto the one God and saying: O God of our fathers, Who art supremely exalted, blessed art Thou.[^210]

*Troparion:*

Being in perplexity and sorrow, and in tribulations, I call upon thee from my heart, O All-holy One: anticipate, deliver me, and grant me consolation, O Pure One.

*Troparion:*

The tempest-tossings of many transgressions and passions assail me, and bring me down unto the depth of perdition: give me thy hand, O Theotokos, and save and have mercy on me by thy power.

*Troparion:*

I bring Thy Mother unto Thee in prayer, O Savior, Lover of mankind: receiving her, deliver me from transgressions and future judgment, and snatch me from eternal torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Having acquired a soul deadened by cruel transgressions: I pray thee, O Sovereign Lady, who by thy childbearing didst deaden hades, revive me through the patterns of repentance.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

God Who knows secret things, and Christ, the Cause of all beings, we exalt and glorify unto ages.[^211]

[f. 171vA] *Troparion:*

Save me, O Most Pure One, from tribulations, and corruption, and passions: for unto thee do I flee, my hope and divine salvation.

*Troparion:*

In the final hour, O Sovereign Lady Virgin, snatch me away from cruel accusers, and guide me thyself unto the divine dwelling-place. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Arise, O soul, and falling down cry aloud: O Hope and Salvation of all, deliver me from the eternal fire. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Let us sing of the only blessed Virgin among women, exalting and glorifying Christ unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Mother of God and Virgin, who givest birth and yet remainest virgin again, not a work of nature, but of God's condescension: therefore as the only one accounted worthy of divine marvels, we ever magnify thee.[^212]

*Troparion:*

The Queen offers Thee supplication on my behalf, O Master Christ: receiving her, deliver me from every torment.

*Troparion:*

I ever pray thee, O Sovereign Lady: deliver my humble soul from future judgment and torment. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Pray my Creator and Fashioner, O Virgin, to deliver me from fearful judgment, and to grant me life. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O All-immaculate Mother, Pure Virgin: deliver thy flock that magnifies thee from spiritual defilements.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 172rA] *(Woodcut: Vignette with Christogram IC XC medallions flanking the Cross and Instruments of the Passion surrounded by floral ornament)*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Wednesday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

At Thy nod, the watery nature, which before was poured forth, was changed into an earthly form, O Lord; traversing wholly dryshod, Israel sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^213]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

With life-flowing waters, O Virgin, water my soul withered by sinful heat: that I may bring forth a fruitful ear of divine compunction, O Pure One.

*Troparion:*

Bestow upon me, O Pure One, drops of compunction through thy bountiful drops: wherewith I may wash away, O Maiden, all spiritual defilement that has come upon it from negligence. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having received thy Creator in thy womb, without corruption thou didst give birth to the Infant, our God. A strange wonder, O Virgin! Thou art Mother and co-eternal Sovereign Lady. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast given birth for us unto the Deliverer, the Liberator, and the Savior Christ for the human race, O Pure Virgin Mother. Therefore knowing thee as the Mediatress of eternal life, we glorify thee.

[f. 172vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

In the beginning, O Lord Savior, Who didst establish the Heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all their host by the all-creating and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession.[^214]

*Troparion:*

Having dyed a woven purple robe from thy most pure blood, the King of all, and having worn it without corruption in His nature, God and man, came forth from thee, O Virgin, possessing an immutable Kingdom.

*Troparion:*

As a treasury of good things, and an ineffable gift given unto all by God, O Virgin, thou providest us bountifully with the marvelous gifts of grace in tribulations, and distresses, and infirmities, who pray unto thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Marvelous Daniel foresaw thee as a Mountain, O Pure One: from which Christ was cut off as a Stone, and crushed the demonic idols.[^215] Therefore as the cause of all good things, we who are delivered hymn thy Childbearing. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The divinely speaking one once foresaw thee as a Scroll, in which the Word was inscribed, O Pure One, by the Father's finger.[^216] Therefore I pray: having torn in pieces the wicked record of my sins, enroll me in the Book of Life.

[f. 173rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Not leaving the Father's bosom, Thou didst descend upon the earth, O Christ God; I heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^217]

*Troparion:*

Make supplication unto Him Who was born of thee, O Theotokos: that I may find help in time of distress, who glorify His sufferings.

*Troparion:*

The myrrh that poured forth from thee, O All-holy Pure One, the Creator of nature, filled all things with fragrance by divine understandings, and drove away the foul-smelling deception. Glory:

*Troparion:*

As an intellectual Ladder set up for those upon earth, O Pure One, Christ God through thee leads the faithful up unto heavenly divine deeds, as He is merciful.[^218] Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Weighed down by a great burden of sin, O Good One, I cry unto thee: appear as my cleansing, O thou who didst give birth to God, Who takes away the sins of the world.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

O Lord my God, from the night I rise early unto Thee, praying: grant me remission of my transgressions, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^219]

[f. 173vA] *Troparion:*

Mother of the Lover of mankind and God, O all-praised Theotokos: send down upon me cleansing of transgressions through thy prayers, who ever praise thee in faith and love with hymns.

*Troparion:*

O Most Pure Theotokos, who art the receptacle of the immutable Light, and the temple of the divine radiance: illuminate my darkened soul with thy light through prayer. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Lord my God, Who didst deign to become man, I pray Thee: be pleased to save me, the prodigal, for the sake of the Virgin who purely gave birth unto Thee, the All-holy Maiden. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Sovereign Lady Mother of God, grant pardon of my transgressions: who didst give birth unto the subsistent Life for the world, O All-immaculate One.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

As I sail in the tempest of worldly surgings, sinking with the ship through sins, cast forth unto the soul-destroying beast, like Jonah I cry unto Thee, O Christ: bring me up from the deadly depth.[^220]

*Troparion:*

From corruption, death, and the sin that is from Adam, we were delivered by thy seedless childbearing, O Theotokos. For He is God and man together in one, and the same is Savior, bestowing deliverance from transgressions upon the faithful.

[f. 174rA] *Troparion:*

Tear up the handwriting of my transgressions, O Sovereign Lady, enrolling me in the Book of the Saved through thy divine prayers: that in praise I may sing of thee who art holier than the Cherubim. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Those who of old were subject by the judgment of Thy righteousness, O Christ, from Thy presence, Thou hast called by Thy bodily coming, as Thou art merciful. And now, O Good One, receive us who have sinned, and change us from evils. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Pure Virgin, who didst give birth unto the Cleansing and the Benefactor of sinners, I pray thee: dissolve the guilt of my immeasurable transgressions by thy maternal prayer; for as a Mother unto the Son thou hast power to do so.

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

All of us earthborn flee unto thy protection: deliver our souls from the eternal fire, O Good One.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Of old the children showed the burning furnace to be dew-bearing, singing unto the one God and saying: O God of our fathers, Who art supremely exalted, blessed art Thou.[^221]

*Troparion:*

Raise up my mind, weighed down by sleep, O All-immaculate One, unto good deeds: that I may bless thee, more honorable than all creation.

[f. 174vA] *Troparion:*

The three young children in the midst of the furnace prefigured thy childbearing; for they were not burnt, O Pure One, having given birth to Fire. Therefore burn up the material passions of my heart. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee to be fair and good among women, the All-comely Lord was incarnate of thee. Him therefore implore, O All-holy Maiden, to save me. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The divine Dew having come forth from thee, O All-immaculate One, bedews those who are withered by sinful heat: therefore I pray thee, bedew my withered soul.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The Bush on Sinai that shared in fire without being consumed revealed God unto Moses, slow of speech and stammering; and the divine zeal showed the three children to be uninjured by the fire, as singers: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^222]

*Troparion:*

The Bush on Sinai, unconsumed by fire, spoke with Moses the God-seer, showing forth thy strange childbearing.[^223] Therefore wondering at thy strange childbearing, O Pure One, rejoicing he cried: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

My mind is deadened by sin: do thou who didst give birth unto Life, O Pure Virgin, quicken and save me, [f. 175rA] changing me unto God by Thy maternal prayers, who ever sing: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having thee, O Pure One, by night and by day as an unyielding Guardian against intelligible and visible enemies, I am in no way terrified by evil and crafty assaults, singing unto Christ: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

From His love of mankind, Thy Son, O Theotokos, inclined as He is Good, gave thee as help against enemies and healing of passions unto those who cry aloud in faith: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Thou who without tasting corruption didst give birth, and didst lend flesh unto the Word, the all-craftsman, O Mother unwedded, Virgin Theotokos, Receptacle of the Uncontainable, Tabernacle of the Uncircumscribed Creator: thee do we magnify.[^224]

*Troparion:*

The unstealable wealth of thy virginity, O Mother, revealed the Ruler of all in the flesh, O Bride unwedded, Virgin Theotokos, who didst nurture Him as an infant, nourishing as Fashioner the whole world, and sanctifying it.

[f. 175vA] *Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Word made fully human, O Virgin, Who takes away the sins of the world: implore Him to send down upon us remission of sins, who bless thee as is due with unhesitating faith. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Being a lover of sin, I tremble and fear lest the end of life overtake me suddenly, O Pure Sovereign Lady, Protectress of all the wronged: be thou entreatable to strengthen me now through the patterns of repentance. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

That Thou send me not into the fire prepared for me, the Virgin who gave birth unto Thee, and the divine incorporeal choir, Apostles, Prophets, Martyrs, Hierarchs, and choirs of the Righteous pray Thee, O All-bountiful and Plenteously Merciful One.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion: after Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Vignette depicting the Mother of God standing in heavenly glory upon clouds surrounded by angel hosts with rays of divine light, flanked by typographical crosses)*

[f. 176rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece of strapwork and floral foliage)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Thursday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

Unto God Who shook Pharaoh in the Red Sea, let us sing a song of victory: for He is glorified.[^225]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Foreseeing the approaching end of my life, O Sovereign Lady, I cry aloud: grant me cleansing of transgressions, O Bride of God.

*Troparion:*

Having run unto the abyss of thy goodness, I cry aloud: O Good Sovereign Lady, deliver me from all torment. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Sovereign Lady, receive us who in faith flee unto thee, and grant deliverance from tribulations and afflictions. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou, O All-pure One, prophetically hast raised up Adam's fallen tabernacle, having carried in thy womb the Savior and God.[^226]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Christ, Who established the Church by faith, which unceasingly sings with hymns, crying aloud: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls.[^227]

*Troparion:*

O thou who ineffably didst conceive God and the Master: deliver me from the tempest of sin that holds me fast, and save me, O Most Pure One.

[f. 176vA] *Troparion:*

O Maiden, illuminate me with the beams of repentance, who am held fast by night in unfitting transgressions, granting me ever to do the works of light. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Theotokos, who didst give birth unto the Heavenly Bread that nourishes the hearts of all the faithful: satisfy my passionate, hungering soul.[^228] Both now:

*Theotokion:*

I have been established, understanding thy divine childbearing, O Virgin, and being enriched by thy protection, crying aloud: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I heard Thy report, and was afraid; I considered Thy works, and was amazed, O Lord.[^229]

*Troparion:*

Groaning before thee, O Sovereign Lady, and diligently crying from my mind: despise me not, neither put me to shame.

*Troparion:*

Burn up the withered thorn of carnal pleasures in my wretched soul, O Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto the divine Fire. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Grant me remission of transgressions, O all-blessed Pure One: and deliver me from eternal and fearful torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The Word Who made His abode in thee, O Most Pure One, refashioned my nature, fallen through transgressions.

[f. 177rA] *Ode 5. Heirmos:*

From the night I rise early, and cry unto Thee, O Lord of peace: illuminate my soul darkened by sins with the light of Thy commandments, and guide me.[^230]

*Troparion:*

I have become like unto unreasoning beasts, most wretched one that I am, in irrational passions, slaving for my wretched flesh: do thou, O Theotokos, grant me recovery.[^231]

*Troparion:*

Have mercy on me, who have sinned more than all men, and flee unto thy fervent protection, O Virgin Theotokos, and snatch me from torment. Glory:

*Troparion:*

May they who confess thee to be the Mother of God, O Virgin Mother, be accounted worthy through thee, O Mother of God, to attain the unending Kingdom and sweetness. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O praised and all-glorious unwedded Mother: quicken my soul deadened by sins and burnt up by uncontrolled passions.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

As Jonah cried out of the belly of hades: Deliver me from corruption; so we cry unto Thee: O Almighty Savior, have mercy on us.[^232]

*Troparion:*

The tempest of sin holds me fast, O Virgin, and leads me unto the corruption of despair: but stretch forth unto me thy hand of help, firm and all-powerful, [f. 177vA] *Troparion:*

Pour out upon me, O Sovereign Lady, the myrrh of thy fervent tender mercy, and deliver me from sins, and snatch me from the eternal fire. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Virgin, who didst release Eve from pain by thy most pure childbearing: release me, O Maiden, from the painful passions of soul and body. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou singest of God incarnate of thee, and weepest seeing Him lifted up upon the Cross, O Pure and all-immaculate Virgin Mother.[^233]

*Lord, have mercy (3). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

The Fruit of thy womb, O Pure One, planted the Cross in the midst of the earth, and delivered the world from corruption.[^234] Therefore as all-glorious we magnify thee.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Cast into the burning furnace, the venerable children turned the fire into cooling, singing thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^235]

*Troparion:*

The condemnation of my transgressions has multiplied, worthy of judgment; falling down, O Pure One, I cry unto thee: before the end grant me cleansing, and tears of compunction, and correction of ways.

*Troparion:*

Afflicted grievously with defiled transgressions, O Pure One, and going unto the grave of despair: do thou who didst give birth unto the Life of all, O Most Pure One, [f. 178rA] quicken me by divine deeds. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Good Mother of God, who by thy all-pure prayers deliverest from intelligible death those who call in faith upon thy grace that ever lives: account them worthy of the Pure One. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Glorify the incomprehensible childbearing, O Maiden, whereby we were delivered from death; for Whose sake having been born into incorruption, we cry: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The only beginningless King of glory, Whom the Heavenly Hosts praise and the angelic ranks tremble before: sing ye, O priests, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^236]

*Troparion:*

The furnace of passions burns me, O Virgin, and sets me on fire with the flame of pleasures: anticipating, quench it with thy merciful dew, granting strong consolation unto my soul.

*Troparion:*

I defiled my soul and corrupted my flesh with pleasures, wallowing in them, and I became a reproach unto men and a laughingstock unto enemies: O Sovereign Lady Theotokos, be my helper. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Implore God Whom thou didst bear, O Virgin, to send down salvation unto those who faithfully honor thee, and release from tribulations, and swift relief from grievous infirmities, and eternal goodness. Both now:

[f. 178vA] *Theotokion:*

Who fashioned Adam after His image, and came forth from his mingled substance, and dissolved the first curse: bless the Lord, ye priests, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^237]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

O all-praised one, higher than the Heavens, who seedlessly didst conceive the beginningless Word, and didst give birth unto the incarnate God for men: therefore all of us ever magnify thee.[^238]

*Troparion:*

O all-praised Virgin Mother Theotokos: with the light of thy prayers guide radiant unto the fear of God my wretched soul, grievously darkened by carnal passions.

*Troparion:*

Despairing of my salvation because of the multitude of my transgressions, and appearing in perplexity, O all-praised Sovereign Lady: send down upon me thy beneficence and thy mercy. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thy childbearing, O Theotokos, which is beyond nature, astonishes angels and terrifies men; for unto all it is ineffable and incomprehensible: singing of this, in godly manner we glorify thee. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Strip off from me the garments of deadness and corruption, O Virgin: who didst give birth unto the incarnate Word, the Garments of Salvation for men: therefore all of us ever magnify thee.[^239]

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

[f. 179rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting Christ in medallion flanked by angels in strapwork)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 7)

*On Friday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos:*

At Thy nod, the watery nature, which before was easily poured forth, was changed into an earthly form, O Lord; traversing wholly dryshod, Israel sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^240]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

O Sovereign Lady Theotokos: deliver from tribulations and sorrows, and from falling, and from eternal flame and torment, those who have hope in thee and flee unto thee.

*Troparion:*

With the dew of thy tender mercy, O Pure Virgin, quench the flame of my falls: that I may not come into the trial of all condemnation, and of the future fire. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Rain down upon me drops of thy tender mercy, O Maiden, who am ever withered by the heat of pleasures: that unceasingly I may glorify God and our Son Who was born of thee. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Water my soul inflamed with the heat of sorrow with the water of thy prayer, O Virgin: that I may bring forth fruits of divine joy unto Him Who was born of thee.

[f. 179vA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

In the beginning, O Lord Savior, Who didst establish the Heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all their host by the all-creating and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession.[^241]

*Troparion:*

I pray thee, O Virgin: grant salvation unto my soul, and cleansing, and abundant grace; for thou art powerful and good, and as a Maiden loving mankind, account me worthy of incorruptible life.

*Troparion:*

O unerring Salvation and Protectress of the world, who didst give birth unto the Fashioner, O Pure Virgin: deliver from tribulations those who faithfully sing of thee, and from the afflictions of this life, and from eternal condemnation. Glory:

*Troparion:*

We diligently pray thy compassions, O Maiden: despise not thy humble servants; but as thou art Good, look upon us with thy merciful eye, and deliver us from the tyranny of the most abominable devil. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

I who am held fast by a multitude of sorrow, O Virgin, have weakened all my strength, and lie all prostrate upon the earth. But from my soul I cry unto thee: raise me up again, and strengthen me by thy consolation.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Not leaving the Father's bosom, Thou didst descend upon the earth, O Christ God; I heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^242]

[f. 180rA] *Troparion:*

Mother of God, deliver me from corruption, and from the deceitful tyrant serpent, who ever prowls around me and trips up my steps which are according to God.[^243]

*Troparion:*

O intelligible Door of Life, open unto me the doors of repentance: for behold, now I, the wretched one, have drawn near unto the doors of despair from many transgressions. Glory:[^244]

*Troparion:*

Shake off well the thorns of passions and pleasures by thy tender mercy, O All-pure One, and show me pure; and clothe me with the light-bearing robe of grace. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Having cleansed the defilement of my soul and the uncleanness of my body, O Pure One, by thy acceptable prayers, present me pure ever to sing and glorify thee.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Night is without light, O Christ, unto unbelievers, but unto the faithful there is enlightenment in the sweetness of Thy words: therefore early do I rise unto Thee, and sing of Thy Divinity.[^245]

*Troparion:*

Pray the All-good Word, O all-immaculate Virgin, that we may be saved: for we have acquired thee as a Protectress, and a strong help in necessities.

*Troparion:*

Surrender me not unto enemies, O Pure One, who greatly seek my ruin because of my sins: but by thy tender mercy snatch me from their harm. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Knowing Thee, O Christ, to be the God of all and the Creator of all: upon Thee alone have we placed the hope of our salvation, [f. 180vA] Who for our sake wast made man. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, O Cloud that pourest forth living water; rejoice, O Virgin, the confirmation of martyrs and of apostles; rejoice, O All-immaculate One, honorable doxology.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Floating in the storm of the surges of this life, and shipwrecked by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, as Jonah, O Christ, I cry unto Thee: bring me up from the deadly deep.[^246]

*Troparion:*

The comeliness and beauty of majesty which I possessed through Baptism have I lost by unworthy deeds, O Sovereign Lady; but by thy fervent intercession, do thou restore this unto those who faithfully honor thee.

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Theotokos, from suffering, and cruel constraint, and griefs, granting me thy consolation: for I, thy servant, have no other protectress save thee, O All-immaculate One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Redeemer, the Benefactor, and the Savior, thou hast strength, and art able to do whatsoever thou wilt, O All-immaculate One; therefore we thy servants pray thee: deliver us from the tumult of passions. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The Prophet Isaiah, enlightened by the Spirit, of old beheld thee as a light cloud, upon which the Lord of Glory, sitting, came and cast down all the idols of Egypt, O Virgin Mother Most Pure.[^247]

[f. 181rA] *Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

Deliver from the eternal fire those who bow down before thy Childbearing: for Christ hath shown thee to be a protectress unto us who sing of thee.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Of old the children showed the burning furnace to be dew-dropping, as they sang of the one God, and said: O God of our fathers, supremely exalted, blessed art Thou.[^248]

*Troparion:*

A multitude of sins holds me fast, and leads me down unto the corruption of ruin and despair: O Theotokos, hasten beforehand and grant me saving help.

*Troparion:*

Deliver me from every malice of the enemy, and from temptation and afflictions, and in stillness and peace preserve my soul, O Most Pure One: that rejoicing I may sing of thy power. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Foreseeing the condemnation of my wicked deeds, I, the wretched one, cry unto thee from the depths of my heart, O Most Pure One: be thou an intercession for me, O Sovereign Lady, and save me. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

With the martyrs, with the angels, and with the apostles, O All-immaculate One, pray thy Son and Lord to save thy servants from misfortunes, perils, and griefs.

[f. 181vA] *Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Having been likened unto the children in the furnace, having received the dew of the Spirit, in faith we cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^249]

*Troparion:*

Be a help unto me, O Most Holy Virgin, who flee unto thee and cry with faith: Have mercy, O Pure One, and before the end grant me cleansing of sins; and in the hour of death grant me salvation, and after my repose bestow rest.

*Troparion:*

A grievous terror holds me fast when I contemplate the fearful examination, and the Judge Who cannot be enticed by those who have wrought evil—such as truly I, the wretched one, have committed—and those torments that have no end: O Sovereign Lady of the world, be my salvation. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O thou who art the cause of salvation for earthborn men, O Champion of the faithful, cleansing of sinners, O Wellspring of all good, and dispenser of blessings, Pure Mother of God: heal the infirmities of my soul, and pour forth eternal grace, and glory and joy. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Given from us unto the Master as a gift from the firstfruits of human nature, receive our supplications, O Most Pure One, returning unto us the fair gifts of thy help: that unceasingly we may sing of Him Who was born of thee, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.

[f. 182rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Mother of God and Virgin, giving birth and remaining a virgin still: this is not a work of nature, but of God's condescension; therefore, as the only one accounted worthy of divine wonders, thee, O Pure One, do we unceasingly magnify.[^250]

*Troparion:*

Under thy Protection, O Virgin, have I now fled; save me who am overwhelmed by a multitude of transgressions and perishing, O thou who didst give birth unto the Savior and Creator, and deliver me from eternal condemnation.

*Troparion:*

Entreat God the Creator and Lord, Who was incarnate from thy most pure blood, on our behalf, O Pure One, that in tender mercy He may have compassion upon thy despondent people. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Most Holy Pure Theotokos, deliver me, who am worthy of the unquenchable fire, the outer darkness, the eternal weeping, and condemnation, through thy fervent intercession unto the Lord. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Preserved under thy grace, O Theotokos, I remain unconsumed by the assaults of adversary foes; therefore, as a divine confirmation and fortress, I sing of thee, and bow down, and magnify thee.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 182vA] *(Woodcut: The Dormition of the Most Holy Theotokos / Full-page woodcut engraving depicting the Dormition within an oval medallion framed by luxuriant baroque foliage; Christ stands in radiance holding the soul of His Mother surrounded by Apostles and angels, flanked by the venerable founders of the Kyiv Caves Monastery, Theodosius and Anthony of the Caves, holding inscribed scrolls)*[^251]

---

# TONE 8

[f. 183rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting the Mother of God with the Christ Child in medallion, flanked by scrolling floral arabesques and cherubim)*

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Saturday at Small Compline,*[^252]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea, for He alone has been gloriously glorified.[^253]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Come mystically, O brethren, as from the beginning unto the Most Holy Theotokos let us the faithful today offer a new song, praising her majesty.

*Troparion:*

Moses, having of old been enlightened in mind by the vision of God, was clearly instructed concerning thy godlike conception surpassing nature, O Pure Virgin, which was revealed unto him in the Bush. Glory:[^254]

[f. 183vA] Unto thee do I present the deeds of my heart; and do thou favorably offer the writing, being near, having placed thee as a shelter of divine intercession before Christ the Master. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Incline thine ear unto me, O Pure One; with orthodox faith turn thy precious face unto me who flee unto thee with love; and unto me who bow down in fear, hear my suppliant voice.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou art the confirmation of those who flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings of Thee.[^255]

*Troparion:*

The Patriarch's Ladder of old prefigured thee, O All-immaculate One: for it revealed the angelic descent, the divine condescension of God unto us in thy womb.[^256]

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, O Tribe of Judah, which Jacob foretold, that from his tribe deliverance should spring forth, Jesus Christ: Whom thou, O Most Pure One, having brought forth, hast glorified. Glory:[^257]

*Troparion:*

Encompassed by sins, I have found thee to be a haven for salvation, O Most Holy Theotokos, our hope and help; therefore guide me unto repentance. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Having thee near unto the Master, O All-glorious Sovereign Lady, I have laid upon thee in faith the book of my deeds: cease not to have compassion upon me.

[f. 184rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

From the overshadowing Mountain of the only Theotokos, O Word, the Prophet godlike perceived that Thou wast about to become incarnate, and with fear he glorified Thy power.[^258]

*Troparion:*

As if clothed in golden fringes, O Bride of the Father, having received grace, adorned with the beauty of virginity, thou hast appeared as the Mother of the Son of God.[^259]

*Troparion:*

As the true Zion, Christ the Word was pleased to take thee unto Himself as a divine habitation, having chosen thee, O chosen Theotokos, for the renewal of the whole world. Glory:[^260]

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, fair palace of the Word, virginal bridal chamber of the King! Rejoice, praise of all the Bodiless Powers! Rejoice, help of mankind! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

They estrange themselves from God, and therefore perish, who reject the icons of thy Son, O Mother of God, Virgin Theotokos, through which those who honor thee are saved.[^261]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Dispel the gloom of my soul, O Giver of Light, Christ God, having driven away the primordial darkness of the abyss; and grant me the light of Thy commandments, O Word, that rising early I may glorify Thee.[^262]

*Troparion:*

Having come together with the divine Gabriel, let us faithfully cry unto the Theotokos: Rejoice, O holy Virgin, full of grace, the Lord is with thee, Who for thy sake consumed sorrow and bestowed rejoicing.

[f. 184vA] *Troparion:*

Gideon beheld thy most holy womb, O Pure Virgin, into which the Word descended as dew, and was incarnate by the Holy Spirit, being undivided from the Godhead of the Father. Glory:[^263]

*Troparion:*

Thou art a helper unto the world and a protectress unto sinful men, O Virgin Mother of God, and unto those who flee unto thee with faith and love, a saving transformation, and the loosening of many transgressions. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Seedlessly hast thou budded forth, before every creature, the Offspring of the Son from the Father, timeless and beginningless, by the Divine Spirit, O Pure Mother of God: the likeness of Whose appearance all of us revere.[^264]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Held fast by many sins, receive me who flee unto Thy tender mercies, O Compassionate One, as the Prophet, O Lord, and save me.[^265]

*Troparion:*

Thee who art the mirror of virginity and the honorable receptacle of the Godhead do we praise with songs, O Virgin unacquainted with wedlock.

*Troparion:*

God was incarnate in thy womb, passionlessly and awesomely, O Bride of God: as in a new scroll written by the Father's Finger. Glory:[^266]

[f. 185rA] We possess thy protection as cleansing, and as our certain hope and defense, O Pure Virgin: put not thy servants to shame, O Sovereign Lady. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

By thine intercession, O Bride of God, calm the unceasing surging of passions, and guide us unto the haven of stillness.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 8, Podoben: "To thee, the Champion Leader" (Возбра́нной Воево́дѣ):*

To thee, the Champion Leader, do we thy servants dedicate a song of victory and thanksgiving as ones delivered from evil, O Theotokos; but as thou hast might unassailable, deliver us from all dangers, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Bride unwedded![^267]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The fire was put to shame of old in Babylon by God's condescension; therefore the children, with joy-stepping feet in the furnace as in a bridal chamber, danced, singing: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^268]

*Troparion:*

Thou hast appeared as a temple unto the incomprehensible God, O Most Pure Virgin; by thy prayers show me also to be a temple of His divine grace, and preserve me unharmed.

*Troparion:*

A Holy Mountain art thou, overshadowed by God, a rich Mountain, O All-immaculate One, a fertile Mountain with divine splendors, a Mountain wherein God was pleased to dwell. Glory:[^269]

[f. 185vA] Wholly as the Pure One hast thou received the Word being incarnate in thy womb; entreat Him, O Most Pure One, to cleanse me from transgressions, who flee unto thee with a contrite heart and pure faith. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast given birth unto One of the Trinity, O Theotokos, Who became unchangeably united in the flesh, being twofold in nature: the visible likeness of Whose form and image we revere.[^270]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who was foretold on the holy mountain, and in the fiery bush revealed unto Moses the mystery of virginity, sing of the Lord and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^271]

*Troparion:*

Thou hast appeared unto the Prophet as the censer of the divine coal that takes away sins, O Virgin Theotokos, Mother of our God.[^272]

*Troparion:*

Daniel foresaw thee as a great Mountain, O Virgin Theotokos, from which the Precious Stone, Christ, was cut out in the flesh, and cast down the temples of idol deceit. Glory:[^273]

*Troparion:*

The great whale of grievous sin and the despair of my passions seek to swallow me; but hasten beforehand and save thy servant, O Sovereign Lady. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

He Who conversed with men through thee, being God of majesty, was pleased to receive the form of a man: Whose countenance we revere in writings, O Virgin.[^274]

[f. 186rA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Confessing thee as truly the Theotokos, saved by thee, O Pure Virgin, with the Heavenly Hosts do we magnify thee.[^275]

*Troparion:*

The wisest one in the Song of Songs sings of thee, O Virgin Theotokos, as an enclosed garden and a sealed fountain by the Divine Spirit; therefore as the Garden of Life, Christ is incarnate.[^276]

*Troparion:*

The Prophet proclaimed thine ineffable Childbearing, foreseeing a sealed Book, whose mystery of the incarnation of thy childbearing no one comprehended. Glory:[^277]

*Troparion:*

Unto thee with compunction of soul do we all pray, despise not our supplications, O Sovereign Lady; but be unto us a benign protection, and hear our prayer. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

I fall down before the image of thy Son and before thine own, and those who doubt concerning honoring them I cast away as the falsehoods of Manes, O Virgin Theotokos: therefore orthodoxly do I conclude the song.[^278]

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 186vA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting the Mother of God with the Christ Child in medallion, flanked by scrolling floral branches)*

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Sunday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

The wonderworking rod of Moses of old, striking crosswise and dividing the sea, submerged Pharaoh the chariot-driver; and it saved Israel who fled on foot, singing a song unto God.[^279]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Most glorious things were spoken of thee, O Most Pure One, who alone hast become the glory of the faithful; therefore by thy prayers show me to be a partaker of future glory, who glorify thee, O Most Holy and All-hymned Virgin.[^280]

*Troparion:*

Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Most Pure One, through Whom all of us earthborn were delivered from the curse, O Blessed and All-honorable One! O ineffable wonder! O incomprehensible report! The salvation of all the faithful! Glory:

*Troparion:*

A multitude of angels ever entreats Thee, O Lord, and the choir of Apostles, the company of Prophets, the assembly of Martyrs, [f. 187rA] and of Thy Venerable Ones: for the Theotokos' sake, grant us forgiveness of transgressions, as Thou art the Lover of mankind. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thee, O Most Pure One, who didst receive in thy womb the uncontainable Fire, do I entreat in faith: deliver me and snatch me from the fire of Gehenna, and free me from eternal torment by thy favorable prayers.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

O Lord, Artificer of the celestial vault and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O Height of desires, Confirmation of the faithful, only Lover of mankind.[^281]

*Troparion:*

Having fallen away from a pure life, O Most Pure One, I have become likened unto the beasts, and am wholly condemned; do thou who didst give birth unto the Judge deliver and save me from every condemnation.[^282]

*Troparion:*

Grant me sighing, O Pure Maiden, and tears of soul, and perpetual compunction, that I may weep over the passions that hold me fast through ignorance, O all-blessed one. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The Heavenly Ranks, the ministering spirits, the company of Prophets, and Thine Apostles and Martyrs, unceasingly pray Thee, O Compassionate One: grant Thy people remission of transgressions. Both now:

[f. 187vA] *Theotokion:*

Since thou art merciful, O Most Pure One, entreat the Merciful One to account me worthy now of divine tender mercy; for having acquired merciless ways without compassion, I have become worthy of the eternal fire.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who left not the Father's bosom, but in mercy visited our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind![^283]

*Troparion:*

Tear up the spiritual handwriting of my sins, O Most Pure One, with the divine Spear that pierced the divine Ribs of Him Who was pleased to be made man from thee; and pray Him to inscribe me in the Book of the Saved, me who foolishly became estranged from Him.[^284]

*Troparion:*

That with thankful voices I may glorify thee, and with divine love magnify thee, O All-immaculate One, who by thy great childbearing dost draw near all that were estranged: account me also, who am exceedingly estranged and have grievously fallen, worthy of divine forgiveness. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The laws of nature were abolished in thee, for beyond nature thou didst give birth unto God the Word; therefore in faith I pray thee, O All-immaculate One: save by thy prayers him who hath sinned exceedingly beyond human nature, and who, having estranged himself from God, has turned back. Both now:

[f. 188rA] *Theotokion:*

The enemy hath wounded all my soul with the darts of sin, and hath defiled my heart with pleasures, and led me away from the righteous path; therefore, O Virgin, I cry unto thee: having turned me back, heal and save me.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy presence, O Light never-setting, and an alien darkness hath covered me, the wretched one? But turn me back, and guide my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray Thee.[^285]

*Troparion:*

Generations of generations now call thee blessed, O most blessed Virgin, and the Heavenly Powers praise the only blessed among women, the restoration of fallen Adam, as having united the earthly with the heavenly.

*Troparion:*

Pray, O good one, having given birth unto the Good Word Who delivered us from unreason, that I may remain whole, and obtain an unshaken standing, walking well and doing what is well-pleasing unto God. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Word Who didst shed Thy Blood upon the Cross, for the sake of the exceedingly great abyss of Thy tender mercy, dry up the abyss of my passions; and for the Theotokos' sake account me worthy to be well-pleasing unto Thee, my God, in compunction. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Mother Virgin Maiden, Bride of God, who in the flesh didst give birth unto the Incomprehensible One, the Glory of angels and the Wound of demons: account my heart, which hath been wounded by sin, worthy of divine healings.

[f. 188vA] *Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities, and bring me up from the depth of evils, I pray Thee; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^286]

*Troparion:*

Human nature was shown mercy through thee, O merciful Sovereign Lady, for thou didst give birth unto the Merciful One, O Most Pure One; therefore I pray thee: have mercy upon my soul.

*Troparion:*

The Bodiless Ranks pray Thee, O Christ, together with Thy Martyrs, Prophets, and Apostles: for the sake of her who gave Thee birth, save Thy people from every tribulation. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O untilled Earth that didst give birth unto the Husbandman and the Creator of all: show my soul, which hath been frozen cold by passions, to be fruitful with divine understanding. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

I have transgressed the commandments that lead me unto God, and unwillingly have become enslaved unto evil passions; therefore I ever pray thee, O Virgin: snatch me from their dominion.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 8, Podoben: "To thee, the Champion Leader" (Возбра́нной Воево́дѣ):*

Let us the faithful praise another piety for the hosts, and a praiseworthy height for our souls, the Virgin Maiden Mother: Rejoice, thou who in thy womb didst conceive the Creator of angels; rejoice, thou who didst incarnate God the Word, the cleansing of those who fall; rejoice, O Bride unwedded![^287]

[f. 189rA] *Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The fire was put to shame of old in Babylon by God's condescension; therefore the children, with joy-stepping feet in the furnace as in a flower-garden, danced, singing: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^288]

*Troparion:*

Ineffable is thy childbearing, and of fearful and ineffable things hast thou accounted us worthy, O Theotokos; therefore deliver me from fearful torment, and fill me with ineffable joy, that worthily I may sing of thee, the all-hymned one.

*Troparion:*
