Having spent my life in slothfulness, I have become unfruitful; and I am terrified of the trial and of the unquenchable fire of Gehenna. O Theotokos who didst give birth unto the unendurable Fire: incline to deliver me from Gehenna by thy prayers. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Exceeding all wonders is the great wonder of thy Childbearing, O Pure Virgin; therefore I cry unto thee, Mother of God: make marvelous thy tender mercies upon me, and deliver me from the wrath to come, and save me. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Unto me who have lived in slothfulness, and contradicted Thy holy laws and Thy precious commandments, O Christ: show compassion, O Savior, for the sake of her who gave Thee birth, as Thou art a beneficent God and plenteous in mercy.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The Chaldean tyrant fiercely kindled the furnace sevenfold for the godly; but having seen them saved by a higher power, he cried aloud unto the Creator and Redeemer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^289]

[f. 189vA] *Troparion:*

Quicken my deadened soul, raise it up fallen, heal it wounded, bring peace unto my mind, calm the waves of temptations, O Virgin, and save me who cry aloud: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

As the Thief I cry unto Thee: Remember me, O Lover of mankind; I weep as the Harlot, and cry: I have sinned as of old the Prodigal; receive me, the repentant sinner, for the Theotokos' sake, that with fervor I may cry unto Thee: Ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Glory:[^290]

*Troparion:*

As servants we cry unto thee, O all-hymned Theotokos, with the countless noetic Powers, Prophets and Venerable Ones, Apostles and Martyrs, jointly praying for all men who divinely sing: Ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

More honorable art thou, O all-immaculate Theotokos, than the ranks of transcendent angels; for thou didst give birth unto their Creator and Lord from a virginal unwedded womb, in two essences, unconfusedly and unchangeably, one Hypostasis incarnate, O Theotokos.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Exceedingly fearful is the report of the ineffable condescension of God: how the Most High voluntarily descended even unto the flesh, having become man from the virginal womb; therefore thee, the Most Pure Theotokos, do we the faithful magnify.[^291]

[f. 190rA] *Troparion:*

O divine Bed of Solomon, which threescore valiant men surround [the words of the Divine Scripture], upon which God rested: preserve me unharmed by thy power, who am ever surrounded by myriads of demons, O Pure and Perpetual Virgin.[^292]

*Troparion:*

O thou who distillest divine sweetness, having given birth unto the Sweetness of all: sweeten my soul which hath been embittered by the venom of the serpent, ever alienating me from bitter harm by thine intercession, O unashamed protectress of the faithful. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Jesus, Sun of Glory: enlighten my wretched soul through the prayers of her who gave Thee birth, the Pure Virgin Maiden of God, and of the Bodiless Servants, Thine honorable Apostles, Hierarchs, Prophets, Martyrs, the Elect, and Thy Venerable Ones. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

I am terrified, O only King, of Thy second and fearful coming, being wholly condemned and in perplexity, O Lord; therefore before the end I cry unto Thee: For the sake of her who gave Thee birth, spare me and save me, O Merciful, Good, Lover of mankind.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after Our Father: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 190vA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting floral arabesques and cherubim)*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Monday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Having traversed the water as upon dry land, and having escaped from the evil of Egypt, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Redeemer and God.[^293]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Knowing thee, O Mother of God, as the prayerful fountain of waters pouring forth streams unto the faithful for souls and bodies, all of us unceasingly glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

Beyond nature hast thou given birth unto the transcendent Word of God, O Virgin, and hast delivered nature from corruption; therefore free me from irrational and unnatural passions. Glory:

*Troparion:*

The creepers of carnal passions now surround me and afflict me grievously: visit thy servant quickly, O Virgin, and save me from those that oppress me. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

He Who upholds all creation, bearing and saving it, was seen circumscribed in thine arms, O Pure One, and hath renewed the human race.

[f. 191rA] *Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou art the confirmation of those who flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings of Thee.[^294]

*Troparion:*

Allay the disturbance of the grievous disease that now holds me fast, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, and free me from transgressions and passions.

*Troparion:*

Unsearchable is the abyss of thy compassions, O Most Pure One: whereby do thou deliver me also from transgressions and disease. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Make haste and visit me that am diseased, O Most Pure One, and deliver me from grievous illness and all affliction. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Magnify the mercy and intercession of thy prayers upon me, O Most Pure One, and deliver me from misfortunes and griefs.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^295]

*Troparion:*

Ever show unto my heart, O Most Pure One, a propitiation, the remembrance of the saints, and the cleansing of transgressions.

*Troparion:*

Grant me salvation of soul and body, O All-immaculate One; and unto him that is sick grant healing, and deliverance from grievous things. Glory:

[f. 191vA] *Troparion:*

Bring to naught the wiles of the subtle demons, O Most Pure One, and the uprising of passions; and grant strength unto us the infirm. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

From thy womb, O Most Pure One, the Sun of Righteousness shone forth, and hath enlightened the world: with Whose divine rays do thou also enlighten me.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy face, O Unwaning Light, and a strange darkness hath covered me, the wretched one; but turn me, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my paths, I pray Thee.[^296]

*Troparion:*

By thy Childbearing the bonds of death have been loosed, and the dominion of corruption hath been bound, O Most Pure and All-hymned Virgin; therefore swiftly loose also the fetters of my transgressions, and heavy sorrows.

*Troparion:*

From the wiles of demons and human malice deliver me, O Sovereign Lady, and swiftly heal the disease of soul and body, O thou who alone didst give birth unto the Physician of all flesh and spirits, the Savior and Lord. Glory:

*Troparion:*

As the tongs of the Divine Coal, O Most Pure One, I pray thee: burn up every impassioned lust of thy servant, and make tolerable the grievous infirmities of diseases, and dry up the torrents of sorrows. Both now:

[f. 192rA] *Theotokion:*

Thy Son, O All-immaculate Sovereign Lady, grants thee as salvation unto all who have believed in Him, and who proclaim Him as God, manifestly incarnate of thee; therefore save me from diverse perils and tribulations.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the abyss of evils, I pray Thee: for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^297]

*Troparion:*

Look upon me with thy merciful eye, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me swiftly from grievous diseases, and from every affliction of mankind now, and from snares and falling.

*Troparion:*

From senseless and impassioned disposition, and from the envy of unworthy men, and from all malice and worldly affliction, deliver me, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, by thy supplications. Glory:

*Troparion:*

He Who fashioned us was fashioned in thee, O Virgin, delivering human nature from corruption; therefore deliver me, O Pure One, from the temptations that hold me fast, by thy prayers. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst appear as a most pure temple of the uncontainable God, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady: by thy prayers show me also to be a temple of His grace, and preserve me unharmed. [f. 193vA] of the surging sea, destroy the assault of all sorrows, and by thy prayers grant me stillness unto all ages.[^298] Glory:

*Troparion:*

He Who formed the earthly body of man, and then placed within him an immortal soul, is fashioned in thee, O Virgin, and renews him again; through thy prayers show Him to be propitious and favorable unto me, and grant strength and speedy deliverance unto all that are in sorrow. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

With light dost thou ever illuminate those who glorify thee, O Most Pure Maiden, from whom the Unwaning Light shone forth; and from the terror of night and darkness, and from the barkings of demons, dost thou deliver thy servants; from which snatch me also, and by thy prayers save me from the evils that encompass me.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Confessing thee to be truly the Theotokos, we who through thee are saved, O Pure Virgin, with the Heavenly Hosts magnify thee.[^299]

*Troparion:*

Exalt the royal horn, O Most Pure One: grant strength unto the faithful against the heathens, and establish the scepters of the Tsardom.

[f. 194rA] *Troparion:*

Cast down the haughty, and vanquish the armies of foreigners that desire wars, O Pure Virgin, and free thy servants unharmed from them, O Maiden. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O sole champion of Orthodox Christians who glorify thee: by thy prayers put swiftly to shame the armed Hagarenes.[^300] Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Instruct, O Virgin, the companies of the faithful, strengthening them against enemies visible and invisible; and save them from every affliction.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion: and after Our Father: Troparia, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

*(Woodcut: Vignette depicting the Mother of God with the Christ Child in an architectural baldachin above the holy table, flanked by clergy and the faithful praying)*

[f. 194vA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting the Mother of God with the Christ Child in medallion, flanked by scrolling floral arabesques)*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Tuesday at Small Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Having traversed the water as upon dry land, and having escaped from the evil of Egypt, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Redeemer and God.[^301]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Holden by many temptations, unto thee do I flee, seeking salvation: O Mother of the Word and Virgin, save me from cruel and grievous things.

*Troparion:*

The assaults of passions disturb me, and many sorrows fill my soul; bring peace, O Maiden, by the calm of thy Son and God, O All-immaculate One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thee who didst give birth unto the Savior and God do I entreat, O Virgin: deliver me from perils; for unto thee do I now flee, reaching out with soul and mind. Both now:

[f. 195rA] *Theotokion:*

Diseased in body and in soul, vouchsafe me divine visitation and providence from thee, O only Mother of God, as thou art good and the Parent of the Good One.

*Second Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea, a song of victory, for He hath been glorified.[^302]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, deliverance from the ancient curse, and fountain of blessing! Rejoice, Mother of Life, destruction of Hades, slaying of death! Rejoice, loosing of sorrow, spacious receptacle of joy! Rejoice, O all-hymned Theotokos!

*Troparion:*

Chariot of the Word, noetic and animate, rejoice, O All-immaculate One! Rejoice, virginal chariot of many names, chariot prefigured by myriads! Rejoice, higher than the Cherubim of the Theophany, and surpassing the Seraphim! Glory:

*Troparion:*

Most hallowed above the Noetic Powers, rejoice, O Bride of God! Rejoice, higher than all created nature! Rejoice, Palace of God! Rejoice, Fiery Throne!

[f. 195vA] *Troparion:*

Rejoice, many-lighted Lampstand! Rejoice, Sovereign Lady celebrated by myriads! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, O undefiled one, who didst bud forth the Rod from the Root of Jesse; rejoice, thou who of old didst prefigure the budding of Aaron's rod, mysteriously and profoundly: for as that rod budded forth flower and almond, so hast thou blossomed forth Christ.[^303]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

O Lord, Artificer of the celestial vault and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O Summit of desires, Confirmation of the faithful, only Lover of mankind.[^304]

*Troparion:*

As protectress and shelter of my life do I set thee, O Virgin Mother of God: pilot me unto thy haven, O cause of good things and confirmation of the faithful, only All-hymned One.

*Troparion:*

I pray thee, O Maiden, dispel the tumult of my soul, and the tempest of my grief: for thou, O Bride of God, hast given birth unto Christ, the Prince of peace, O only Most Pure One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Benefactor, the Author of good things, pour forth the wealth of beneficence unto all: for thou canst do all things, as having given birth unto Christ Who is mighty in strength, O Blessed of God. Both now:

[f. 196rA] *Theotokion:*

Unto me who am tested by grievous diseases and painful passions, do thou help me, O Virgin: for I know thee, O All-immaculate One, to be an inexhaustible and unfailing treasury of healings.

*Second Canon. Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou art the confirmation of those who flee unto Thee, O Lord, and Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings of Thee.[^305]

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, most mighty weapon for the faithful, O Sovereign Lady! Rejoice, steadfast Protectress! Rejoice, golden armory! Rejoice, helper of sinners! Rejoice, rampart of those who call upon thee! Rejoice, Joy of the world!

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, steadfast confirmation of human salvation! Rejoice, recall of Adam and Eve, through whom they received again their ancient inheritance; rejoice, thou who didst open Paradise anew! Glory:

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, Unburnt Bush! Rejoice, Bearer of the Light! Rejoice, Cloud of the Sun! Rejoice, womb-seat most glorious and proclaimed everywhere! Rejoice, animate City of Christ the Living God! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Unwedded Mother, rejoice, O Pure Sovereign Lady! Rejoice, unplowed and untilled field that didst cultivate and sprout forth God, the Husbandman of all! Rejoice, earth that didst cause truth to shine forth!

[f. 196vA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^306]

*Troparion:*

O thou who didst give birth unto the Lord the Helmsman: still the disturbance of my passions, and the tempest of my transgressions, O Bride of God.

*Troparion:*

Unto me who call upon Thee grant the abyss of thy tender mercy, O thou who didst give birth unto the compassionate Savior of all who hymn Thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Delighting in thy gifts, O Most Pure One, we sing a thanksgiving song unto thee, knowing thee to be the Mother of God. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Having acquired thee as hope and confirmation, and an immovable rampart of salvation, O All-immaculate One, we are delivered from all grievous evils.

*Second Canon. Ode 4. Heirmos:*

The Prophet Habakkuk with spiritual eyes foresaw Thy coming, O Lord; therefore he also cried aloud: God shall come from Theman; glory to Thy power, glory, O Christ, to Thy coming![^307]

*Troparion:*

Orient of the Father's illumination, and of the Sun Who shone forth before the morning star from the Father, rejoice, O Theotokos, light and animate Cloud, O Virgin; rejoice, O Mother! Rejoice, O blessed one! Rejoice, O supremely glorified and all-immaculate one!

[f. 197rA] *Troparion:*

Thou art the golden Censer, O Sovereign Lady, of the unendurable and immaterial Coal, whereby the torn handwriting of Adam's disobedience was burned up; therefore I cry unto thee: Rejoice, through whom joy, and the sweetness of Paradise, and delight have been given unto all! Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thou art the Glory, Honor, and Praise of men, and the Crown and Diadem of Angels: therefore earth and Heaven, having formed one Church, in diverse manners cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, Sovereign Lady of the world! Rejoice, Helper of all men! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Honorable Bed, which Solomon prefigured, surrounded by threescore valiant men, rejoice, O Virgin, gilded Ark of noetic sanctification![^308] Rejoice, Divine Tongs! Rejoice, Fiery Bush! Rejoice, Door, and Ladder, and Bridge!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Enlighten us with Thy commandments, O Lord, and with Thy high arm; and grant Thy peace unto us, O only Lover of mankind.[^309]

*Troparion:*

Fill my heart with gladness, O Pure One, giving mine incorruptible joy, O thou who didst give birth unto the Cause of gladness.

*Troparion:*

Deliver us from perils, O Pure Theotokos, who didst give birth unto eternal deliverance, and the peace that passes all understanding. Glory:

[f. 197vA] *Troparion:*

Dispel the gloom of my transgressions, O Bride of God, by the light of thy brightness, O thou who didst give birth unto the divine and pre-eternal Light. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Heal the infirmity of my soul, O Pure One, having accounted me worthy of thy visitation; and grant me health by thy prayers.

*Second Canon. Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Early in the morning we cry unto Thee, O Lord, save us: for Thou art our God, and beside Thee we know no other.[^310]

*Troparion:*

Grant me at the time of my departure to pass through without sorrow the spiritual adversaries and their tormenting aerial host: that joyfully, O Sovereign Lady, I may cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, unashamed hope of all!

*Troparion:*

As having conceived Joy, rejoice, O All-immaculate One! Rejoice, O Most Pure One! Rejoice, purple-formed, sweet-smelling Flower of purity! Rejoice, crimson-dipped scarlet Rose of virginity, and fragrant odor of God! Glory:

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, mystical Myrrh-vessel, pouring forth sweet-smelling myrrh! Rejoice, Divine Spring, who dost pour forth living water!

[f. 198rA] Rejoice, untilled Vineyard, O Sovereign Lady, who bearest the living Cluster! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, impassable Gate, through which Christ the Lord passed! Rejoice, Thou who by Thy Childbearing didst open the gates of Paradise! Rejoice, through whom Heaven rejoices and earth keeps festival; rejoice, Thou who hast joined things above with things below![^311]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord, and unto Him will I declare my sorrow, for my soul is filled with evils, and my life hath drawn near unto Hades; but like Jonah I pray: From corruption, O my God, lead me up![^312]

*Troparion:*

As He hath saved my nature from death and corruption by giving Himself over unto death, when it was held fast by corruption and death: do thou, O Virgin, pray the Lord and thy Son to deliver me from the malice of enemies.

*Troparion:*

I know thee as the protectress of my life and a steadfast guardian, O Virgin, dispelling the tumult of temptations and driving away the assaults of demons; and I pray thee ever, O All-immaculate One, to deliver me from corruption and passions. Glory:

*Troparion:*

We have acquired thee as a rampart of refuge, O Maiden, and perfect salvation for souls, and relief in sorrows, and by thy light [f. 198vA] we ever rejoice, O Sovereign Lady! And now save us from passions and perils. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Upon a bed of sickness I now lie low, and there is no healing for my flesh; but unto thee who didst give birth unto God, the Savior of the world and Deliverer from diseases, do I pray, O Good One: raise me up from the corruption of diseases.

*Second Canon. Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Grant me a radiant robe, Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, O Plenteous in mercy, Christ our God.[^313]

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, animate Scarlet Vesture, who from thy most pure blood didst dye a purple robe for the King of all, and didst cover the nakedness of Adam: Rejoice, O All-hymned One!

*Troparion:*

Natural, sensory bread strengthens indeed human hearts, O Maiden, but thy holy Name when sung confirms Christian souls; therefore with joy every tongue cries Rejoice unto thee. Glory:[^314]

*Troparion:*

Rejoice, golden-radiant Lampstand! Rejoice, Ark that contained God! Rejoice, Tabernacle! Rejoice, Holy Mountain! Rejoice, animate City of the Living God! Rejoice, Palace of Christ! Rejoice, divine, many-lighted Temple! Both now:

[f. 199rA] *Theotokion:*

O Most Pure and All-immaculate One, rejoice, excellent Flower of nature! Rejoice, common honor and God-given grace of the human race; rejoice, thou who hast honored dishonored human nature by thy Childbearing!

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*

Beholding the Lamb, the Shepherd, and the Savior of the world upon the Cross, she that gave Thee birth said weeping: The world indeed rejoices, receiving deliverance through Thee, but mine inward parts burn, beholding Thy Crucifixion, which Thou endurest for all, my Son and my God.[^315]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Children coming down from Judea in Babylon of old trampled down the flame of the furnace by their faith in the Trinity, singing: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^316]

*Troparion:*

When Thou wast pleased to order our salvation, O Savior, Thou didst dwell in the Virgin's womb, and didst show her forth as a protectress unto the world: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.

*Troparion:*

Entreat Him Who desires mercy, Whom thou didst bear, O Pure Mother, to deliver from transgressions and defilement of soul those who cry in faith: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou. Glory:

[f. 199vA] A treasury of salvation, and fountain of incorruption, a pillar of confirmation, and door of repentance hast Thou shown forth her that gave Thee birth unto those who cry: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Mother of God who didst bear for us the Savior Christ: vouchsafe to heal bodily weaknesses and spiritual diseases of those who with love approach thy divine protection.

*Second Canon. Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Hebrew Children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and having changed the fire into dew, they cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers![^317]

*Troparion:*

Thou only who knewest not a man, and thou only who art incorrupt, rejoice, O Sovereign Lady, Virgin and Mother who gavest birth to a Child, yet unwedded! Rejoice, strange and fearful mystery! Rejoice, Sweetness of Angels! Rejoice, Joy unto men!

*Troparion:*

O Zion, animate City of Christ, King of kings, concerning whom most glorious things were spoken: rejoice, O Bride of God! Rejoice, Ladder whereby we ascend unto Heaven from the earth, and from corruption unto life! Glory:[^318]

*Troparion:*

A God-planted Garden, a fragrant enclosure cultivated by God hast thou appeared, O Virgin, who didst blossom forth the Flower of immortality; therefore in unison we cry Rejoice [f. 200rA] unto thee: Rejoice, Giver of joy! Rejoice, Fountain of sweetness! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, deliver me from dishonorable passions, that joyfully I may cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, Fountain of purity! Rejoice, Treasury of all honor! Rejoice, Receptacle of God! Rejoice, Dwelling-place of Christ!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

The King of Heaven, Whom all the angelic hosts hymn: praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^319]

*Troparion:*

Despise not him that requires help of thee, O Virgin, who hymns and supremely exalts thee, O Maiden, unto all ages.

*Troparion:*

Heal the infirmity of my soul, and bodily diseases, O Virgin, that I may glorify thee unto all ages. Glory:

*Troparion:*

A wealth of healings dost thou pour out upon those who faithfully hymn thee, O Virgin, and supremely exalt thee unto all ages. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou drivest away the assaults of temptations, and the onsets of passions, O Virgin, that I may glorify thee unto all ages.

*Second Canon. Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Him Who was manifested on the holy mountain, and in the fiery Bush revealed the mystery of the Virgin unto Moses, the Lord: praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^320]

[f. 200vA] *Troparion:*

Rejoice, Rod that budded from the Root of Jesse! Rejoice, thou who without watering didst blossom forth the fair Flower, Christ! Rejoice, bountiful Mountain! Rejoice, overshadowing Mountain! Rejoice, Mountain of God, in which God the Word was well pleased to dwell before all ages![^321]

*Troparion:*

With the iron rod of thy mighty protection, drive away the passions that bitterly oppress me, which like barking dogs and roaring beasts surround my humble soul: that I may cry Rejoice unto thee, O Virgin. Glory:[^322]

*Troparion:*

Thou hast adorned thyself with the beauties of all fair things, O thou who art fair among women, having given birth unto Christ Who is comelier than the sons of men; therefore with joy we cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, O Theotokos, Pure One full of grace! Rejoice, more glorious than all creation! Both now:[^323]

*Theotokion:*

Daniel foresaw thee of old as the unhewn Mountain, O Mother of God; rejoice, from whom the cornerstone was cut without hands! Rejoice, God-receiving, Fire-bearing Tongs! Rejoice, holier than the immaterial Angels! Rejoice, more honorable than all creation![^324]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Confessing thee to be truly the Theotokos, we who through thee are saved, O Pure Virgin, with the Heavenly Hosts magnify thee.[^325]

[f. 201rA] *Troparion:*

Turn not away from the stream of my tears, O Virgin who didst give birth unto Christ Who takes away every tear from every face.[^326]

*Troparion:*

Fill my heart with joy, O Virgin, who didst receive the fullness of joy, consuming sinful sorrow. Glory:

*Troparion:*

With the rays of thy light enlighten, O Virgin, driving away the gloom of ignorance from those who with right faith confess thee to be the Theotokos. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Be a haven and protection unto those who flee unto thee, O Virgin, and an immovable rampart, a refuge and shelter and rejoicing.

*Second Canon. Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Thee, the true Theotokos, let us the people honor, who didst receive in thy womb the Divine Fire without being consumed: in songs do we magnify thee.[^327]

*Troparion:*

Behold, all generations bless thee, the ever-blessed one, as thou didst foretell by the Holy Spirit, O Mother of God; and as unto her who gave birth unto Joy, I cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, Treasury of life! Rejoice, Fountain that pourest forth spiritual honey![^328]

*Troparion:*

Thou hast set upright, O Pure Sovereign Lady, the stumbling of forefather Adam, and the sorrow of foremother Eve hast thou turned into joy; therefore as unto the Cause of Joy [f. 201vA] we cry Rejoice unto thee: Rejoice, Joy of the faithful! Rejoice, Gladness of Christians! Glory:

*Troparion:*

It is worthy to chant Rejoice unto thee: for in thee substantially hath dwelt the transcendent Joy,[^329] O Virgin Maiden Mother of God; rejoice, delightful Paradise! Rejoice, golden-flowing fountain of immortality! Rejoice, thou who pourest forth the true drink of immortality! Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thy longing, O All-hymned One, hath pierced my heart with a sweet dart, and constrains me to cry Rejoice unto thee, and ever to chant: Rejoice, calm haven! Rejoice, sweetest impassable Sea, who didst drown the noetic Pharaoh!

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father, and the usual dismissal.*

*(Engraving: The Coronation of the Mother of God by the Holy Trinity / The Most Holy Theotokos kneeling before Christ and God the Father, with the Holy Spirit as a Dove above, flanked by ornate decorative crosses)*

[f. 202rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting floral arabesques and cherubim)*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Wednesday at Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Armed Pharaoh did the wonderworking staff of Moses drown of old, striking the sea crosswise and dividing it; but it saved Israel fleeing on foot, chanting a song unto God.[^330]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Thou hast given birth unto the incarnate Word of God, Who was bodiless before, beyond nature, O All-immaculate Theotokos and Virgin, Who lived in the world as the God-man. Therefore we all glorify thee, who art our protection after God.

*Troparion:*

Adorned with diverse colors,[^331] O woman full of the grace of God, thou hast given birth beyond mind and word unto the Father's Word, Who became flesh through unutterable tender mercy, O blessed one, having remained an undefiled Virgin. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having sprouted, O pure one, from the human root,[^332] thou hast given birth beyond mind and understanding unto the King Christ, the Word of God, incarnate of thy pure blood, shown forth in two natures, yet single in hypostasis. Both now:

[f. 202vA] *Theotokion:*

Whosoever proclaims thee to be the Theotokos, O Most Pure One, bears victory over great heresies: for thou, O Mother of God, hast inchangeably given birth in the flesh unto the Word of God, Who is co-eternal, higher than all creation.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens in understanding, and didst found the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of the Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^333]

*Troparion:*

God dwelt in thy holy womb and became incarnate, as He alone knows, O Mother of God, and He saved us by His life-bearing Passion; therefore do we glorify thee as the door of salvation.

*Troparion:*

Having become incarnate on earth, the supremely divine One, Who breathed the breath of life into all men, lived among men, having come forth from thy womb without seed; therefore all we the faithful in songs bless thee, O Pure One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Having conceived in thy womb without wedlock Him Who was born of the Father before all ages, O Virgin, thou hast brought forth[^334] for us at once God and man, perfect in both natures and indivisible. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

All of us know thee, O Pure One, as the Rod that budded the Flower of incorruption, Christ, and as the golden Censer, bearing in thine hand the Coal of divine nature, O Virgin blessed of God.[^335]

[f. 203rA] *Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Thou art my strength, O Lord, yea, Thou art my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who, without leaving the Father’s bosom, hast visited our poverty in Thy mercy. Therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind![^336]

*Troparion:*

The Ark that received the Law written by God prefigured from afar the Life-originating Word in thy womb, unutterably conceived, O All-immaculate One, richly nourishing the souls of those who chant: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

Thou alone, O Mother of God, didst become the true cause of salvation, lending flesh from thy most pure blood unto the Creator and Builder, whereby the gates of death were crushed, and life was bestowed upon men. Glory:

*Troparion:*

That He might in every way renew the image buried by passions, the Wealth of grace dwelt in the Virgin's womb, whereof Thou, the Wisdom of God, didst build Thyself a temple; wherein having lived among men, O Compassionate One, Thou hast saved the ends of the world. Both now:

[f. 203vA] *Theotokion:*

After God we have acquired thee as protectress, O All-immaculate One: for thou didst become the Mother of God, the Creator and Builder, Who took our form, and saved it from corruption and perils, and glorified it with divine glory.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy countenance, O Never-setting Light, and an alien darkness hath covered me, a wretch? But turn me back, and guide my ways unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray Thee.[^337]

*Troparion:*

As having conceived God, the Builder, Creator, and Lord, O Most Pure One, all generations of men call thee blessed, and the bodiless noetic leaders of the ranks glorify thee as Mother unto God.

*Troparion:*

The hosts of the heavenly Powers bless thee, O Most Pure One: for through thee alone earthly beings were united with heavenly beings; we hymn thy Childbearing. Glory:

*Troparion:*

In an ineffable manner, O Virgin, the Word dwelt in thee and became man, He Who shone forth from the Father before the ages, and He delivers men from corruption, leading them unto incorruptible life, as the only compassionate One. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast adorned thyself with virginal beauties, O Most Pure Virgin Maiden, and hast loosed the ugly shame of the first Eve by giving birth unto Christ, [f. 204rA] Who grants the garment of immortality unto those who chant unto thee.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities, and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray Thee; for unto Thee have I cried, and do Thou hear me, O God of my salvation.[^338]

*Troparion:*

He Who upholds all things by His almighty power is clothed from thee in the weakness of the flesh, O All-immaculate Virgin, for the benefaction of men, as the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:*

The all-effecting Spirit came upon thee, O All-immaculate One, and the Word of God dwelt in thee, and became flesh ineffably, remaining unchanging. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Supremely glorious is the miracle of thy compassions: show compassion upon me, O Most Pure Theotokos, and appear unto me as a fervent helper and salvation in temptations. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

A dwelling-place of God, divine in truth and most honorable, we piously hymn thee, O Most Pure Mary: for thou wast accounted worthy to contain God, Whom nothing can contain and Who is unsearchable.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*

As the all-immaculate Bride of the Creator, as the unwedded Mother of the Redeemer, as the [f. 204vA] receptacle of the Comforter, O All-hymned One: seeing that I am become a foul dwelling-place of iniquity and a plaything of demons in my understanding, make haste to deliver me from their evil deeds; and having radiantly made me a dwelling-place of virtues, O light-giving and incorruptible one, disperse the cloud of passions, and account me worthy of communion with the Highest and of the never-waning Light by thy prayers.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The fire was put to shame before the condescension of God in Babylon of old; therefore the Children in the furnace, dancing with joyful step as in a bridal chamber, chanted: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^339]

*Troparion:*

O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, I fall down and pray unto thee, bowing before thee as a speedy helper: entreat for me a sinner thy Son, Christ our God, that He may have mercy upon me, an unworthy one, who fervently cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers.

*Troparion:*

Now all things are filled with divine light through thee, O Most Pure One: for thou hast appeared as the Door whereby God conversed with the world, enlightening those who call in faith: Blessed is the God of our fathers. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Behold, now is fulfilled the divine prophecy of divine David: those who have acquired [f. 205rA] the wealth of grace truly entreat thy face,[^340] O Pure Mother of God, now blessing the God of our fathers. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst conceive, O Most Pure One, God and the Lord over all, Who was well pleased to save the human race from death and corruption; Whom worthily chanting, we cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Sevenfold did the Chaldean tormentor furiously heat the furnace for the godly; but seeing them saved by a higher Power, he cried unto the Creator and Redeemer: Children, bless; priests, hymn; people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^341]

*Troparion:*

O blessed and Most Pure One, cleanse the wounds of my soul and the sores of my sin, thou who didst give birth unto God over all from thy virginal and unwedded womb, O Pure Maiden; Whom, ye children, bless, and supremely exalt unto the ages.

*Troparion:*

Thou didst become the receptacle of the Never-setting Light, O All-immaculate One, shining with the beauties of virginity and illuminating all who from their soul confess thee to be the true Theotokos, and cry: Children, bless; and supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Cleansed by the Holy Spirit, O All-immaculate Theotokos, thou didst truly receive into thy womb the unoriginate [f. 205vA] and co-eternal Son, incarnate in thee, O Pure One, for benefaction unto those who cry in faith: Children, bless; priests, sing; and supremely exalt Him unto all ages. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

As thou hast given birth unto God beyond word and thought, entreat Him, O Most Pure Mother of God, to be clement unto all of us the unworthy at His fearful and awesome coming, when He shall judge human nature, when all shall stand naked and be judged in fear.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Heaven was astonished thereat, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God appeared unto men in the flesh, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the ranks of angels and men magnify thee as the Theotokos.[^342]

*Troparion:*

We who were cast out from eternal abiding and had fallen wretchedly into death hast thou called back, O Most Pure One, having become the Mother of the Redeemer, and hast accounted us worthy to run back unto our former homeland; therefore unceasingly we magnify thee as the Mother of God.

*Troparion:*

Grant me easily, O Theotokos, to cross the surge of this life, and temptations, and the onset of passions, calming them as good, and guiding me unto the virtue of the heavenly journey: that as my Benefactress I may unceasingly magnify thee. Glory:

[f. 206rA] *Troparion:*

O Pure and most glorious Mother of God, save from temptations those who chant unto thee with love, graciously fulfilling their supplication: for having given birth unto God, whatsoever thou desirest to do thou doest without hindrance, and hast power, O Virgin; therefore we all magnify thee. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Show forth the precious Church triumphing over youthful heresies,[^343] which Christ Thy Son, O Full of Grace, acquired as good with His own precious Blood; and save her from perils and from the oppression that holds us fast, O Sovereign Lady.

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: and the rest as usual: and the Dismissal.*

*(Engraving: Christ the Savior and the Most Holy Theotokos seated in glory amid clouds and radiant light, flanked by floral decorative crosses)*

[f. 206vA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate headpiece depicting floral arabesques and cherubim)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Thursday at Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea; for He alone hath been gloriously glorified.[^344]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

By thy prayers make Christ merciful unto me, O All-immaculate Virgin, delivering me on the day of fearful judgment.

*Troparion:*

Rain down upon me, I pray thee, O Sovereign Lady, drops of contrition, washing away the foulness that is in me, that I may glorify thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Enlighten me, O Virgin who didst give birth unto the Never-setting Light, driving away the deep darkness of my sloth. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Save me, O Theotokos, who am perished in many sins, and deliver me from all torment and cruel condemnation.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

O Lord, Artificer of the celestial vault and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O Summit of desires, Confirmation of the faithful, only Lover of mankind.[^345]

[f. 207rA] *Troparion:*

Having fallen away from a holy life, O Most Pure One, I have become like unto the beasts,[^346] and am wholly condemned; but do thou, who didst give birth unto the Judge, deliver me from all condemnation and save me.

*Troparion:*

Unto thee do I flee ever, O Sovereign Lady, surrounded by a multitude of temptations; have compassion and save me, thou who didst give birth unto the Savior of all and Lord, O only All-hymned One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O impassable Door who leadest unto God, O Maiden: open unto me the gates of repentance, I pray thee, having cleansed the foulness of my sins with the showers of thy mercy, O woman full of the grace of God. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Deliver me from the onslaught of passions, O Sovereign Lady, and conquer my warring enemies now; establish me upon the rock of God's desires, and enlighten my soul, O door of divine light.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^347]

*Troparion:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, O Virgin full of the grace of God, and enlighten my mind, O Pure One, darkened by the assaults of passions.

*Troparion:*

Unto me who sleep in despondency and am covered with sin, call me unto repentance, O Most Pure One:

[f. 207vA] as Mother of the Word. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O unwedded Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto the incarnate Word: enlighten my soul, and deliver it from Gehenna and torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Upon thee have I placed all my hope, O Virgin Mother: preserve my soul, thou who didst give birth unto God, my Savior.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy countenance, O Never-setting Light, and an alien darkness hath covered me, a wretch? But turn me back, and guide my ways unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray Thee.[^348]

*Troparion:*

Heal the wholly incurable passions of my soul, O Maiden, and rekindle my lamp that hath been extinguished through sloth, and guide me unto the way of repentance, O Virgin, that in faith and love I may glorify thee.

*Troparion:*

My shameful deeds condemn me before the trial, O thou who hast been gladdened of God, and I am uniquely condemned; but stand before me, as thou art the Protectress of all, and deliver me from cruel condemnation. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Deliver me from captivity, and from the soul-corrupting turmoil that holds me fast, and from cruel passions that unceasingly slay me, O Most Pure Maiden, protectress of sinners, ready helper of men. Both now:

[f. 208rA] *Theotokion:*

Set me upright, O Most Pure One, who am wholly slain by the sting of death through transgression, thou who didst conceive the Life for the world, the Redeemer and King; and guide me unto the light.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities, and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray Thee; for unto Thee have I cried, and do Thou hear me, O God of my salvation.[^349]

*Troparion:*

Make me alive, who am slain by many transgressions, O Most Pure Virgin Theotokos, who didst unutterably give birth unto the Life of men; and guide me to do the Lord's will.

*Troparion:*

We all the faithful have acquired thee as protectress and rampart, being ever submerged in the depth of evils, turmoils, and griefs, O Theotokos, only refuge of the faithful. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Thou who by thy holy budding didst wither the gardens of godlessness, O All-immaculate One: consume the enemy's malice that ever grows within me, O Most Pure One. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Sanctify my mind and enlighten my heart, O Holy Mother of God, and change the evils that hold me fast, that I may glorify thee as my firm protectress.[^350]

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*

O marvel of wonder! O fearful mystery! cried the Ewe-lamb, beholding her Son upon the Tree of the Cross [f. 208vA] stretched out: What is this, O immortal Word of God! How art Thou seen dead! And Thou shakest the earth, as Almighty: Yet do I hymn Thy fearful and divine condescension.[^351]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Hebrew Children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and having changed the fire into dew, they chanted: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto the ages.[^352]

*Troparion:*

Be merciful unto me, O Virgin, and heal me who am wounded by the weapon of sin, with the medicine of thy prayer; and snatch me from the unquenchable fire unto the ages.

*Troparion:*

Deliver me, O Mother of the Word, from the cruel captivity that holds me, from evil thoughts and the grip of transgressions; that being ever saved, I may worthily glorify thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Now do I flee unto thee, O Mother of God, bound by the cords of transgressions: through the tender mercy of thy compassion loose me, O Virgin, and deliver me eternally from torment and malice. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Have compassion and save me, O Virgin, who didst give birth unto the compassionate Word of God; and with the light that is in thee illuminate my soul, and deliver me eternally from cruel craftiness.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Sevenfold did the Chaldean tormentor furiously heat the furnace for the godly; but seeing them saved by a higher Power, he cried unto the Creator and Redeemer:

[f. 209rA] Children, bless; priests, hymn; people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^353]

*Troparion:*

Perfect in all things, and unapproachable in nature, He appeared touchable unto me, clothed in flesh from thee, O unwedded one; Whom diligently entreat to lighten the burden of mine iniquities, and to deliver me from the judgment to come.

*Troparion:*

Having given birth beyond word unto the Judge and Lord, entreat Him as thy Son, O Most Pure One, in the hour of judgment to deliver me from condemnation, and fire, and unilluminated darkness, and gnashing of teeth,[^354] who ever chant unto thee piously in faith. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O Most Pure Mother of God, cleanse the wounds of my soul and the stumbling blocks of sin, washing them with the fountain from the side of Him born of thee,[^355] and with the streams that flowed therefrom: for unto thee do I call, and unto thee do I flee, and thee do I entreat, O woman full of the grace of God. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

My soul that is slain by the serpent's biting, do thou make alive, O only all-immaculate one who didst manifestly give birth unto Life; and hasten, O Virgin, that we may act according to the desires of the deeds of Him Who was born of thee, as I cry: Children, bless; priests, hymn; people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.

[f. 209vA] *Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Heaven was astonished thereat, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God appeared unto men in the flesh, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the ranks of angels and men magnify thee as the Theotokos.[^356]

*Troparion:*

Enlighten my soul, O door of light, which is blinded by passions, and blackened and afflicted by evil thoughts; and deliver me from temptations, and the malice of demons, and cruel interrogation, and the future flame and torment.

*Troparion:*

Spare me, O Savior born of the Virgin, Who didst preserve her that gave Thee birth incorrupt even after Childbearing: when Thou shalt sit to judge my deeds, overlook mine iniquities and sins, as sinless, and as the merciful God and Lover of mankind. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Bearing the Heavenly Fire in thine hands as tongs,[^357] O woman full of the grace of God: burn up the passions of my soul, O Pure One, and deliver me from fearful condemnation, and the fire, and cruel torment of demons. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

By thy noetic radiance enlighten our thoughts, and hearts, and minds, O Maiden full of the grace of God: that walking uprightly along the paths of life, we may obtain mercy, ever proclaiming thy praises.

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*

[f. 210rA] *(Woodcut: Elaborate scene of the Nativity of Christ — the Virgin Mary and Saint Joseph adoring the Christ Child in the manger, surrounded by angels, shepherds, and animals, within a decorative floral frame)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE (TONE 8)

*On Friday at Compline,*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos:*

Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea; for He alone hath been gloriously glorified.[^358]

*Verse: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion:*

Put to death the wisdom of my flesh,[^359] O Most Pure Virgin who didst give birth unto Life; and deliver me from all torment.

*Troparion:*

With the supremely bright ray of Him Who shone forth from thee, O Virgin, enlighten the pupils of my soul, that I may glorify thee. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Pray unto the Master, O only Holy Theotokos, that on the day of judgment I may obtain salvation and divine brightness. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Theotokos, assuage the pains of my heart, thou who didst without travail give birth unto Christ beyond mind and word.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Thou art the confirmation of those who flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings of Thee.[^360]

[f. 210vA] *Troparion:*

Deliver me by thy prayers, O Most Pure One, from the enemy's arrows, and from the assault of thoughts; spare my heart.

*Troparion:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, O All-immaculate One, and soothe the turmoil of my heart, O Mother of God, by thy prayers. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Calm the ceaseless rising of my thoughts, O Pure Sovereign Lady, and take away all sorrow from my soul, thou who didst give birth unto Joy. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, salvation of those who flee unto thee, O Virgin Mother of Christ; rejoice, praise of Apostles and Martyrs.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^361]

*Troparion:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, O Word of God, Who wast wounded for my sake as the Lover of mankind; and enlighten the darkness of my mind.[^362]

*Troparion:*

The slumber of my sloth and the sleep of sin have overtaken me, O Virgin; but by thy vigilant prayer, raise me up unto repentance. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O fountain that didst pour forth the water of forgiveness, O All-immaculate One: water my heart that is dried up by all manner of transgressions. Both now:

[f. 211rA] *Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Bride of God, Sovereign Lady of the world: save me, changing what is from ages past, and driving away the turmoil of passions.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Early in the morning we cry unto Thee, O Lord, save us: for Thou art our God, and beside Thee we know no other.[^363]

*Troparion:*

Look down, O Most Pure One, and hear my voice, and cleanse all mine iniquities, O Sovereign Lady.

*Troparion:*

Thou who didst give birth unto the Never-setting Sun, O Theotokos: wholly enlighten me who am darkened by passions, that I may glorify and praise thee, O All-immaculate One. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Have compassion upon my soul, O Most Pure Maiden, and deliver it from judgment and eternal torment. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Heal my diseased soul, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto Him Who takes away the diseases of all.[^364]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

The abyss of sins and tempest of transgressions troubles me, and thrusts me into the depth of sudden despair; but stretch forth unto me Thy mighty hand as unto Peter, O Helmsman, and save me.[^365]

*Troparion:*

Put to death, O Theotokos, the disorderly motions of my flesh, and me who am slain by malice do thou make alive by thy living Childbearing, that I may glorify thee as the cause of human renewal.

[f. 211vA] *Troparion:*

Having given birth unto the Abyss of compassions, O Virgin Theotokos, save my soul sorrowful from this life, and open unto me the gates of spiritual joy; for upon thee alone have I placed my hope. Glory:

*Troparion:*

That I may joyfully hymn thy majesties, O All-pure Virgin, and the great grace of thy wonders, change thou by thy prayers the unruly thoughts that ever torment me. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Thou hast given birth beyond nature unto Him Who is inseparable from the Father's bosom, and Who through thee conversed with men, O Virgin; but as thou art the boast and confirmation of us all, O Theotokos, have compassion upon those who flee unto thee.

*Lord, have mercy (3). Glory: Both now:*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*

The Word of the Father came down upon earth, and a radiant Angel said unto the Theotokos: Rejoice, blessed one, who alone hast preserved the Bridal Chamber, having received the conception of the Pre-eternal God and Lord, that as God He might save the human race from deception.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

The Hebrew children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and changing the fire into dew, they chanted: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto the ages.[^366]

*Troparion:*

Woe is me, how shall I escape the torments awaiting me, having lived evilly here! How shall I appear, wretched one, before the fearful Judge; [f. 212rA] but do thou, O Mother of God and Sovereign Lady, be my helper then.

*Troparion:*

Quench the flame of my passions, and calm the tempest of my heart, O pure Mother of God; and deliver me, O All-pure One, from the torment of demons and from eternal fire. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Behold my affliction, O Virgin, which the multitude of mine evils hath brought on, and grant me rest before my departure, imploring thy Son by thy maternal prayers. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

The flame of the passions of my heart quench with thy dew, O Virgin, and snatch me, O Maiden, from the fearful fire, and from eternal judgment, and from demonic torment.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Bless the Creator Father, O children equal in number to the Trinity; praise God, the Word Who descended and transformed the fire into dew; and highly exalt throughout the ages the All-Holy Spirit Who gives life unto all.[^367]

*Troparion:*

Put to death, O Virgin, all our bodily motions, who by thy life-bearing Childbearing hast made dead the serpent;[^368] and pray that we may obtain the life that grows not old, that we may hymn thee unto the ages.

*Troparion:*

Thee do I ever call upon for help, O Mother of God; put me not to shame, who have acquired shameful deeds, [f. 212vA] but have compassion upon me, O Most Pure One, and snatch me from the flame, and deliver me from eternal torments. Glory:

*Troparion:*

O full of grace, be revealed as my Helper in the distress of evils, delivering me from the assaults of demonic snares, that I may ever bless thee, O Theotokos, Protectress of all. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

O Most Pure Maiden, Boast of the Apostles, Glory of the Martyrs: make me a partaker of eternal glory as I cry: Bless the All-Holy Spirit unto all the ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Thou hast surpassed the ordinances of nature, having conceived the Creator and Lord, and hast become the door of salvation unto the world; therefore, O Theotokos, we unceasingly magnify thee.[^369]

*Troparion:*

By thy never-waning Light, O door of the light, drive away the grievous darkness of my soul, and show unto me the radiant paths of repentance, that walking in them, I may escape the murk of sin and unceasingly magnify thee.

*Troparion:*

Cleanse Thy servants, O Good One, and grant remission of transgressions, and deliver us from the eternal flame, and make us partakers of Thy Kingdom, [f. 213rA] O Word of God, for in desiring to save the human race, Thou didst endure the Cross. Glory:

*Troparion:*

Grant me easily, O Theotokos, to pass through the course of this life, calming as a good one the risings of temptations and passions, and guiding me unto the virtue of the heavenly Kingdom and divine rest, that being saved, I may glorify thee. Both now:

*Theotokion:*

Heal my soul, diseased with grievous passion, as a merciful one, O thou who didst give birth unto the most good God, and deliver me from those who afflict me again, O Most Pure One, and from those who abide in cruel malice, that being saved, I may fervently magnify thee who hast magnified our race.[^370]

*Then, It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father:*

*and straightway we say these Troparia:*

*(Woodcut: Christ seated in glory in heaven among clouds and angels, below the faithful raise their hands in supplication, flanked by ornamental crosses)*

[f. 213vA] *(Woodcut: Ornamental headpiece depicting floral motifs)*

---

# WEEKDAY OCTOECHOS

## ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON SUNDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion of the Temple, Tone 1:*

In thy childbearing thou didst preserve thy virginity, and in thy falling asleep thou didst not forsake the world, O Theotokos; thou hast passed over unto life, being the Mother of Life, and by thy prayers thou deliverest our souls from death.[^371]

*And of the Day, Tone 4:*

O Leaders of the heavenly hosts, we the unworthy do ever beseech you, that by your prayers you may shelter us under the roof of the wings of your immaterial glory, preserving us who fall down diligently and cry:

[f. 214rA] Deliver us from perils, as Commanders of the higher Powers.[^372]

*Then unto our Venerable Father Anthony of the Caves, Troparion, Tone 4:*

Having departed from worldly turmoil, and by renunciation of the world having followed the Gospel of Christ, and having lived an angel-like life, thou didst attain unto the quiet haven of the Holy Mountain of Athos; whence, by the blessing of the Fathers having come unto the hills of Kyiv, and having laboriously accomplished thy life there, thou didst enlighten thy homeland; and having shown unto a multitude of monastics the paths leading unto the heavenly Kingdom, thou didst bring them unto Christ;[^373] [f. 214vA] Whom do thou beseech, O Venerable Anthony, that He may save our souls.

*Kontakion, Tone 8:*

Having committed thyself unto God, Who was beloved above all from thy youth, O Venerable One, thou didst follow Him with love from all thy soul; and having accounted the corruptible things of the world as naught, thou didst make a cave in the earth, and therein didst struggle valiantly against the snares of the invisible enemy, shining forth like a radiant sun unto all ends of the earth; whence rejoicing thou hast passed over unto the heavenly bridal chambers; and standing now with the Angels before the Master's throne, remember us who honor thy memory, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, Anthony our Father!

[f. 215rA] *Unto our Venerable Father Theodosius of the Caves, Troparion, Tone 8:*

Having been exalted in virtue, having loved the monastic life from youth, and having valiantly attained unto thy desire, thou didst settle in the Cave, and didst adorn thy life with fasting and radiance; in prayers thou didst abide as one bodiless, shining forth in the land of Rus' like a radiant luminary,[^374] O Father Theodosius; beseech Christ God that our souls may be saved.[^375]

*Kontakion, Tone 4:*

Today let us honor the star of Rus', which shone forth from the East and came unto the West; for it illumined all this land with wonders and goodness, and [f. 215vA] enriched all of us with the ascetic labor and grace of the monastic rule of the blessed Theodosius.[^376]

*Troparion, Tone 4:*

O God of our Fathers, Who dealest ever with us according to Thy gentleness, withdraw not Thy mercy from us; but by their supplications order our life in peace.[^377]

*Troparion, Tone 4:*

Thy Church, having been adorned with the blood of Thy Martyrs throughout the whole world as with purple and fine linen, through them cries unto Thee, O Christ God: Send down Thy compassions upon Thy people, grant peace unto Thy commonwealth, and unto our souls great mercy.[^378] Glory:

[f. 216rA] *Kontakion, Tone 8:*

With the Saints give rest, O Christ, unto the souls of Thy servants, where there is no sickness, nor sorrow, nor sighing, but life unending.[^379] Both now:

*Theotokion:*

By the prayers, O Lord, of all the Saints and of the Theotokos, grant us Thy peace, and have mercy upon us, as Thou alone art compassionate.

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON MONDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion of the Temple: In thy childbearing virginity [was preserved]...*

*And of the Day, Tone 2:*

The memory of the righteous is celebrated with hymns of praise, but the witness of the Lord suffices thee, O Forerunner; for thou wast shown forth truly as the most honorable of the Prophets, in that thou wast accounted worthy to baptize in the streams Him Whom thou didst preach. Therefore, having suffered for the truth with rejoicing, thou didst proclaim the good tidings even unto those in hades [f. 216vA] God revealed in the flesh, Who takes away the sin of the world, and grants us great mercy.[^380]

*Troparion:* Having departed from worldly turmoil...

*Troparion:* Having been exalted in virtue...

*Troparion:* O God of our Fathers, Who dealest ever with us...

*Troparion:* Thy Church... throughout the whole world...

*Glory:* With the Saints give rest...

*Both now:* By the prayers, O Lord...

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON TUESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion to the Cross, Tone 1:*

Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance, granting victories unto our right-believing Sovereign[^381] over adversaries, and by Thy Cross preserving Thine estate.[^382]

*Troparion of the Temple: In thy childbearing virginity [was preserved]...*

[f. 217rA] *Troparion:* Having departed from worldly turmoil...

*Troparion:* Having been exalted in virtue...

*Troparion:* O God of our Fathers, Who dealest ever with us...

*Troparion:* Thy Church... throughout the whole world...

*Glory:* With the Saints give rest...

*Both now:* By the prayers, O Lord of all the Saints...

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON WEDNESDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion of the Temple: In thy childbearing virginity [was preserved]...*

*And of the Day, Tone 2:*

Holy Apostles, beseech the merciful God, that He may grant remission of sins unto our souls.[^383]

*Then unto Saint Nicholas, Troparion, Tone 4:*

A rule of faith and an image of meekness, a teacher of temperance did the Lord reveal Thee [f. 217vA] unto thy flock by the truth of things; therefore by humility thou didst acquire high things, and by poverty rich things: O our Father, Hierarch Nicholas, beseech Christ God that our souls may be saved.[^384]

*Troparion:* Having departed from worldly turmoil...

*Troparion:* Having been exalted in virtue...

*Troparion:* O God of our Fathers, Who dealest ever with us...

*Troparion:* Thy Church... throughout the whole world...

*Glory:* With the Saints give rest...

*Both now:* By the prayers, O Lord of all the Saints...

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON THURSDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion of the Day: Save, O Lord, Thy people...*

*Troparion of the Temple: In thy childbearing virginity [was preserved]...*

[f. 218rA] *Troparion:* Having been exalted in virtue...

*Troparion:* O God of our Fathers, Who dealest ever with us...

*Glory:* With the Saints give rest...

*Both now:* By the prayers, O Lord of all the Saints...

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON FRIDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*Troparion, Tone 2:*

Apostles, Prophets, and holy Martyrs, Venerable and Righteous ones, who have well finished the course and kept the faith, having boldness before the Savior, we beseech you: pray unto Him as good on our behalf, that He may save our souls.[^385]

*Glory: Both now:*

*Kontakion, Tone 8:*

Unto Thee, O Lord, the Creator of creation, the inhabited world offers [f. 218vA] the God-bearing Martyrs as firstfruits of nature; by their prayers keep Thy Church and Thy commonwealth in deep peace for the sake of the Theotokos, O only Most Merciful One.[^386]

*(Ornamental headpiece)*

## ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE

*ON SATURDAY AT SMALL COMPLINE:*

*The Hypakoai of the current Tone are read.*

*Hypakoe, Tone 1:*

The repentance of the Thief opened Paradise, and the weeping of the Myrrhbearers announced Joy, for Christ God is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^387]

*Hypakoe, Tone 2:*

After the Passion the women went unto the Tomb to anoint Thy Body, O Christ God, and they saw Angels in the Tomb and were dismayed; [f. 219rA] for they heard a voice from them: The Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^388]

*Hypakoe, Tone 3:*

Astonishing by his appearance, shedding dew by his words, the radiant Angel said unto the Myrrhbearers: Why seek ye the living in the tomb? He is risen, having emptied the graves! Understand the Incorruptible One as the Changer of corruption; say unto God: How fearful are Thy works! For Thou hast saved the human race.[^389]

*Hypakoe, Tone 4:*

Anticipating the dawn of Thy glorious Rising, the Myrrhbearers preached unto the Apostles, O Christ, that Thou art risen as God, granting great mercy unto the world.[^390]

[f. 219vA] *Hypakoe, Tone 5:*

Troubling the mind with angelic appearance, but enlightening the soul with divine Rising, the Myrrhbearers announced good tidings unto the Apostles: Proclaim among the nations the Resurrection, the Lord working together with wonders, granting us great mercy.[^391]

*Hypakoe, Tone 6:*

By Thy voluntary and life-creating Death, O Christ God, having shattered the gates of hades as God, Thou hast opened ancient Paradise unto us; and having risen from the dead, Thou hast delivered our life from corruption.[^392]

*Hypakoe, Tone 7:*

Thou Who didst assume our likeness, and didst endure Crucifixion in the flesh: save us [f. 220rA] by Thy Resurrection, O Christ God, as the Lover of mankind.[^393]

*Hypakoe, Tone 8:*

The Myrrhbearers standing before the Tomb of the Giver of life sought the Master, the Immortal One among the dead, and having received from the Angel the joy of good tidings, they announced unto the Apostles that Christ God is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^394]

*Be it known also this: If there occur a Saint having a Polyeleos, or only a Doxology, outside of Sunday, we read their proper Kontakion. We chant: Lord, have mercy (40). Then the Prayer of Saint Basil the Great:*[^395]

Thou Who at all times and at every hour, in heaven and on earth, art worshipped and glorified, O Christ God,[^396] good, longsuffering, and plenteous in mercy; Who lovest the righteous and hast mercy upon sinners; [f. 220vA] Who callest all unto salvation through the promise of good things to come: do Thou Thyself, O Lord, receive also our supplications at this hour, and direct our life in goodness unto Thy commandments. Sanctify our souls, purify our bodies, correct our thoughts, sober our understanding and chasten it; and deliver us from all affliction, evils, diseases, and soul-passions; encompass us about with Thy holy Angels, that preserved and guided by their encampment, we may attain unto the unity of the faith and unto the knowledge of Thine unapproachable glory; for Thou art blessed unto the ages. Amen.

*Lord, have mercy (3). Glory: Both now: More honorable than the Cherubim:*

*In the Name of the Lord bless, father.*

> **Priest:** *God be bountiful unto us, and bless us...*

*Then:*

[f. 221rA] *Prayer to the Most Holy Theotokos,*

*of Paul the Monk, of Evergetis, that is, of the Monastery of the Benefactress:*[^397]

*(Woodcut: The Most Holy Theotokos holding the Christ Child, with radiant crowns and halos)*

O spotless, undefiled, incorrupt, most pure Sovereign Lady, Bride of God, who by thy glorious childbearing didst unite God the Word unto men, and didst join the fallen nature of our race unto the heavenly; thou who art the only Hope of the hopeless, and the Helper of the oppressed, the ready Protection of those who flee unto thee, and the Refuge of all Christians: disdain not me, a sinner and defiled, who have made myself wholly unprofitable by foul thoughts, words, and deeds,[^398] [f. 221vA] and through indolence of habits having become a slave unto pleasures: but as the Mother of God Who lovest mankind, lovingly have compassion upon me, a sinner and prodigal, and accept my supplication offered unto thee from defiled lips; and implore Thy Son and our Master and Lord, having assumed boldness with thy maternal boldness, that He may open also unto me the loving compassion of His goodness, and overlooking my countless transgressions, may turn me unto repentance, and show me forth as an approved doer of His commandments. And be thou ever present with me as merciful, compassionate, and loving goodness in this present life, an ardent Protectress and Helper, repulsing the ranks of adversaries, and guiding me unto salvation; [f. 222rA] and at the time of my departure guarding my wretched soul, and driving far from it the dark forms of evil demons; and in the dreadful day of righteous judgment delivering me from eternal torment, and showing me forth as an heir of the ineffable glory of Thy Son and our God:[^399] which may I obtain, my Sovereign Lady, Most Holy Theotokos, through thy mediation and protection, by the grace and love for mankind of Thine Only-begotten Son, our Lord and God and Savior Jesus Christ, unto Whom is due all glory, honor, and worship, with His unoriginate Father, and His All-Holy, Good, and Life-creating Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen.

[f. 222vA] *Then this Prayer, of Saint Antioch the Monk:*[^400]

And grant unto us, O Master, as we go to sleep, rest of soul and body, and preserve us from the gloomy sleep of sin, and from all dark and nocturnal voluptuousness. Quench the assault of passions, and extinguish the burning darts of the evil one which are craftily cast against us; appease the rebellions of our flesh, and lull to sleep all our earthly and material minding. And grant unto us, O God, a watchful mind, a chaste thought, a sober heart, a light sleep, and free from every satanic phantom. And raise us up at the time of prayer, established in Thy commandments, [f. 223rA] and having within ourselves the remembrance of Thy judgments continually; and grant us a night-long doxology, to chant and bless and glorify Thine all-honorable and majestic Name, of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen.

*O most glorious, Ever-Virgin Theotokos, Mary, Mother of Christ our God, receive our prayers and present them unto thy Son and our God, that for thy sake He may save and enlighten our souls.*[^401]

*Then, the Prayer of Saint Joannicius:*[^402]

God is our hope, and Christ is our refuge, and the Holy Spirit is our protector: O Holy Trinity, glory to Thee!

*And straightway the chanters chant these Troparia, Tone 6:*

*Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... Glory:*

*Lord, have mercy on us, for we have hoped in Thee... Both now:*

*Open unto us the doors of compassion...*[^403]

[f. 223vA] *After the chanting, the Priest says this Litany:*[^404]

> **Priest:** "Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy, we pray Thee, hear and have mercy."

> **Choir:** *Lord, have mercy (3).*

> **Priest:** "Again we pray that this holy Monastery (or: this city), and every Monastery, and every city, countryside, and land may be preserved from famine, pestilence, earthquake, flood, hail, fire, sword, the invasion of aliens, and civil war; and that our good and man-befriending God may be merciful, propitious, and easily entreated, and turn away all wrath stirred up against us, and deliver us from His impending righteous chastisement, and have mercy on us."

> **Choir:** *Lord, have mercy (40).*

*Exclamation:*

[f. 224rA] **Priest:** Hear us, O God our Savior, the hope of all the ends of the earth and of those who are far off upon the sea; and, O Merciful One, be merciful unto our sins, and have mercy on us: for a merciful God art Thou, and the Lover of mankind, and unto Thee do we send up glory: to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages.

> **Choir:** "Amen."

> **Priest:** "Glory to Thee, O Christ God,[^405] our hope, glory to Thee."

> **Choir:** *Glory: Both now: Lord, have mercy (3). Bless.*

> **Priest:** *Dismissal: If it be Sunday: He Who rose from the dead... If on other days:* Christ our true God, through the prayers of His most pure Mother, of our venerable and God-bearing Fathers, and of all the Saints, have mercy on us and save us, as He is good and the Lover of mankind.[^406]

*If it be Holy Week, or after "God is our hope...", the Priest says this Prayer:*[^407]

[f. 224vA] O Master, great in mercy, Lord Jesus Christ our God: through the prayers of our all-immaculate Lady, Theotokos and Ever-Virgin Mary; by the power of the precious and life-creating Cross; by the protection of the honorable bodiless Heavenly Powers; of the honorable and glorious Prophet, Forerunner, and Baptist John; of the holy, glorious, and all-praised Apostles; of the holy and victorious Martyrs; of our venerable and God-bearing Fathers; of the holy and righteous Ancestors of God, Joachim and Anna; and of Saint *[Name]*, whose temple it is; and of all Thy Saints: make our prayer acceptable; grant us remission of our transgressions; cover us with the shelter of Thy wings; and drive away from us every enemy and adversary. Pacify our life, O Lord; [f. 225rA] have mercy on us and on Thy world, and save our souls, as Thou art good and the Lover of mankind.

*Then he says this:*[^408]

> **Priest:** "Let us pray for our Pious Sovereign, Tsar and Grand Prince *[Name]:*"[^409]

> **Choir:** *God preserve and strengthen the realm of his kingdom.*

> **Priest:** "For his Christ-loving armed forces:"

> **Choir:** *God give them good success.*

> **Priest:** "For the founders of this holy Monastery (or: of this holy Temple):"

> **Choir:** *God pardon and have mercy on them.*

> **Priest:** "For the Most Holy Patriarch of all Rus' *[Name]:*"[^410]

> **Choir:** *God have mercy on him and save him.*

> **Priest:** "For the Most Reverend Metropolitan *[Name]:*"

> **Choir:** *God have mercy on him and save him.*

> **Priest:** "For our all-honorable Father Archimandrite *[Name],* and all our Brotherhood in Christ:"

[f. 225vA] **Choir:** *God have mercy on them and save them.*

> **Priest:** "For our fathers and brethren sent away on service:"

> **Choir:** *God have mercy on them and give them good success.*

> **Priest:** "For all who minister and have ministered in this holy Monastery (or: in this holy Temple):"

> **Choir:** *God pardon and have mercy on them.*

> **Priest:** "For those who have given us alms, and for those who have bidden us, unworthy though we be, to pray for them:"

> **Choir:** *God pardon and have mercy on them.*

> **Priest:** "For Orthodox Christians travelling by land and voyaging by water:"

> **Choir:** *God pardon them and direct their journey.*

> **Priest:** "For the sick and suffering:"

> **Choir:** *God have mercy on them and heal them.*

> **Priest:** "And for all Orthodox Christians:"

> **Choir:** *God pardon and have mercy on them.*

> **Priest:** "And for the abundance of the fruits of the earth:"

> **Choir:** *God multiply the fruits of the earth.*

*And if it be not Sunday, nor a renowned Saint, [f. 226rA] he says also this at Daily Midnight Office and Compline:*[^411]

> **Priest:** "Let us remember our Orthodox Tsars;[^412] and the founders of this holy Monastery; our parents and kinsfolk; and our fathers and brethren departed before us, who lie here and the Orthodox everywhere."

*And the Choir responds unto all the petitions:*

> **Choir:** *God remember and give rest unto them.*

*And at the end the Priest says:*

> **Priest:** "Memory eternal unto them!"

*And the Superior, or the same Priest, says:*

> **Priest:** "Through the prayers of Thy most pure Mother, for the sake of her honorable and glorious Dormition,[^413] of our venerable and God-bearing Fathers Anthony and Theodosius of the Caves, and of all the Saints: O Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us. Amen."

*The Priest makes a prostration unto the ground before the Brethren, saying:*

> **Priest:** "Bless, holy fathers, and forgive me, a sinner, whereinsoever I have sinned all the days [f. 226vA] of my life, and this day, in deed, word, and thought, and in all my senses."

*And all the Brethren answer together:*

**Brethren:** *God forgive thee and have mercy on thee, holy father.*

*Then the Brethren also bow their knees unto the ground, saying this mutual forgiveness:*

**Brethren:** *Forgive me, holy father, and bless, whereinsoever I have sinned all the days of my life, and this day, in deed, word, and thought, and in all my senses; and remember me, a sinner.*

*The Priest responds:*

> **Priest:** "By His grace may God forgive and have mercy on you."

*(Tailpiece: An ornamental woodcut garland of blossoms, fruit, and festoons)*

[f. 227rA] *(Woodcut: God the Father enthroned amidst the clouds, holding the sphere and blessing, with angelic hosts and the Holy Spirit)*

## PRAYERS FOR THOSE GOING TO SLEEP

*Prayers for Those Going to Sleep:*[^414]

*If a Priest: Blessed is our God... If not, then: Through the prayers... Trisagion, and after Our Father: the Troparia: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... Then: Lord, have mercy (12). And read these prayers in order, with attention and contrition:*

*Prayer 1, of Saint Macarius the Great, unto God the Father:*[^415]

O Eternal God and King of all creation, Who hast vouchsafed me to arrive even unto this hour: forgive me the sins which I have committed this day in deed, word, and thought; and cleanse, O Lord, my humble soul from all defilement [f. 227vA] of flesh and spirit; and grant unto me, O Lord, to pass this sleep in peace during this night, that rising from my humble bed, I may be well-pleasing unto Thine All-Holy Name all the days of my life, and trample down the enemies that war against me, bodily and bodiless; and deliver me, O Lord, from vain thoughts that defile me, and from evil desires: for Thine is the kingdom, and the power, and the glory, with Thine Only-begotten Son, and Thine All-Holy Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen.

*Prayer 2, unto our Lord Jesus Christ, of Saint Antioch:*[^416]

O Almighty Word of the Father, Jesus Christ, Who art Thyself perfect: for the sake of Thy manifold mercy, never depart from me, Thy servant, but [f. 228rA] ever rest in me, O Jesus, the good Shepherd of Thy sheep; deliver me not over unto the rebellion of the serpent, and leave me not unto the desire of Satan, for the seed of corruption is in me.[^417] Do Thou therefore, O Lord God worshipped, Holy King Jesus Christ, preserve me while I sleep with the unwaning light, by Thy Holy Spirit, wherewith Thou didst sanctify Thy disciples. Grant also unto me, Thine unworthy servant, O Lord, Thy salvation upon my bed: enlighten my mind with the light of the understanding of Thy holy Gospel; my soul with the love of Thy Cross; my heart with the purity of Thy word; my body with Thy passionless Passion; preserve my thought with Thy humility; and raise me up in due time unto [f. 228vA] Thy doxology: for Thou art glorified above all with Thine unoriginate Father, and with the All-Holy Spirit, unto the ages. Amen.

*Prayer 3, unto the All-Holy Spirit:*[^418]
